1. Greetings & Introductions (问候 wèn hòu)

Words

ChinesePinyinEnglish
你好nǐ hǎohello
再见zài jiàngoodbye
谢谢xiè xiethank you
早安zǎo āngood morning
晚安wǎn āngood night
对不起duì bu qǐsorry
没关系méi guān xiit doesn't matter
高兴gāo xìnghappy, glad
认识rèn shito know, to recognize

Sentences

ChinesePinyinEnglish
你好吗？nǐ hǎo ma?How are you?
我很好。wǒ hěn hǎo.I'm fine.
挺好。你呢？tǐng hǎo. nǐ ne?Pretty good. You?
今天过得怎么样？jīn tiān guò de zěn me yàng?How was your day?
认识你很高兴。rèn shi nǐ hěn gāo xìng.Nice to meet you.
我也很高兴。wǒ yě hěn gāo xìng.I'm also very happy (to meet you).

From Class Lessons

ChinesePinyinEnglish
我爱你wǒ ài nǐI love you · L3
不用谢búyòngxièyou're welcome · L9
最近zuìjìnrecently / lately · L12
不错búcuònot bad / pretty good · L12
还可以hái kěyǐit's ok / passable · L12
怎么了zěnme lewhat's wrong? · L21
你呢nǐ neand you? / what about you? · L27
不对bú duìnot correct, wrong · L1

2. Pronouns (代词 dài cí)

Words

ChinesePinyinEnglish
我wǒI, me
你nǐyou (singular)
您nínyou (formal/polite)
他tāhe, him
她tāshe, her
它tāit
我们wǒ menwe, us
你们nǐ menyou (plural)
他们tā menthey, them (male/mixed)
她们tā menthey, them (female)

Sentences

ChinesePinyinEnglish
我是…wǒ shì…I am…
你是…nǐ shì…You are…
他是…tā shì…He is…
她是…tā shì…She is…
她不是…tā bú shì…She is not…
她是…吗？tā shì…ma?Is she…?
她是不是…？tā shì bú shì…?Is she…? (A-not-A question)
他很…tā hěn…He is very…
他也很…tā yě hěn…He is also very…
他们都很…tā men dōu hěn…They are all very…
我爱你。wǒ ài nǐ.I love you.

From Class Lessons

ChinesePinyinEnglish
你的nǐ deyour · L22

3. Name & Identity (名字 míng zi)

Words

ChinesePinyinEnglish
叫jiàoto call / to be called
姓xìngsurname is / family name is
名字míng ziname
什么shén mewhat
是shìto be (am, is, are)
介绍jiè shàoto introduce

Sentences

ChinesePinyinEnglish
你叫什么？nǐ jiào shén me?What's your name?
我叫…wǒ jiào…My name is…
你姓什么？nǐ xìng shén me?What's your family name?
我姓…wǒ xìng…My family name is…
您贵姓？nín guì xìng?What's your family name? (polite)
我来介绍一下。wǒ lái jiè shào yí xià.Let me introduce (someone).

4. Family Members (家人 jiā rén)

Words

ChinesePinyinEnglish
家jiāfamily, home
爸爸bà bafather
妈妈mā mamother
父母fù mǔparents
爷爷yé yegrandpa (paternal)
奶奶nǎi naigrandma (paternal)
哥哥gē geelder brother
弟弟dì diyounger brother
姐姐jiě jieelder sister
妹妹mèi meiyounger sister
儿子ér zison
女儿nǚ érdaughter
宝宝bǎo baobaby
爱人ài rénhusband or wife, spouse
丈夫zhàng fuhusband
妻子qī ziwife
姥姥lǎo lǎograndma (maternal)
姥爷lǎo yégrandpa (maternal)
兄弟姐妹xiōng dì jiě mèisiblings
孩子hái zichild
独生子dú shēng zǐonly son
独生女dú shēng nǚonly daughter
家庭jiā tíngfamily (as a unit)
兄弟xiōng dìbrothers
姐妹jiě mèisisters

Sentences

ChinesePinyinEnglish
我爸爸是老师。wǒ bà ba shì lǎo shī.My father is a teacher.
我妈妈是医生。wǒ mā ma shì yī shēng.My mother is a doctor.
我爱我家。wǒ ài wǒ jiā.I love my family.
妈，这是大龙。mā, zhè shì dà lóng.Mom, this is Da Long.
你父母多大年纪了？nǐ fù mǔ duō dà nián jì le?How old are your parents?
她是我妈妈。tā shì wǒ mā ma.She is my mother.
他是我爸爸。tā shì wǒ bà ba.He is my father.
你姐姐怎么样？nǐ jiě jie zěn me yàng?How is your elder sister?
李伟的爸爸做什么工作？lǐ wěi de bà ba zuò shén me gōng zuò?What does Liwei's father do?
他爸爸是老师，在大学工作。tā bà ba shì lǎo shī, zài dà xué gōng zuò.His father is a teacher, works at a university.
你有妹妹吗？nǐ yǒu mèi mei ma?Do you have a younger sister?
有，我有一个妹妹。yǒu, wǒ yǒu yí gè mèi mei.Yes, I have one younger sister.
你妈妈在哪儿工作？nǐ mā ma zài nǎr gōng zuò?Where does your mom work?
她在医院工作。tā zài yī yuàn gōng zuò.She works at a hospital.
你妹妹做什么工作？nǐ mèi mei zuò shén me gōng zuò?What does your younger sister do?
我妹妹在北京学习。wǒ mèi mei zài běi jīng xué xí.My younger sister studies in Beijing.
你的中国家庭怎么样？nǐ de zhōng guó jiā tíng zěn me yàng?How is your Chinese family?
我的中国家庭非常好。wǒ de zhōng guó jiā tíng fēi cháng hǎo.My Chinese family is very good.

6. Demonstratives & Question Words (指示词和疑问词)

Words

ChinesePinyinEnglish
这zhèthis
那nàthat
这个zhè gethis one
那个nà gethat one
谁shéi / shuíwho
哪nǎwhich
哪儿nǎrwhere
哪里nǎ lǐwhere (also: "you flatter me")
怎么样zěn me yànghow about, what about
多大duō dàhow old
多少duō shǎohow many, how much
几jǐhow many (small number)

Sentences

ChinesePinyinEnglish
这是我的书。zhè shì wǒ de shū.This is my book.
那是你的笔吗？nà shì nǐ de bǐ ma?Is that your pen?
这不是我的杯子。zhè bú shì wǒ de bēi zi.This is not my cup.
这个苹果真大。zhè ge píng guǒ zhēn dà.This apple is really big.
这个多少钱？zhè ge duō shǎo qián?How much is this?
多少钱？duō shǎo qián?How much money?
你猜多少钱？nǐ cāi duō shǎo qián?Guess how much it costs?
对不对？duì bú duì?Right or not? (A-not-A question)
错不错？cuò bú cuò?Wrong or not?
这个人zhè ge rénthis person
这棵树zhè kē shùthis tree

From Class Lessons

ChinesePinyinEnglish

7. Numbers (数字 shù zì)

Basic Numbers

ChinesePinyinEnglish
零língzero (0)
一yīone (1)
二èrtwo (2)
三sānthree (3)
四sìfour (4)
五wǔfive (5)
六liùsix (6)
七qīseven (7)
八bāeight (8)
九jiǔnine (9)
十shíten (10)
百bǎihundred (100)
千qiānthousand (1,000)
万wànten thousand (10,000)
亿yìhundred million
两liǎngtwo (before measure words)

Tens

ChinesePinyinEnglish
二十èr shítwenty (20)
三十sān shíthirty (30)
四十sì shíforty (40)
五十wǔ shífifty (50)
六十liù shísixty (60)
七十qī shíseventy (70)
八十bā shíeighty (80)
九十jiǔ shíninety (90)
一百yì bǎione hundred (100)
一千yì qiānone thousand (1,000)
一万yí wànten thousand (10,000)
二 (èr) vs 两 (liǎng):
- Use 二 for digits, serial numbers, ordinals, fractions: 十二, 第二, 二月, 零点二
- Use 两 before measure words: 两个人, 两本书, 两天
- Use 两 in spoken language for 百/千/万/亿: 两百, 两千
- Exception: 2 o'clock = 两点 (liǎng diǎn)

Number Sentences

ChinesePinyinEnglish
他的电话号码是多少？tā de diàn huà hào mǎ shì duō shǎo?What is his phone number?
两本书liǎng běn shūtwo books
两个人liǎng gè réntwo people
两百liǎng bǎitwo hundred

Emergency Numbers

ChinesePinyinEnglish
110yāo yāo língPolice station
120yāo èr língAmbulance
119yāo yāo jiǔFirefighting
Note: In phone numbers, "一" is read as "yāo" instead of "yī" to avoid confusion with "七" (qī / seven).

From Class Lessons

ChinesePinyinEnglish
一个yí geone (of something) · L5
两个liǎng getwo (of something) · L5
三个sān gethree (of something) · L5
元yuányuan (formal/written) · L24
块kuàiyuan (spoken, "buck") · L24
角jiǎo1/10 yuan (formal) · L24
毛máo1/10 yuan (spoken) · L24

8. Age & Life (年龄 nián líng)

Words

ChinesePinyinEnglish
岁suìyears of age
年niányear
年龄nián língage
年纪nián jìage
年轻nián qīngyoung
高寿gāo shòulongevity (polite: elderly's age)
左右zuǒ yòuabout, approximately
猜cāito guess
觉得jué deto feel, to think
出生chū shēngto be born

Sentences - Asking About Age

ChinesePinyinEnglish
你多大？nǐ duō dà?How old are you? (same generation)
你几岁？nǐ jǐ suì?How old are you? (to children <10)
您多大年纪？nín duō dà nián jì?How old are you? (to seniors/CEOs)
您高寿？nín gāo shòu?How old are you? (to elderly 70+)
我十八岁。wǒ shí bā suì.I'm 18 years old.
你呢？nǐ ne?And you?
我也十八岁。wǒ yě shí bā suì.I'm also 18 years old.
你猜他多大？nǐ cāi tā duō dà?Guess how old he is?
我觉得他四十岁左右。wǒ jué de tā sì shí suì zuǒ yòu.I think he is about 40 years old.
她看起来很年轻。tā kàn qǐ lái hěn nián qīng.She looks very young.
我出生在北京。wǒ chū shēng zài běi jīng.I was born in Beijing.
你哪年出生？nǐ nǎ nián chū shēng?What year were you born?

From Class Lessons

ChinesePinyinEnglish

生日shēngrìbirthday · L17

Words

ChinesePinyinEnglish

The 12 Animals

ChinesePinyinEnglish
鼠shǔrat / mouse
牛niúox / cow
虎hǔtiger
兔tùrabbit
龙lóngdragon
蛇shésnake
马mǎhorse
羊yángsheep / goat
猴hóumonkey
鸡jīrooster / chicken
狗gǒudog
猪zhūpig

Sentences

ChinesePinyinEnglish
你属什么？nǐ shǔ shén me?What's your zodiac sign?
我属马。wǒ shǔ mǎ.I was born in the year of the Horse.
我属虎。wǒ shǔ hǔ.I was born in the year of the Tiger.
…年出生，属…，今年…岁。…nián chū shēng, shǔ…, jīn nián…suì.Born in…, zodiac is…, this year…years old.
白大龙，2000年出生，属龙，今年25岁。bái dà lóng, èr líng líng líng nián chū shēng, shǔ lóng, jīn nián èr shí wǔ suì.Bai Da Long, born in 2000, year of the Dragon, 25 this year.

Sentences - Descriptions & Intensifiers

ChinesePinyinEnglish
她很聪明。tā hěn cōng míng.She is smart. (moderate "very")
她非常聪明。tā fēi cháng cōng míng.She is VERY smart.
她特别聪明。tā tè bié cōng míng.She is exceptionally intelligent.
她真聪明。tā zhēn cōng míng.She is so smart.
我很可爱，她也很可爱。wǒ hěn kě ài, tā yě hěn kě ài.I'm cute, and she's cute too.
你真漂亮！nǐ zhēn piào liang!You are really beautiful!
你说得对。nǐ shuō de duì.You're right. (What you said is correct.)
漂亮人piào liang rénbeautiful person
我觉得他很…wǒ jué de tā hěn…I feel he is very…
她个子很矮。tā gè zi hěn ǎi.She is very short.
他又…又…tā yòu…yòu…He is both… and…
他很帅吧？tā hěn shuài ba?He is very handsome, right?
对，他很帅。duì, tā hěn shuài.Yes, he is very handsome.
不，我觉得他不帅。bù, wǒ jué de tā bú shuài.No, I feel he is not handsome.
她的头发很长。tā de tóu fa hěn cháng.Her hair is very long.
她又漂亮又可爱。tā yòu piào liang yòu kě ài.She is both beautiful and cute.
她很漂亮，也很可爱。tā hěn piào liang, yě hěn kě ài.She is very beautiful and also cute.
张丽个子很高。zhāng lì gè zi hěn gāo.Zhang Li is very tall.
你比我渣。nǐ bǐ wǒ zhā.You are more scummy than me.
谁更渣？shéi gèng zhā?Who is more scummy?
她比我更浪漫。tā bǐ wǒ gèng làng màn.She is even more romantic than me.

12. Weather & Temperature (天气 tiān qì)

Words

ChinesePinyinEnglish
热rèhot
冷lěngcold
炎热yán rèscorching hot
下雨xià yǔrainy, to rain

Sentences

ChinesePinyinEnglish
有点热。yǒu diǎn rè.It's a bit hot.
有点冷。yǒu diǎn lěng.It's a bit cold.
下雨了。xià yǔ le.It's raining.
天上下雨了。tiān shàng xià yǔ le.It's raining from the sky.

13. Adverbs (副词 fù cí)

ChinesePinyinEnglish
很hěnvery (moderate intensity)
非常fēi chángvery (stronger)
特别tè biéespecially, exceptionally
真zhēnreally, so
太…了tài…letoo (excessive)
挺tǐngquite, pretty (good)
最zuìmost, -est (superlative)
都dōuall, both
也yěalso, too, as well
又…又…yòu…yòu…both…and…
不bùno, not
有点yǒu diǎna little (slightly negative)
经常jīng chángoften
平时píng shíusually, normally
已经yǐ jīngalready
一定yí dìngmust, certainly
当然dāng ráncertainly, of course
先xiānfirst

Sentences - Nationality & Origin

ChinesePinyinEnglish
你是美国人吗？nǐ shì měi guó rén ma?Are you American?
对，我是美国人。duì, wǒ shì měi guó rén.Yes, I'm American.
你是哪国人？nǐ shì nǎ guó rén?What nationality are you?
他是哪儿的人？tā shì nǎr de rén?Where is he from?
他是东京人。tā shì dōng jīng rén.He is from Tokyo.
我猜他是日本人。wǒ cāi tā shì rì běn rén.I guess he is Japanese.
我觉得他是日本人。wǒ jué de tā shì rì běn rén.I think he is Japanese.

我的家乡在北京。wǒ de jiā xiāng zài běi jīng.My hometown is in Beijing.
北京在哪儿？běi jīng zài nǎr?Where is Beijing?
北京在中国。běi jīng zài zhōng guó.Beijing is in China.
北京是中国的大城市。běi jīng shì zhōng guó de dà chéng shì.Beijing is a big city in China.
大龙，来自美国。dà lóng, lái zì měi guó.Da Long, comes from America.
这是哪国的？zhè shì nǎ guó de?Which country is this from?
这是美国的。zhè shì měi guó de.This is from America.
我从北京到上海旅游。wǒ cóng běi jīng dào shàng hǎi lǚ yóu.I travel from Beijing to Shanghai.
我12月从波兰到中国旅游。wǒ shí èr yuè cóng bō lán dào zhōng guó lǚ yóu.In December I travel from Poland to China.
我们美国人。wǒ men měi guó rén.We are American.

Nominal Predicate Sentences: In Chinese, nouns can serve as predicates directly (without 是). Common for age, dates, nationality, and names. E.g., 他22岁 (NOT 他是22岁).

25. Pinyin Tone Examples (声调 shēng diào)

The 4 tones of Chinese:
1st tone (ˉ) - high and level: mā 妈 (mother)
2nd tone (ˊ) - rising: má 麻 (fibrous crops)
3rd tone (ˇ) - dip then rise: mǎ 马 (horse)
4th tone (ˋ) - falling: mà 骂 (to scold)
Neutral tone - light and short: ma 吗 (question particle)
ChinesePinyinEnglish
妈mā (1st tone)mother
麻má (2nd tone)fibrous crops
马mǎ (3rd tone)horse
骂mà (4th tone)to scold
Tone Sandhi Rule (3rd + 3rd):
When two 3rd-tone syllables appear together, the first changes to 2nd tone.
Example: 你好 nǐ hǎo → pronounced as ní hǎo (but written as nǐ hǎo)

"一" tone changes:
- yì + 1st/2nd/3rd tone (reads as 4th tone): 一只 yì zhī
- yí + 4th tone (reads as 2nd tone): 一个 yí gè
- yī when counting: 一二三 yī èr sān
- yāo in phone numbers: 110 = yāo yāo líng

28. Dialogue Practice (对话练习 duì huà liàn xí)

Dialogue: Guessing the Girlfriend (猜女朋友)
Zhang Li and Da Long discuss a photo. This dialogue practices describing appearance, using 又…又… and A-not-A questions.
ChinesePinyinEnglish
哪个是你女朋友？nǎ gè shì nǐ nǚ péng yǒu?Which one is your girlfriend?
你猜。她头发很长。nǐ cāi. tā tóu fa hěn cháng.Guess. Her hair is very long.
是她吗？shì tā ma?Is it her?
不是。我女朋友眼睛很大。bú shì. wǒ nǚ péng yǒu yǎn jīng hěn dà.No. My girlfriend's eyes are very big.
她个子高吗？tā gè zi gāo ma?Is she tall?
她又高又瘦。tā yòu gāo yòu shòu.She is both tall and thin.
是她吗？shì tā ma?Is it her?
对。你觉得她怎么样？duì. nǐ jué de tā zěn me yàng?Yes. What do you think of her?
她很漂亮，也很可爱。tā hěn piào liang, yě hěn kě ài.She is very beautiful and also cute.
哈哈，你猜错了！hā ha, nǐ cāi cuò le!Haha, you guessed wrong!
这是我姐姐。我没有女朋友。zhè shì wǒ jiě jie. wǒ méi yǒu nǚ péng yǒu.This is my elder sister. I don't have a girlfriend.
A Day in Chinese Time Click each period to see details and common activities · times as taught in class (Lesson 28)

下午 xià wǔ Afternoon 13:00 - 19:00

wǒ měi tiān zǎo shàng liù diǎn bàn qǐ chuáng.

tā líng chén yī diǎn cái shuì jiào.
He doesn't go to sleep until 1 AM. (才 = so late!)
Second option — 夜里 (yè lǐ): "at night" in general, any late hour. 凌晨 is the exact name for midnight to 5 AM: 凌晨两点 = 2 AM, 凌晨四点 = 4 AM.

Step 1: When? Start here!

The 3 most useful time words in Chinese

昨天zuó tiānyesterday
今天jīn tiāntoday
明天míng tiāntomorrow
Notice the pattern: they all end in 天 (tiān) = day.
昨 (zuó) = past, 今 (jīn) = present, 明 (míng) = bright/next.

Try saying:
今天星期几？ jīn tiān xīng qī jǐ? — What day is today?
昨天很冷。 zuó tiān hěn lěng. — Yesterday was cold.
明天见！ míng tiān jiàn! — See you tomorrow!

Step 2: What time is it?

Just 3 words to tell time: number + 点 + (半)

The Simple Formula
Number + 点 diǎn = o'clock!
That's it! 三点 (sān diǎn) = 3 o'clock. Add 半 (bàn) for :30. Done!
1:00一点yī diǎn
3:00三点sān diǎn
3:30三点半sān diǎn bàn
7:30七点半qī diǎn bàn
12:00十二点shí èr diǎn
Want to be more specific? Add the time of day BEFORE the number:
早上七点 zǎo shàng qī diǎn — 7:00 AM (morning 7 o'clock)
晚上七点 wǎn shàng qī diǎn — 7:00 PM (evening 7 o'clock)
下午三点半 xià wǔ sān diǎn bàn — 3:30 PM (afternoon 3:30)
凌晨两点 líng chén liǎng diǎn — 2:00 AM (small hours) — 两点, never 二点!

Step 3: Have a conversation!

Learn by practicing real questions and answers

现在几点？xiàn zài jǐ diǎn?
What time is it now?
现在早上八点半。
xiàn zài zǎo shàng bā diǎn bàn.
It's 8:30 AM now.
你几点起床？nǐ jǐ diǎn qǐ chuáng?
What time do you get up?
我早上六点起床。
wǒ zǎo shàng liù diǎn qǐ chuáng.
I get up at 6:00 AM.
你几点上课？nǐ jǐ diǎn shàng kè?
What time does class start?
我上午八点上课。
wǒ shàng wǔ bā diǎn shàng kè.
My class starts at 8 AM.
你几点吃晚饭？nǐ jǐ diǎn chī wǎn fàn?
What time do you eat dinner?
我晚上七点吃晚饭。
wǒ wǎn shàng qī diǎn chī wǎn fàn.
I eat dinner at 7 PM.
你几点睡觉？nǐ jǐ diǎn shuì jiào?
What time do you sleep?
我晚上十一点睡觉。
wǒ wǎn shàng shí yī diǎn shuì jiào.
I sleep at 11 PM.
你一般几点起床？nǐ yì bān jǐ diǎn qǐ chuáng?
What time do you usually get up?
一般来说，我早上七点起床。你呢？
yì bān lái shuō, wǒ zǎo shang qī diǎn qǐ chuáng. nǐ ne?
Generally I get up at 7 AM. And you?
你几点上班？几点下班？nǐ jǐ diǎn shàng bān? jǐ diǎn xià bān?
When do you start and finish work?
我上午九点上班，下午六点下班。
wǒ shàng wǔ jiǔ diǎn shàng bān, xià wǔ liù diǎn xià bān.
I start work at 9 AM and finish at 6 PM.
The magic question word: 几点 (jǐ diǎn) = "what time?"
Just put 几点 where the time would go:
你几点起床？ → 我六点起床。
你几点吃饭？ → 我七点吃饭。

From class (Lessons 26–27): 一般来说 (yì bān lái shuō) = generally / usually, put it at the start. Bounce the question back with 你呢？ (nǐ ne?) = and you?

Step 4: Days of the week

You already know the numbers 1-6. Just add 星期!

Super easy pattern: 星期 (xīng qī) + number = day of the week!
Monday = 星期一, Tuesday = 星期二, ... Saturday = 星期六
Sunday is the exception: 星期天 or 星期日 (not 星期七!)
星期一xīng qī yīMonday
星期二xīng qī èrTuesday
星期三xīng qī sānWednesday
星期四xīng qī sìThursday
星期五xīng qī wǔFriday
星期六xīng qī liùSaturday
星期天xīng qī tiānSunday
Second option — 周 (zhōu), from class (Lesson 17): shorter and very common in everyday speech. Same pattern: 周 + number. Sunday = 周天 or 周日.
Ask: 今天周几？ (jīn tiān zhōu jǐ?) = What day is it today? · 周末 = weekend
周一zhōu yīMonday
周二zhōu èrTuesday
周三zhōu sānWednesday
周四zhōu sìThursday
周五zhōu wǔFriday
周六zhōu liùSaturday
周天 / 周日zhōu tiān / zhōu rìSunday
周末zhōu mòweekend
周几？zhōu jǐ?which day?

Step 5: Last, This, Next

One simple pattern works for weeks, months, and years

The magic pattern:
上 (shàng) = last   |   这 (zhè) = this   |   下 (xià) = next

Apply it to anything: 上个星期 = last week, 这个月 = this month, 下年... wait, years are different! See below.
LastThisNext
Day前天 qián tiān
day before yesterday		昨天 / 今天
yesterday / today		明天 / 后天
tomorrow / day after
Week上个星期
shàng gè xīng qī		这个星期
zhè gè xīng qī		下个星期
xià gè xīng qī
Month上个月
shàng gè yuè		这个月
zhè gè yuè		下个月
xià gè yuè
Year去年
qù nián		今年
jīn nián		明年
míng nián
Before & after (for events):
吃饭以前 chī fàn yǐ qián — before eating
吃饭以后 chī fàn yǐ hòu — after eating

Try saying: 吃饭以后，我去散步。After eating, I go for a walk.

From class (Lesson 27): 以后 / 以前 go AFTER the action:
起床以后，我去公园跑步。 qǐ chuáng yǐ hòu, wǒ qù gōng yuán pǎo bù — after getting up, I go running in the park
睡觉以前，我看书。 shuì jiào yǐ qián, wǒ kàn shū — before sleeping, I read

Step 6: Time building blocks

From seconds to years — smallest to biggest

second秒miǎo
minute分钟fēn zhōng
hour小时xiǎo shí
day天tiān
week星期 / 周xīng qī / zhōu
month月yuè
year年nián
How to say "how long": just put a number in front!
五分钟 = 5 minutes  |  两个小时 = 2 hours  |  三天 = 3 days
两个星期 = 2 weeks  |  六个月 = 6 months  |  一年 = 1 year

Note: 小时 (hour) and 星期 (week) need 个 (gè) before them: 两个小时, 三个星期.

Step 7: Beyond whole hours

Once you're comfortable with 点 and 半, try these

分fēnminute (on clock)
刻kèquarter (15 min)
差chà"to" (lacking)
2:15两点十五分 / 两点一刻liǎng diǎn shí wǔ fēn / liǎng diǎn yí kè
6:05六点零五分liù diǎn líng wǔ fēn
7:10七点十分qī diǎn shí fēn
4:45四点四十五 / 差一刻五点sì diǎn sì shí wǔ / chà yí kè wǔ diǎn
8:50八点五十 / 差十分九点bā diǎn wǔ shí / chà shí fēn jiǔ diǎn
Tip: For minutes under 10, add 零 (líng) = zero: 六点零五分 = 6:05.
差 (chà) means "lacking" — think of it like English "10 to 9": 差十分九点.

Step 8: Tell your day as a story!

Put it all together with 从 (from) ... 到 (to)

Pattern: 从 + start time + 到 + end time + , + I do something
Example: 从七点到八点，我吃早饭。From 7 to 8, I eat breakfast.
6:00 - 6:30
从早上六点到六点半，我起床、洗脸、刷牙。 cóng zǎo shàng liù diǎn dào liù diǎn bàn, wǒ qǐ chuáng, xǐ liǎn, shuā yá. From 6:00 to 6:30 AM, I get up, wash my face, and brush my teeth.
6:30 - 7:00
从六点半到七点，我吃早饭，喝咖啡。 cóng liù diǎn bàn dào qī diǎn, wǒ chī zǎo fàn, hē kā fēi. From 6:30 to 7:00, I eat breakfast and drink coffee.
8:00 - 12:00
从上午八点到十二点，我上课。 cóng shàng wǔ bā diǎn dào shí èr diǎn, wǒ shàng kè. From 8 AM to 12, I attend class.
12:00 - 13:00
从中午十二点到一点，我吃午饭、休息。 cóng zhōng wǔ shí èr diǎn dào yī diǎn, wǒ chī wǔ fàn, xiū xi. From noon to 1:00, I eat lunch and rest.
13:00 - 17:00
从下午一点到五点，我工作。 cóng xià wǔ yī diǎn dào wǔ diǎn, wǒ gōng zuò. From 1 to 5 PM, I work.
19:00
我晚上七点吃晚饭。 wǒ wǎn shàng qī diǎn chī wǎn fàn. I eat dinner at 7 PM.
20:00 - 22:00
从晚上八点到十点，我复习中文、看电影。 cóng wǎn shàng bā diǎn dào shí diǎn, wǒ fù xí zhōng wén, kàn diàn yǐng. From 8 to 10 PM, I review Chinese and watch movies.
22:30
我晚上十点半睡觉。 wǒ wǎn shàng shí diǎn bàn shuì jiào. I go to sleep at 10:30 PM.

Step 9: Early or late?

From class (Lesson 27): say how you feel about the time

早zǎoearly
晚wǎnlate
就…了jiù … lealready (earlier than expected)
才cáinot until (later than expected)
怎么zěn mehow come
那么nà meso (that much)
就…了 = earlier than you'd expect: 他六点就起床了。tā liù diǎn jiù qǐ chuáng le. — He's up at 6 already!
才 = later than you'd expect (NO 了!): 他十点才起床。tā shí diǎn cái qǐ chuáng. — He doesn't get up until 10!
Try saying:
你怎么那么早起床？ nǐ zěn me nà me zǎo qǐ chuáng? — How come you get up so early?
你怎么那么晚睡觉？ nǐ zěn me nà me wǎn shuì jiào? — How come you go to bed so late?
我凌晨一点才睡觉。 wǒ líng chén yī diǎn cái shuì jiào. — I don't go to sleep until 1 AM.

Step 10: How often? & the seasons

From class (Lesson 28): 每 + period + 两次, and the four seasons

每天两次měi tiān liǎng cìtwice a day
每周两次měi zhōu liǎng cìtwice a week
每月两次měi yuè liǎng cìtwice a month
每年两次měi nián liǎng cìtwice a year
Pattern: 每 + period + verb + number + 次 + object. "Two times" = 两次, never 二次.
Example: 我每周去两次超市。wǒ měi zhōu qù liǎng cì chāo shì. — I go to the supermarket twice a week.
季节jì jiéseason
春天 🌸chūn tiānspring
夏天 🌳xià tiānsummer
秋天 🍂qiū tiānautumn
冬天 ❄️dōng tiānwinter
Every season = something + 天, just like 今天 and 明天.
你最喜欢什么季节？ nǐ zuì xǐ huan shén me jì jié? — What's your favorite season?
我最喜欢秋天。 wǒ zuì xǐ huan qiū tiān. — I like autumn best.
Chinese Question Words Click any card to flip and see example sentences

shén me
What?
tap to flip

What's your name?

nǐ yào hē shén me?
What do you want to drink?

nǐ mèi mei zuò shén me gōng zuò?

nǐ shǔ shén me?
What's your zodiac sign?

shéi gèng zhā?

几 / 多少

How many? / How much?

几/多少 - How many/much?

nǐ jǐ suì?
How old are you? (to kids)

zhè ge duō shǎo qián?

tā de diàn huà hào mǎ shì duō shǎo?
What is his phone number?
几 = small numbers (under ~10), 多少 = any amount

How long?

cóng běi jīng dào shàng hǎi yào duō jiǔ?
How long from Beijing to Shanghai?

nǐ xué zhōng wén duō jiǔ le?

dà gài yí gè duō xiǎo shí.
About a little over an hour.

为什么 - Why?

nǐ wèi shén me xué zhōng wén?
Why do you study Chinese?
为什么不去？
wèi shén me bú qù?

nǐ wèi shén me bù chī?
Why don't you eat?
你觉得…？
nǐ jué de...?
What do you think...?
tap to flip

你觉得 - What do you think?

nǐ jué de tā zěn me yàng?
What do you think of her?
我觉得他很帅。
wǒ jué de tā hěn shuài.
I think he's handsome.
我觉得他是日本人。
wǒ jué de tā shì rì běn rén.
I think he's Japanese.
Pattern: 你觉得 + [topic] + 怎么样？
Use 觉得 (jué de) to express opinions and feelings.
再说一遍
zài shuō yí biàn
Can you repeat?
tap to flip

Asking to repeat

请再说一遍。
qǐng zài shuō yí biàn.
Please say it again.
你可以再说一遍吗？
nǐ kě yǐ zài shuō yí biàn ma?
Can you say it again?
对不起，我没听懂。
duì bu qǐ, wǒ méi tīng dǒng.
Sorry, I didn't understand.
慢一点
màn yì diǎn
Can you speak slower?
tap to flip

Asking to slow down

请说慢一点。
qǐng shuō màn yì diǎn.
Please speak slower.
你可以说慢一点吗？
nǐ kě yǐ shuō màn yì diǎn ma?
Can you speak a bit slower?
太快了，我听不懂。
tài kuài le, wǒ tīng bù dǒng.
Too fast, I can't understand.
The 4 Tones of Chinese Click each tone to see examples - tones change the meaning completely!
1st Tone ā high & level
2nd Tone á rising
3rd Tone ǎ dip then rise
4th Tone à sharp falling
Neutral a light & short

1st Tone (ˉ) - High and Level

Keep your voice high and flat, like singing a sustained note.

妈māmother
他tāhe/she
书shūbook
喝hēto drink
吃chīto eat
天tiānsky/day
飞fēito fly
猫māocat

2nd Tone (ˊ) - Rising

Voice rises from middle to high, like asking "huh?" in English.

麻máhemp
人rénperson
来láito come
国guócountry
学xuéto study
名míngname
牛niúcow
茶chátea

3rd Tone (ˇ) - Dip then Rise

Voice dips low then rises back up, like a surprised "really?"

马mǎhorse
你nǐyou
我wǒI/me
好hǎogood
小xiǎosmall
水shuǐwater
狗gǒudog
买mǎito buy

4th Tone (ˋ) - Sharp Falling

Voice drops sharply from high to low, like a firm command: "Stop!"

骂màto scold
大dàbig
是shìto be
去qùto go
对duìcorrect
看kànto look
快kuàifast
叫jiàoto call

Neutral Tone - Light and Short

Quick, unstressed syllable. Usually the second syllable in a word. No tone mark in pinyin.

吗maquestion particle
呢neand you?
的depossessive
了lecompleted action
吧basuggestion

Minimal Pairs - Same Sound, Different Meaning

The same syllable with different tones becomes a completely different word!

妈māmother
vs
马mǎhorse
买mǎito buy
vs
卖màito sell
是shìto be
vs
十shíten
书shūbook
vs
树shùtree
汤tāngsoup
vs
糖tángsugar
杯bēicup
vs
北běinorth

Tone Change Rules (变调 biàn diào)

3rd + 3rd = 2nd + 3rd

When two 3rd-tone syllables are together, the first one changes to 2nd tone in speech (but pinyin stays the same).

你好   nǐ hǎo → pronounced ní hǎo
我也   wǒ yě → pronounced wó yě

一 (yī) Tone Changes

The number one changes tone depending on what follows:

Before 4th tone → yí:   一个 yí gè (one [item])
Before 1st/2nd/3rd tone → yì:   一只 yì zhī (one [animal])
When counting alone → yī:   一二三 yī èr sān

不 (bù) Tone Changes

不 normally has 4th tone, but changes before another 4th tone:

Before 4th tone → bú:   不是 bú shì (is not)
Before other tones → bù:   不好 bù hǎo (not good)

一 in Phone Numbers

In phone numbers and addresses, 一 is read as yāo (1st tone) to avoid confusion with 七 (qī).

110 = yāo yāo líng (not yī yī líng)

很少

tā hé gēge dōu zài jīchǎng gōngzuò
He and his older brother both work at the airport.
地铁站
tap to flip
dìtiězhàn
subway station (钅 = metal rail, 立 = stand)
我家附近有两个地铁站。
wǒ jiā fùjìn yǒu liǎng ge dìtiězhàn
There are two subway stations near my home.
累
tap to flip
lèi
tired
我很累，不想去公园。
wǒ hěn lèi, bù xiǎng qù gōngyuán
I'm very tired, I don't want to go to the park.
裙子
tap to flip
qúnzi
skirt / dress (衤 = clothing)
妹妹非常爱绿色的裙子。
mèimei fēicháng ài lǜsè de qúnzi
My younger sister really loves green skirts.
鸡蛋
tap to flip
jīdàn
egg
我每天都吃一个鸡蛋。
wǒ měitiān dōu chī yí ge jīdàn
I eat an egg every day.
爱
tap to flip
ài
to love
妹妹非常爱绿色的裙子。
mèimei fēicháng ài lǜsè de qúnzi
My younger sister really loves green skirts.

What this lesson covers
Introducing yourself, asking about nationality, family words, pronouns, and the possessive 的. These are the building blocks of every Chinese conversation!
Step 1 — New Words: Read through the vocabulary first. These are the ingredients you need before building sentences.

New Words

爸爸 bàba dad
妈妈 māma mom
哥哥 gēge older brother
弟弟 dìdi younger brother
辣 là spicy
佛 fó Buddha
饿 è hungry
狗屁 gǒupì nonsense (slang)
不 bù not, no
女 nǚ female, woman
绿色 lǜsè green (color)
你 nǐ you
我 wǒ I, me
他 tā he, him
她 tā she, her
中国 zhōngguó China
美国 měiguó USA
英国 yīngguó England
人 rén person
是 shì to be (am/is/are)
吗 ma question particle
对 duì correct, right
不对 bú duì not correct, wrong
也 yě also, too
知道 zhīdào to know
可能 kěnéng maybe, possibly
的 de 's (possessive)
叫 jiào to be called
什么 shénme what
名字 míngzì name
忘 wàng to forget
Step 2 — Expressions: Now see how these words combine into real sentences!
T
我是中国人。
wǒ shì zhōngguó rén.
I am Chinese.
P
我不是美国人。
wǒ bú shì měiguó rén.
I am not American.
T
你是中国人吗？
nǐ shì zhōngguó rén ma?
Are you Chinese?
P
对 / 不对
duì / bú duì
Correct / Not correct
T
你是哪国人？
nǐ shì nǎ guó rén?
What nationality are you?
P
我也是中国人。
wǒ yě shì zhōngguó rén.
I am also Chinese.
T
我不知道。
wǒ bù zhīdào.
I don't know.
P
他可能是美国人。
tā kěnéng shì měiguó rén.
He might be American.
T
你叫什么名字？
nǐ jiào shénme míngzì?
What is your name?
P
你的爸爸叫什么名字？
nǐ de bàba jiào shénme míngzì?
What is your dad's name?
T
我忘了。
wǒ wàng le.
I forgot.

Grammar Spotlight

...shì...rén — ...是...人 — saying nationality
Country + 人 = person from that country. Use 是 (shì, to be) to connect.
我是中国人。
wǒ shì zhōngguó rén.
I am Chinese. (lit: I am China-person)
bú shì — 不是 — "am not / is not"
Put 不 (bù) before 是 (shì) to make it negative. Simple!
我不是美国人。
wǒ bú shì měiguó rén.
I am not American.
...ma? — ...吗？ — yes/no question
Add 吗 (ma) at the end of any statement to make it a question. Word order stays the same!
你是中国人。→ 你是中国人吗？
nǐ shì zhōngguó rén. → nǐ shì zhōngguó rén ma?
You are Chinese. → Are you Chinese?
de — 的 — possessive ('s)
Person + 的 (de) + thing = person's thing.
你的 = your, 我的 = my, 他的 = his, 她的 = her
nǐ de, wǒ de, tā de, tā de
yě — 也 — "also" (goes BEFORE the verb)
也 (yě) always goes right before the verb, unlike English.
我也是中国人。
wǒ yě shì zhōngguó rén.
I am also Chinese.
kěnéng — 可能 — "maybe / possibly"
Put 可能 (kěnéng) before the verb when you're not sure.
他可能是美国人。
tā kěnéng shì měiguó rén.
He might be American.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Say "I am American" in Chinese:
我是美国人。(wǒ shì měiguó rén)
2. Ask someone "Are you British?"
你是英国人吗？(nǐ shì yīngguó rén ma?)
3. Say "She is not Chinese":
她不是中国人。(tā bú shì zhōngguó rén)
4. Ask "What is your name?"
你叫什么名字？(nǐ jiào shénme míngzì?)
5. Say "He might also be British":
他也可能是英国人。(tā yě kěnéng shì yīngguó rén)
The Scene
Zhang Li and Da Long are looking at photos. Zhang Li wants to guess which girl is Da Long's girlfriend. Da Long gives clues about her appearance...
ZL
哪个是你女朋友？
nǎ gè shì nǐ nǚ péngyǒu?
Which one is your girlfriend?
DL
你猜。她头发很长。
nǐ cāi. tā tóufa hěn cháng.
You guess. Her hair is very long.
ZL
是她吗？
shì tā ma?
Is it her?
DL
不是。我女朋友眼睛很大。
bú shì. wǒ nǚ péngyǒu yǎnjīng hěn dà.
No. My girlfriend has very big eyes.
ZL
她个子高吗？
tā gèzi gāo ma?
Is she tall?
DL
她又高又瘦。
tā yòu gāo yòu shòu.
She is both tall and thin.
ZL
是她吗？
shì tā ma?
Is it her?
DL
对。你觉得她怎么样？
duì. nǐ juéde tā zěnmeyàng?
Yes. What do you think of her?
ZL
她很漂亮，也很可爱。
tā hěn piàoliang, yě hěn kě'ài.
She's very pretty, and also very cute.
DL
哈哈，你猜错了！这是我姐姐。我没有女朋友。
hāha, nǐ cāi cuò le! zhè shì wǒ jiějie. wǒ méi yǒu nǚ péngyǒu.
Haha, you guessed wrong! This is my older sister. I don't have a girlfriend.

Key Vocabulary

女朋友 nǚ péngyǒu girlfriend
猜 cāi to guess
头发 tóufa hair
长 cháng long
眼睛 yǎnjīng eyes
个子 gèzi height
高 gāo tall
瘦 shòu thin
漂亮 piàoliang pretty
可爱 kě'ài cute
觉得 juéde to think
姐姐 jiějie older sister
错 cuò wrong
又...又... yòu...yòu... both...and...

Grammar Spotlight

yòu A yòu B — 又 A 又 B — "both A and B"
Describe two qualities at the same time.
她又高又瘦。
tā yòu gāo yòu shòu.
She is both tall and thin.
nǐ juéde...zěnmeyàng? — 你觉得...怎么样？
A natural way to ask someone's opinion = "What do you think of...?"
你觉得她怎么样？
nǐ juéde tā zěnmeyàng?
What do you think of her?
verb + cuò + le — verb + 错 + 了 — "did it wrong"
Add 错 (cuò) after a verb = did it incorrectly. 了 (le) = it happened.
你猜错了！
nǐ cāi cuò le!
You guessed wrong!
Subject + hěn + Adjective — 很 — describing people
No "is" needed — just 很 (hěn) + adjective.
她头发很长。
tā tóufa hěn cháng.
Her hair (is) very long.

Test Yourself

Tap the blurred answer to reveal!

1. Da Long's girlfriend has long hair — true or false?
Trick question! He doesn't have a girlfriend. The girl is his older sister (姐姐).
2. What does 又高又瘦 mean?
Both tall and thin — the 又...又... pattern means "both...and..."
3. How do you ask "What do you think of her?"
你觉得她怎么样？(nǐ juéde tā zěnmeyàng?)
4. Say "I don't have a girlfriend" in Chinese:
我没有女朋友。(wǒ méi yǒu nǚ péngyǒu)
Connection: Notice 吗 and 是 appear in both lessons! In Lesson 1 you learned 你是中国人吗？ — and here Zhang Li uses the same pattern: 是她吗？ Same grammar, different topic!
What this lesson covers
Fruit vocabulary, saying what you like and dislike with 喜欢, and a reading passage about Lily's family. This lesson also reviews nationality patterns from Lesson 1!
Step 1 — Homework Review: These sentences use patterns from Lesson 1. Can you read them out loud before peeking at the pinyin?
T
你是美国人吗？
nǐ shì měiguó rén ma?
Are you American?
P
狗屁！不对。我是波兰人。
gǒupì! bú duì. wǒ shì Bōlán rén.
Nonsense! Wrong. I am Polish.
T
你妈妈叫什么名字？
nǐ māma jiào shénme míngzì?
What is your mom's name?
P
我忘了。
wǒ wàng le.
I forgot.
T
你知道她是哪国人吗？
nǐ zhīdào tā shì nǎ guó rén ma?
Do you know what nationality she is?
P
她可能是美国人。
tā kěnéng shì měiguó rén.
She might be American.
T
我哥哥是中国人，他不是英国人，也不是美国人。
wǒ gēge shì zhōngguó rén, tā bú shì yīngguó rén, yě bú shì měiguó rén.
My older brother is Chinese, he is not British, and not American either.
Step 2 — New Words: Today's topic: fruits! Learn these fruit names — you'll need them to express your likes and dislikes.

New Words — Fruits

苹果 píngguǒ apple
西瓜 xīguā watermelon
芒果 mángguǒ mango
香蕉 xiāngjiāo banana
草莓 cǎoméi strawberry
葡萄 pútáo grape
椰子 yēzi coconut
桔子 júzi tangerine
橙子 chéngzi orange
榴莲 liúlián durian

Key Expressions

喜欢 xǐhuān to like
很喜欢 hěn xǐhuān really like
不喜欢 bù xǐhuān don't like
一般般 yībānbān so-so
香 xiāng fragrant
臭 chòu smelly
Step 3 — Conversation Practice: Use 喜欢 to talk about fruit preferences. Three possible answers: 很喜欢, 不喜欢, or 一般般!
T
你喜欢苹果吗？
nǐ xǐhuān píngguǒ ma?
Do you like apples?
P
很喜欢！
hěn xǐhuān!
I really like them!
T
你喜欢榴莲吗？
nǐ xǐhuān liúlián ma?
Do you like durian?
P
不喜欢！很臭！
bù xǐhuān! hěn chòu!
I don't like it! Very smelly!
T
你喜欢芒果吗？
nǐ xǐhuān mángguǒ ma?
Do you like mangoes?
P
一般般。
yībānbān.
So-so.
T
你喜欢西瓜吗？
nǐ xǐhuān xīguā ma?
Do you like watermelon?
P
很喜欢！很香！
hěn xǐhuān! hěn xiāng!
I really like it! So fragrant!
Step 4 — Reading Practice: Read this paragraph about Lily. It uses patterns from Lesson 1 (是...人, 的, 叫, 哪国人). Try reading it out loud!
Reading: Lily's Family
Lily是美国人，她的妈妈也是美国人，爸爸是中国人。
Lily shì měiguó rén, tā de māma yě shì měiguó rén, bàba shì zhōngguó rén.
Lily is American. Her mom is also American. Her dad is Chinese.

她的哥哥叫Kevin，弟弟叫Jason。
tā de gēge jiào Kevin, dìdi jiào Jason.
Her older brother is called Kevin, her younger brother is called Jason.

我不知道Lily的哥哥弟弟是哪国人。
wǒ bù zhīdào Lily de gēge dìdi shì nǎ guó rén.
I don't know what nationality Lily's brothers are.

Grammar Spotlight

xǐhuān...ma? — 喜欢...吗？ — asking about likes
Use 喜欢 (xǐhuān, to like) + object + 吗 to ask if someone likes something. Same 吗 pattern from Lesson 1!
你喜欢苹果吗？
nǐ xǐhuān píngguǒ ma?
Do you like apples?
bù + verb — 不 + verb — negating verbs
In Lesson 1 you learned 不是 (not be). The same 不 works with any verb: 不喜欢, 不知道, etc.
我不喜欢榴莲。
wǒ bù xǐhuān liúlián.
I don't like durian.
hěn + adjective — 很 + adj — "very ___"
很 (hěn) goes directly before an adjective. No "is" needed — Chinese doesn't use 是 with adjectives!
榴莲很臭。香蕉很香。
liúlián hěn chòu. xiāngjiāo hěn xiāng.
Durian is very smelly. Bananas are very fragrant.
yībānbān — 一般般 — "so-so / just okay"
A casual way to say you feel neutral. Use it as a complete answer to 喜欢...吗 questions.
你喜欢芒果吗？一般般。
nǐ xǐhuān mángguǒ ma? yībānbān.
Do you like mangoes? So-so.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "Do you like watermelon?" in Chinese:
你喜欢西瓜吗？(nǐ xǐhuān xīguā ma?)
2. Say "I don't like durian, it's very smelly":
我不喜欢榴莲，很臭。(wǒ bù xǐhuān liúlián, hěn chòu)
3. What nationality is Lily's dad?
中国人 (zhōngguó rén) — Chinese
4. Say "I really like strawberries":
我很喜欢草莓。(wǒ hěn xǐhuān cǎoméi)
5. How do you say "so-so" when asked if you like something?
一般般 (yībānbān)
Connection: Notice the 吗 question pattern works exactly the same way! In Lesson 1: 你是中国人吗？ → Here: 你喜欢苹果吗？ Same structure, new verb!
What this lesson covers
Jobs and professions, asking "What do you do?" with 你是做什么的？, the plural pronouns 我们／你们／他们, and a reading passage plus self-introduction. This lesson reuses 是...人, 的, 叫 and 喜欢 from earlier lessons!
Step 1 — Homework Review: Words and expressions from last time. Read them out loud before peeking at the pinyin!

Review Words

我爱你 wǒ ài nǐ I love you
白色 báisè white
美国 měiguó USA
对 duì correct
包子 bāozi steamed bun
猫 māo cat
小偷 xiǎotōu thief
豆奶 dòunǎi soy milk
牛奶 niúnǎi (cow's) milk
Step 2 — New Words: Today's topic — jobs! These are the professions you need to say what people do for work.

New Words — Professions

老师 lǎoshī teacher
学生 xuésheng student
医生 yīshēng doctor
服务员 fúwùyuán waiter / server
律师 lǜshī lawyer
经理 jīnglǐ manager
会计 kuàijì accountant
不工作 bù gōngzuò doesn't work

Key Expressions

你是做什么的？ nǐ shì zuò shénme de? What do you do?
美女 měinǚ pretty woman
帅哥 shuàigē handsome guy
我们 wǒmen we / us
你们 nǐmen you (plural)
他们 tāmen they / them
Step 3 — Conversation Practice: Ask about jobs with 你是做什么的？ Answer with 我是 + profession.
T
你是做什么的？
nǐ shì zuò shénme de?
What do you do?
P
我是老师。
wǒ shì lǎoshī.
I'm a teacher.
T
你爸爸是做什么的？
nǐ bàba shì zuò shénme de?
What does your dad do?
P
我爸爸是医生，我妈妈是律师。
wǒ bàba shì yīshēng, wǒ māma shì lǜshī.
My dad is a doctor, my mom is a lawyer.
T
他呢？他是做什么的？
tā ne? tā shì zuò shénme de?
And him? What does he do?
P
他不工作。
tā bù gōngzuò.
He doesn't work.
Step 4 — Reading Practice: Read this passage out loud, then record a voice message. It reuses 是...人, 的, 叫 and 喜欢.
Reading: My Teacher Steve
我的老师是英国人，叫Steve。
wǒ de lǎoshī shì Yīngguó rén, jiào Steve.
My teacher is British, named Steve.

他的爸爸是医生，妈妈是律师。
tā de bàba shì yīshēng, māma shì lǜshī.
His dad is a doctor, his mom is a lawyer.

他们的猫叫"包子"。
tāmen de māo jiào "bāozi".
Their cat is called "Baozi" (steamed bun).

Steve老师喜欢西瓜，不喜欢草莓。
Steve lǎoshī xǐhuān xīguā, bù xǐhuān cǎoméi.
Teacher Steve likes watermelon, doesn't like strawberries.
Self-Introduction (example to memorize)
我叫Hao，我是中国人。
wǒ jiào Hǎo, wǒ shì Zhōngguó rén.
My name is Hao, I'm Chinese.

我的爸爸是中国人，我的妈妈也是中国人。
wǒ de bàba shì Zhōngguó rén, wǒ de māma yě shì Zhōngguó rén.
My dad is Chinese, my mom is also Chinese.

我是老师。我喜欢西瓜和葡萄，我不喜欢苹果。
wǒ shì lǎoshī. wǒ xǐhuān xīguā hé pútáo, wǒ bù xǐhuān píngguǒ.
I'm a teacher. I like watermelon and grapes, I don't like apples.

Grammar Spotlight

你是做什么的？— nǐ shì zuò shénme de? — asking someone's job
Literally "you are doing what one?" — the set phrase for asking what someone does for a living. Answer with 我是 + profession.
你是做什么的？我是会计。
nǐ shì zuò shénme de? wǒ shì kuàijì.
What do you do? I'm an accountant.
是 + profession — 我是老师 — stating a job
Use 是 (shì) + the job. No "a/an" needed — Chinese has no articles. Same 是 you used for nationality.
我妈妈是律师，我爸爸是医生。
wǒ māma shì lǜshī, wǒ bàba shì yīshēng.
My mom is a lawyer, my dad is a doctor.
pronoun + 们 — 我们／你们／他们 — making pronouns plural
Add 们 (men) to 我／你／他 to make "we / you (plural) / they". Simple and regular!
他们是学生，我们是老师。
tāmen shì xuésheng, wǒmen shì lǎoshī.
They are students, we are teachers.
也 — yě — "also / too"
也 goes right before the verb to mean "also". From the passage: 我的妈妈也是中国人.
我爸爸是中国人，我妈妈也是中国人。
wǒ bàba shì Zhōngguó rén, wǒ māma yě shì Zhōngguó rén.
My dad is Chinese, my mom is also Chinese.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "What do you do?" in Chinese:
你是做什么的？(nǐ shì zuò shénme de?)
2. Say "I am a teacher":
我是老师。(wǒ shì lǎoshī)
3. Say "My dad is a doctor, my mom is a lawyer":
我爸爸是医生，我妈妈是律师。(wǒ bàba shì yīshēng, wǒ māma shì lǜshī)
4. How do you say "they" (plural)?
他们 (tāmen)
5. Say "He doesn't work":
他不工作。(tā bù gōngzuò)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages and write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, then answer the questions. (2) Make five sentences with today's new words and patterns. (3) Read the passage above and send a voice message. (4) Write your own self-introduction based on the passage, memorize it, and record a video next class.
What this lesson covers
Colors, drinks, and a handful of everyday words. You'll learn to say what you like to drink, whether something is tasty, and what color things are — reusing 喜欢 and 是 from earlier lessons!
Step 1 — New Words: Drinks and a few everyday nouns. 喜欢 (to like) comes back from Lesson 2 — you'll pair it with 喝 (to drink).

New Words — Drinks & Everyday

咖啡 kāfēi coffee
啤酒 píjiǔ beer
白酒 báijiǔ baijiu (white liquor)
喝 hē to drink
好喝 hǎohē tasty (to drink)
好 hǎo good
喜欢 xǐhuān to like
去 qù to go
哥哥 gēge older brother
狗 gǒu dog
鸡 jī chicken
七 qī seven
Step 2 — New Words: Colors! Every color is just its name + 色 (sè, "color"). Learn the umbrella word 颜色 first.

New Words — Colors (颜色 yánsè)

颜色 yánsè color
红色 hóngsè red
蓝色 lánsè blue
黄色 huángsè yellow
绿色 lǜsè green
白色 báisè white

Extra Colors

黑色 hēisè black
紫色 zǐsè purple
橙色 chéngsè orange
Step 3 — Conversation Practice: Ask what someone likes to drink with 喜欢喝, check if it's tasty with 好喝吗？, and ask about colors with 什么颜色？
T
你喜欢喝什么？
nǐ xǐhuān hē shénme?
What do you like to drink?
P
我喜欢喝咖啡和啤酒。
wǒ xǐhuān hē kāfēi hé píjiǔ.
I like to drink coffee and beer.
T
白酒好喝吗？
báijiǔ hǎohē ma?
Is baijiu tasty?
P
不好喝！
bù hǎohē!
Not tasty!
T
你喜欢什么颜色？
nǐ xǐhuān shénme yánsè?
What color do you like?
P
我喜欢红色和蓝色。
wǒ xǐhuān hóngsè hé lánsè.
I like red and blue.
T
你的狗是什么颜色？
nǐ de gǒu shì shénme yánsè?
What color is your dog?
P
我的狗是白色的。
wǒ de gǒu shì báisè de.
My dog is white.

Grammar Spotlight

好 + verb — hǎo + verb — "nice to ___"
好 (good) before a sense verb makes "pleasant to ___": 好喝 = nice to drink (tasty). The same trick gives 好吃 (hǎochī, tasty to eat) and 好看 (hǎokàn, good-looking).
咖啡很好喝。
kāfēi hěn hǎohē.
Coffee is very tasty.
color + 色 — ___ + sè — naming colors
Add 色 (sè, "color") to a base word: 红色, 蓝色, 黄色, 绿色. 颜色 (yánsè) is the word "color" itself.
红色，蓝色，绿色，黄色。
hóngsè, lánsè, lǜsè, huángsè.
Red, blue, green, yellow.
A 是 [color] 的 — describing what color something is
Use 是 + color + 的 to say "X is [color]". The 的 makes it "a [color] one". Ask with 什么颜色？(what color?).
我的狗是白色的。
wǒ de gǒu shì báisè de.
My dog is white.
喜欢 + 喝 + noun — stacking verbs
喜欢 (to like) can be followed by another verb + its object: 喜欢喝咖啡 = "like to drink coffee". Same trick works with any action.
我喜欢喝啤酒，不喜欢喝白酒。
wǒ xǐhuān hē píjiǔ, bù xǐhuān hē báijiǔ.
I like drinking beer, I don't like drinking baijiu.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "What do you like to drink?" in Chinese:
你喜欢喝什么？(nǐ xǐhuān hē shénme?)
2. Say "I like to drink coffee":
我喜欢喝咖啡。(wǒ xǐhuān hē kāfēi)
3. Ask "Is beer tasty?":
啤酒好喝吗？(píjiǔ hǎohē ma?)
4. Say the colors red, blue, and green:
红色，蓝色，绿色 (hóngsè, lánsè, lǜsè)
5. Say "My dog is white":
我的狗是白色的。(wǒ de gǒu shì báisè de)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages and write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, then answer the questions and send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
Comparing what you prefer (更喜欢…还是…), saying "and" / "both" (和…都), talking about what you have (有 / 没有), family & pets, and counting people with 个. Builds straight on the colors from Lesson 4!
Step 1 — New Words: Preference and connecting words. These let you compare two things and answer "I like both" or "neither".

New Words — Prefer & Connect

更喜欢 gèng xǐhuān to prefer (like more)
还是 háishì or (in a question)
和 hé and
都 dōu all / both
都喜欢 dōu xǐhuān like both
Step 2 — New Words: 有 (to have) and the people, pets & counting words you'll talk about having.

New Words — Have, People & Pets

有 yǒu to have
没有 méiyǒu to not have
猫 māo cat
狗 gǒu dog
姐姐 jiějie older sister
妹妹 mèimei younger sister
朋友 péngyou friend
男朋友 nán péngyou boyfriend
女朋友 nǚ péngyou girlfriend
老公 lǎogōng husband
老婆 lǎopó wife
一个 yí ge one (of something)
两个 liǎng ge two (of something)
三个 sān ge three (of something)
Step 3 — New Words: Colors in action! These everyday words all reuse the color characters (红/绿/白/黑) from last lesson — a fun way to lock them in.

Colors in Action — Compound Words

红茶 hóngchá black tea (lit. red tea)
绿茶 lǜchá green tea
口红 kǒuhóng lipstick
红绿灯 hónglǜdēng traffic light
红包 hóngbāo red envelope (money gift)
红酒 hóngjiǔ red wine
白葡萄酒 bái pútáojiǔ white wine
鸡蛋 jīdàn egg
蛋白 dànbái egg white
蛋黄 dànhuáng egg yolk
黑马 hēimǎ dark horse
白马王子 báimǎ wángzǐ Prince Charming
Step 4 — Conversation Practice: Ask preferences with 更喜欢…还是…？, answer with 都 (both), and ask what someone has with 有…吗？
T
你更喜欢红色还是黄色？
nǐ gèng xǐhuān hóngsè háishì huángsè?
Do you prefer red or yellow?
P
我更喜欢红色。
wǒ gèng xǐhuān hóngsè.
I prefer red.
T
红色和黄色，你都喜欢吗？
hóngsè hé huángsè, nǐ dōu xǐhuān ma?
Red and yellow — do you like both?
P
红色和黄色，我都喜欢。
hóngsè hé huángsè, wǒ dōu xǐhuān.
Red and yellow, I like both.
T
你有猫吗？
nǐ yǒu māo ma?
Do you have a cat?
P
我没有猫，我有狗。
wǒ méiyǒu māo, wǒ yǒu gǒu.
I don't have a cat, I have a dog.
T
你有哥哥吗？
nǐ yǒu gēge ma?
Do you have older brothers?
P
我有两个哥哥。
wǒ yǒu liǎng ge gēge.
I have two older brothers.

Grammar Spotlight

更喜欢 A 还是 B？ — gèng xǐhuān A háishì B — "prefer A or B?"
更 (gèng) means "more", so 更喜欢 = "prefer". 还是 (háishì) is the "or" used in questions. Answer with 我更喜欢… (I prefer…).
你更喜欢茶还是咖啡？
nǐ gèng xǐhuān chá háishì kāfēi?
Do you prefer tea or coffee?
A 和 B，… 都… — A hé B, … dōu … — "and / both"
和 (hé) joins two nouns. 都 (dōu, all/both) goes before the verb to mean "both". Make it negative with 都不 = "neither".
红色和蓝色，我都喜欢。
hóngsè hé lánsè, wǒ dōu xǐhuān.
I like both red and blue.
有 / 没有 — yǒu / méiyǒu — to have / not have
有 means "to have". Important: negate it ONLY with 没 → 没有 (never 不有!). Ask a yes/no question with 有…吗？
我有狗，没有猫。
wǒ yǒu gǒu, méiyǒu māo.
I have a dog, I don't have a cat.
Number + 个 + noun — counting with 个
Put the measure word 个 (ge) between a number and a noun: 一个, 两个, 三个. Note: for counting you say 两 (liǎng), not 二, before 个!
我有两个姐姐。
wǒ yǒu liǎng ge jiějie.
I have two older sisters.
Culture corner — colors carry meaning: 红包 (hóngbāo) = a red envelope of money for celebrations. 红事 (hóngshì, "red affair") = a wedding/happy event; 白事 (báishì, "white affair") = a funeral. Watch out for 绿帽子 (lǜ màozi, "green hat") — it means someone's partner is cheating on them! And a 白马王子 (báimǎ wángzǐ, "prince on a white horse") is Prince Charming.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "Do you prefer red or yellow?":
你更喜欢红色还是黄色？(nǐ gèng xǐhuān hóngsè háishì huángsè?)
2. Say "Red and yellow, I like both":
红色和黄色，我都喜欢。(hóngsè hé huángsè, wǒ dōu xǐhuān)
3. Ask "Do you have a cat?":
你有猫吗？(nǐ yǒu māo ma?)
4. Say "I don't have a cat, I have a dog":
我没有猫，我有狗。(wǒ méiyǒu māo, wǒ yǒu gǒu)
5. Say "I have two older brothers":
我有两个哥哥。(wǒ yǒu liǎng ge gēge)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages and write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, then answer the questions and send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
Numbers 1–10, food & meats, and a few everyday words. You'll learn to say what you like to eat, whether it's tasty (好吃), and the names of meats — all built from 吃 (to eat) and the 好+verb trick from earlier lessons!
Step 1 — New Words: Numbers 1 to 10. You already know a few (二, 三, 七) — here's the full set.

New Words — Numbers 1–10

一 yī 1
二 èr 2
三 sān 3
四 sì 4
五 wǔ 5
六 liù 6
七 qī 7
八 bā 8
九 jiǔ 9
十 shí 10
Step 2 — New Words: Food, meat & drink. 吃 (to eat) pairs with 好 to make 好吃 (tasty), just like 好喝 from Lesson 4.

New Words — Food, Meat & Drink

吃 chī to eat
好吃 hǎochī tasty (to eat)
肉 ròu meat
牛肉 niúròu beef
猪肉 zhūròu pork
鸡肉 jīròu chicken (meat)
包子 bāozi steamed bun
中国菜 zhōngguócài Chinese food
茶 chá tea
水 shuǐ water
杯子 bēizi cup / glass
Step 3 — New Words: Animals, people & everyday words.

New Words — Animals, People & Everyday

猪 zhū pig
蛇 shé snake
爷爷 yéye grandpa (paternal)
外国人 wàiguórén foreigner
老外 lǎowài foreigner (casual)
日本 Rìběn Japan
厕所 cèsuǒ toilet
知道 zhīdào to know
白色 báisè white
⚠️ Slang warning — 傻逼 (shǎbī): This came up in the lesson, but it's a very rude swear word (roughly "dumbass / idiot"). Recognize it so you know when someone's being offensive — but do not say it. It will seriously insult people.
Step 4 — New Words: Connector words to build longer sentences — link ideas with "but", give reasons with "because… so…", and compare things with 比 (than).

New Words — Connectors & Comparison

但是 dànshì but
可是 kěshì but (casual)
因为 yīnwèi because
所以 suǒyǐ so / therefore
比 bǐ than (comparison)
或者 huòzhě or (in a statement)
这个 zhège this one
那个 nàge that one
Step 5 — Conversation Practice: Ask what someone likes to eat with 喜欢吃, check if it's tasty with 好吃吗？, offer a choice with 还是, and contrast with 但是 (but).
T
你喜欢吃中国菜吗？
nǐ xǐhuān chī zhōngguócài ma?
Do you like eating Chinese food?
P
我喜欢吃中国菜。
wǒ xǐhuān chī zhōngguócài.
I like eating Chinese food.
T
包子好吃吗？
bāozi hǎochī ma?
Are baozi tasty?
P
包子很好吃！
bāozi hěn hǎochī!
Baozi are very tasty!
T
你吃牛肉还是猪肉？
nǐ chī niúròu háishì zhūròu?
Do you eat beef or pork?
P
我吃鸡肉。
wǒ chī jīròu.
I eat chicken.
T
你喝水还是喝茶？
nǐ hē shuǐ háishì hē chá?
Do you drink water or tea?
P
我喝茶。
wǒ hē chá.
I drink tea.
T
牛肉比鸡肉好吃吗？
niúròu bǐ jīròu hǎochī ma?
Is beef tastier than chicken?
P
牛肉很好吃，但是我喜欢鸡肉。
niúròu hěn hǎochī, dànshì wǒ xǐhuān jīròu.
Beef is tasty, but I like chicken.

Grammar Spotlight

好 + 吃 → 好吃 — hǎo + chī — "tasty to eat"
Same trick as 好喝 (tasty to drink): 好 (good) + a sense verb = "nice to ___". 好吃 = good to eat. 好看 (hǎokàn) = good-looking.
包子很好吃。
bāozi hěn hǎochī.
Baozi are very tasty.
animal + 肉 — ___ + ròu — naming meats
Add 肉 (ròu, "meat") to an animal: 牛 (cow) → 牛肉 (beef), 猪 (pig) → 猪肉 (pork), 鸡 (chicken) → 鸡肉 (chicken meat).
我喜欢吃牛肉，不喜欢吃猪肉。
wǒ xǐhuān chī niúròu, bù xǐhuān chī zhūròu.
I like eating beef, I don't like eating pork.
country + 菜 — ___ + cài — naming cuisines
菜 (cài) means "dish / cuisine". Add it to a country: 中国 (China) → 中国菜 (Chinese food). Same works for 日本菜 (Japanese food).
中国菜很好吃。
zhōngguócài hěn hǎochī.
Chinese food is very tasty.
外国人 / 老外 — saying "foreigner"
外国 (wàiguó) = "foreign country", + 人 (person) = 外国人 (foreigner). 老外 (lǎowài) is the casual, everyday version — friendly, not rude.
他是外国人。
tā shì wàiguórén.
He is a foreigner.
…，但是… — …, dànshì … — "…, but …"
但是 (or the casual 可是, kěshì) joins two clauses to show contrast, like English "but". It goes at the start of the second clause.
我喜欢吃牛肉，但是不喜欢吃猪肉。
wǒ xǐhuān chī niúròu, dànshì bù xǐhuān chī zhūròu.
I like eating beef, but I don't like eating pork.
因为…，所以… — yīnwèi …, suǒyǐ … — "because …, so …"
Chinese pairs them up: 因为 (because) gives the reason, 所以 (so / therefore) gives the result. Using BOTH together is normal and correct — not redundant like it would be in English.
因为中国菜很好吃，所以我喜欢吃。
yīnwèi zhōngguócài hěn hǎochī, suǒyǐ wǒ xǐhuān chī.
Because Chinese food is tasty, I like eating it.
A 比 B + adjective — A bǐ B … — "A is more ___ than B"
To compare, put 比 (than) between the two things, then the adjective: A 比 B 好吃 = "A is tastier than B". Note: no 很 before the adjective here. ⚠️ Careful: you cannot say "更喜欢…比…". For preference, use 更喜欢这个 (prefer this one) or 我喜欢这个，不喜欢那个 (I like this one, not that one).
牛肉比猪肉好吃。
niúròu bǐ zhūròu hǎochī.
Beef is tastier than pork.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Count from 1 to 10 in Chinese:
一二三四五六七八九十 (yī èr sān sì wǔ liù qī bā jiǔ shí)
2. Ask "Do you like eating Chinese food?":
你喜欢吃中国菜吗？(nǐ xǐhuān chī zhōngguócài ma?)
3. Say "Baozi are very tasty":
包子很好吃。(bāozi hěn hǎochī)
4. Say the meats: beef, pork, chicken:
牛肉，猪肉，鸡肉 (niúròu, zhūròu, jīròu)
5. Say "He is a foreigner":
他是外国人。(tā shì wàiguórén)
6. Say "I like beef, but I don't like pork":
我喜欢吃牛肉，但是不喜欢吃猪肉。(wǒ xǐhuān chī niúròu, dànshì bù xǐhuān chī zhūròu)
7. Say "Because baozi are tasty, I like eating them" (因为…所以…):
因为包子很好吃，所以我喜欢吃。(yīnwèi bāozi hěn hǎochī, suǒyǐ wǒ xǐhuān chī)
8. Say "Beef is tastier than pork" (用 比):
牛肉比猪肉好吃。(niúròu bǐ zhūròu hǎochī)

What this lesson covers
Asking and telling age with 岁 (years old) and 多大了？(how old are you?), describing things with 的 (白色的猫 = the white cat), and 差不多 (about / roughly). It finishes with a reading passage that reviews almost everything you've learned so far!
Step 1 — New Words: Age words. 多大 literally means "how big" — that's how Chinese asks someone's age.

New Words — Age

岁 suì years old
多大 duō dà how old (lit. how big)
多大了？ duō dà le? how old are you?
差不多 chàbùduō about / roughly
Step 2 — Conversation Practice: Ask someone's age with 多大了？, answer with just a number + 岁 (no 是 needed!), and soften a guess with 差不多.
T
你多大了？
nǐ duō dà le?
How old are you?
P
我25岁。
wǒ èrshíwǔ suì.
I'm 25 years old.
T
你的妈妈多大了？
nǐ de māma duō dà le?
How old is your mother?
P
我妈妈差不多50岁。
wǒ māma chàbùduō wǔshí suì.
My mom is about 50.
T
你有白色的猫吗？
nǐ yǒu báisè de māo ma?
Do you have a white cat?
P
我没有猫，我有白色的狗。
wǒ méiyǒu māo, wǒ yǒu báisè de gǒu.
I don't have a cat, I have a white dog.

Grammar Spotlight

多大了？ — duō dà le? — asking age
多大 literally means "how big" — it's the standard way to ask an adult's age. 了 completes the question. Answer with a number + 岁.
你多大了？我三十岁。
nǐ duō dà le? wǒ sānshí suì.
How old are you? I'm 30.
Number + 岁 — stating age (NO 是!)
To say your age, use just number + 岁 — do not add 是. 我25岁 = "I am 25". Saying 我是25岁 is wrong.
我妈妈五十二岁。
wǒ māma wǔshí'èr suì.
My mom is 52.
adjective + 的 + noun — describing things
Put a description before a noun with 的: 白色的猫 = "white cat", 白色的杯子 = "white cup". It's the same 的 as in 我的 (my) — it links a describer to a noun.
白色的杯子，白色的猫。
báisè de bēizi, báisè de māo.
A white cup, a white cat.
差不多 — chàbùduō — "about / almost"
Use 差不多 before a number to mean "roughly / about" (差不多50岁 = about 50), or on its own to mean "more or less the same".
我爷爷差不多八十岁。
wǒ yéye chàbùduō bāshí suì.
My grandpa is about 80.
Step 3 — Reading Practice: Read this paragraph about Maggie & Tom out loud. It reviews nationality, jobs, age, 有, 都喜欢, 白色的…, and 更喜欢 from Lessons 1–6!

Words in the Passage — 南非 is new, the rest is review

南非 nánfēi South Africa ✨ NEW
南非人 nánfēi rén South African
医生 yīshēng doctor (L3)
男朋友 nán péngyou boyfriend (L5)
叫 jiào to be called (L1)
也 yě also (L1)
他们 tāmen they (L3)
都喜欢 dōu xǐhuān both like (L5)
咖啡 kāfēi coffee (L4)
红茶 hóngchá black tea (L5)
更喜欢 gèng xǐhuān to prefer (L5)
白色的 báisè de white (+ 的)
Reading: Maggie & Tom
Maggie是南非人，25岁，她是医生。
Maggie shì nánfēi rén, èrshíwǔ suì, tā shì yīshēng.
Maggie is South African, 25 years old, she is a doctor.

她有一个男朋友，叫Tom，28岁，也是医生。
tā yǒu yí ge nán péngyou, jiào Tom, èrshíbā suì, yě shì yīshēng.
She has a boyfriend, called Tom, 28 years old, also a doctor.

Maggie和Tom都喜欢喝咖啡和红茶，他们有白色的猫和白色的狗。
Maggie hé Tom dōu xǐhuān hē kāfēi hé hóngchá, tāmen yǒu báisè de māo hé báisè de gǒu.
Maggie and Tom both like drinking coffee and black tea; they have a white cat and a white dog.

他们的猫喜欢吃鸡肉，狗更喜欢吃牛肉。
tāmen de māo xǐhuān chī jīròu, gǒu gèng xǐhuān chī niúròu.
Their cat likes eating chicken; the dog prefers eating beef.

1. Ask "How old are you?":
你多大了？(nǐ duō dà le?)
2. Say "I'm 25 years old":
我25岁。(wǒ èrshíwǔ suì)
3. Ask "How old is your mother?":
你的妈妈多大了？(nǐ de māma duō dà le?)
4. Say "a white cat" and "a white cup":
白色的猫，白色的杯子 (báisè de māo, báisè de bēizi)
5. Say "My grandpa is about 80" (差不多):
我爷爷差不多80岁。(wǒ yéye chàbùduō bāshí suì)
6. From the passage: How old is Tom, and what's his job?
Tom 28岁，是医生。(Tom èrshíbā suì, shì yīshēng) — Tom is 28 and a doctor.
7. From the passage: What nationality is Maggie?
她是南非人。(tā shì nánfēi rén) — She is South African.

What this lesson covers
How much you like something! A full scale of degree words from 非常 (extremely) down to 一点儿也不 (not at all), the 好+verb family (好吃/好喝/好看/好听/好玩儿), and 觉得 (to think / feel) for giving opinions — plus some family & everyday words.
Step 1 — The Degree Scale: These words go before 喜欢 (or before 好喝 / 好吃) to say how strong your feeling is. Here they are from strongest 🥰 to weakest 😤 — try reading top to bottom!

Degree Words — 我 ___ 喜欢 (strongest → weakest)

非常 fēicháng 🥰 extremely / really
很 hěn 😊 very
比较 bǐjiào 🙂 quite / relatively
一般般 yìbānbān 😐 so-so
不太 bùtài 😒 not very
不 bù 🙁 not
一点儿也不 yìdiǎnr yě bù 😤 not at all
Step 2 — 好 + verb: 好 (good) + an action verb = "nice to ___". You already know 好吃 & 好喝 — here's the whole family, plus the plain verbs they come from.

好 + Verb — "nice to ___"

吃 → 好吃 chī → hǎochī eat → tasty
喝 → 好喝 hē → hǎohē drink → tasty
看 → 好看 kàn → hǎokàn look → good-looking
听 → 好听 tīng → hǎotīng listen → nice to hear
玩儿 → 好玩儿 wánr → hǎowánr play → fun
Step 3 — 觉得 (to think / feel): Use 觉得 to give an opinion. Ask 你觉得…好…吗？and answer 我觉得… Agree with 我也觉得 (I think so too).

New Words — Opinions, Family & Everyday

觉得 juéde to think / feel
我也觉得 wǒ yě juéde I think so too
认识 rènshi to know / meet (a person)
谢谢 xièxie thank you
不用谢 búyòngxiè you're welcome
儿子 érzi son
女儿 nǚ'ér daughter
姐姐 jiějie older sister
裙子 qúnzi skirt / dress
歌 gē song
学 xué to study / learn
今天 jīntiān today
⚠️ Rude word — 滚 (gǔn): Literally "roll", but as a command it means "get lost! / f*** off!". It came up in the lesson so you recognize it — never say it to someone unless you mean to be very aggressive.
Step 4 — Conversation Practice: Ask an opinion with 你觉得…好…吗？, answer with 我觉得 + a degree word, and agree with 我也觉得.
T
你觉得咖啡好喝吗？
nǐ juéde kāfēi hǎohē ma?
Do you think coffee is tasty?
P
我觉得咖啡很好喝。
wǒ juéde kāfēi hěn hǎohē.
I think coffee is very tasty.
T
白酒呢？你觉得好喝吗？
báijiǔ ne? nǐ juéde hǎohē ma?
And baijiu? Do you think it's tasty?
P
白酒一点儿也不好喝！
báijiǔ yìdiǎnr yě bù hǎohē!
Baijiu isn't tasty at all!
T
哈哈，我也觉得。这个歌好听吗？
hāhā, wǒ yě juéde. zhège gē hǎotīng ma?
Haha, I think so too. Is this song nice?
P
非常好听！
fēicháng hǎotīng!
Really nice!

Grammar Spotlight

degree word + 喜欢 / 好喝 — how strong your feeling is
Put a degree word right before 喜欢 or a 好+verb: 非常 (extremely) > 很 (very) > 比较 (quite) > 一般般 (so-so) > 不太 (not very) > 不 (not) > 一点儿也不 (not at all).
我非常喜欢吃西瓜，不太喜欢吃苹果。
wǒ fēicháng xǐhuān chī xīguā, bùtài xǐhuān chī píngguǒ.
I really like eating watermelon, and don't much like apples.
好 + verb — hǎo + verb — "nice to ___"
好 + an action = "pleasant to ___": 好吃 (tasty), 好喝 (tasty to drink), 好看 (good-looking), 好听 (nice to hear), 好玩儿 (fun).
这个歌很好听。
zhège gē hěn hǎotīng.
This song is very nice (to hear).
你觉得…好…吗？ — nǐ juéde … hǎo… ma? — asking opinions
觉得 = "to think / feel". Ask 你觉得 + thing + 好喝/好吃/好看 + 吗？ Answer with 我觉得… Agree with 我也觉得 (I think so too).
你觉得啤酒好喝吗？我觉得比较好喝。
nǐ juéde píjiǔ hǎohē ma? wǒ juéde bǐjiào hǎohē.
Do you think beer is tasty? I think it's fairly tasty.
一点儿也不 + … — yìdiǎnr yě bù — "not … at all"
The strongest "no": 一点儿也不 + verb/adjective = "not … in the slightest". Stronger than plain 不.
我觉得白酒一点儿也不好喝。
wǒ juéde báijiǔ yìdiǎnr yě bù hǎohē.
I think baijiu isn't tasty at all.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Say "I really (extremely) like eating watermelon":
我非常喜欢吃西瓜。(wǒ fēicháng xǐhuān chī xīguā)
2. Say "I don't really like eating apples":
我不太喜欢吃苹果。(wǒ bùtài xǐhuān chī píngguǒ)
3. Ask "Do you think beer is tasty?":
你觉得啤酒好喝吗？(nǐ juéde píjiǔ hǎohē ma?)
4. Say "I think baijiu isn't tasty at all":
我觉得白酒一点儿也不好喝。(wǒ juéde báijiǔ yìdiǎnr yě bù hǎohē)
5. Say the 好+verb words: tasty, good-looking, nice to hear, fun:
好吃 / 好看 / 好听 / 好玩儿 (hǎochī / hǎokàn / hǎotīng / hǎowánr)
6. Reply "Thanks!" and "You're welcome":
谢谢！— 不用谢。(xièxie! — búyòngxiè)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages and write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, then answer the questions and send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
Vegetables! You'll learn the two big umbrella words 水果 (fruit) and 蔬菜 (vegetables), name common vegetables, and a couple of citrus fruits — then reuse 喜欢吃, 觉得, and the degree words from Lesson 9 to say what you like.
Step 1 — The Two Categories: Every edible plant is either 水果 (fruit) or 蔬菜 (vegetables). Learn these two umbrella words first — you'll sort everything under them.

New Words — Categories

水果 shuǐguǒ fruit (lit. water-fruit)
蔬菜 shūcài vegetables
Step 2 — Vegetables (蔬菜): Common vegetables. Notice 瓜 (guā, melon/gourd) shows up in several — the same 瓜 as in 西瓜 (watermelon) from Lesson 2!

New Words — Vegetables

辣椒 làjiāo chili pepper
红辣椒 hóng làjiāo red pepper
黄辣椒 huáng làjiāo yellow pepper
南瓜 nánguā pumpkin (south-melon)
番茄 / 西红柿 fānqié / xīhóngshì tomato (two names)
土豆 tǔdòu potato (earth-bean)
洋葱 yángcōng onion
黄瓜 huángguā cucumber (yellow-melon)
Two handy notes: (1) Add a color before 辣椒 to name a pepper: 红辣椒 (red pepper), 黄辣椒 (yellow pepper). (2) Tomato has two names — 番茄 (fānqié) and 西红柿 (xīhóngshì, lit. "western red persimmon"). Both are correct and common!
Step 3 — Citrus (水果): Two sour fruits. 青 (qīng) means "green", so a "green lemon" is a lime!

New Words — Citrus

柠檬 níngméng lemon
青柠檬 qīng níngméng lime (green lemon)
Step 4 — Conversation Practice: Reuse everything so far — ask 喜欢吃…吗？, add a degree word (非常/一点儿也不), and check opinions with 觉得…是…还是…？
T
你喜欢吃蔬菜吗？
nǐ xǐhuān chī shūcài ma?
Do you like eating vegetables?
P
我很喜欢！我非常喜欢吃黄瓜和番茄。
wǒ hěn xǐhuān! wǒ fēicháng xǐhuān chī huángguā hé fānqié.
I like them a lot! I really like eating cucumber and tomato.
T
你觉得番茄是水果还是蔬菜？
nǐ juéde fānqié shì shuǐguǒ háishì shūcài?
Do you think a tomato is a fruit or a vegetable?
P
哈哈，我觉得是蔬菜。
hāhā, wǒ juéde shì shūcài.
Haha, I think it's a vegetable.
T
你喜欢吃辣椒吗？
nǐ xǐhuān chī làjiāo ma?
Do you like eating chili?
P
我一点儿也不喜欢辣椒！
wǒ yìdiǎnr yě bù xǐhuān làjiāo!
I don't like chili at all!

Grammar Spotlight

水果 vs 蔬菜 — sorting food with 是
Use 是 to say which category something is: 番茄是蔬菜 (a tomato is a vegetable), 柠檬是水果 (a lemon is a fruit). Ask which one with 是…还是…？
土豆是蔬菜，柠檬是水果。
tǔdòu shì shūcài, níngméng shì shuǐguǒ.
A potato is a vegetable; a lemon is a fruit.
瓜 = melon / gourd — a word-building block
Many round veg/fruit end in 瓜 (guā): 西瓜 (watermelon), 南瓜 (pumpkin), 黄瓜 (cucumber). Spotting 瓜 tells you it's a melon-type plant.
西瓜是水果，南瓜和黄瓜是蔬菜。
xīguā shì shuǐguǒ, nánguā hé huángguā shì shūcài.
Watermelon is a fruit; pumpkin and cucumber are vegetables.
degree word + 喜欢吃 + food (review)
Reuse Lesson 9's scale with these new foods: 非常喜欢吃 (really like eating) … 一点儿也不喜欢 (don't like at all).
我非常喜欢吃黄瓜，一点儿也不喜欢吃洋葱。
wǒ fēicháng xǐhuān chī huángguā, yìdiǎnr yě bù xǐhuān chī yángcōng.
I really like cucumber, and don't like onion at all.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Say "fruit" and "vegetables":

2. Say the vegetables: chili, potato, onion:
辣椒，土豆，洋葱 (làjiāo, tǔdòu, yángcōng)
3. Ask "Do you like eating vegetables?":

柠檬 / 青柠檬 (níngméng / qīng níngméng)
7. Say "red pepper" and "yellow pepper":
红辣椒 / 黄辣椒 (hóng làjiāo / huáng làjiāo)
8. Give both names for "tomato":
番茄 / 西红柿 (fānqié / xīhóngshì)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages and write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, then answer the questions and send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
Your favorites! One tiny word — 最 (zuì, "most") — turns 喜欢 into 最喜欢 (favorite) and 最不喜欢 (like the least). You'll ask "what's your favorite ___?" using category words, and learn a whole family of berries.
Step 1 — 最 = "the most": Put 最 (zuì) right before 喜欢 to make a superlative. 最喜欢 = like the most (favorite); 最不喜欢 = like the least.

New Words — 最 (most) & Categories

最 zuì most
最喜欢 zuì xǐhuān to like most / favorite
最不喜欢 zuì bù xǐhuān to like least
动物 dòngwù animal
Step 2 — Category Words: These are the "buckets" you ask about — most are review. Plug any of them into 你最喜欢什么___？

Categories — 你最喜欢什么___？

蔬菜 shūcài vegetables
水果 shuǐguǒ fruit
动物 dòngwù animal
肉 ròu meat
颜色 yánsè color
酒 jiǔ alcohol / drink
菜 cài dish / cuisine
茶 chá tea
Step 3 — The Berry Family (莓): Every berry ends in 莓 (méi). Learn 莓 once and the rest is just the color/plant in front!

New Words — Berries (莓)

草莓 cǎoméi strawberry (grass-berry)
蓝莓 lánméi blueberry (blue-berry)
红莓 hóngméi cranberry (red-berry)
黑莓 hēiméi blackberry (black-berry)
树莓 shùméi raspberry (tree-berry)
Step 4 — Conversation Practice: Ask favorites with 你最喜欢什么___？, answer with 我最喜欢… or the fuller 我最喜欢的___是…, and flip it with 最不喜欢.
T
你最喜欢什么水果？
nǐ zuì xǐhuān shénme shuǐguǒ?
What's your favorite fruit?
P
我最喜欢草莓。
wǒ zuì xǐhuān cǎoméi.
I like strawberries the most.
T
你最喜欢什么蔬菜？
nǐ zuì xǐhuān shénme shūcài?
What's your favorite vegetable?
P
我最喜欢的蔬菜是番茄。
wǒ zuì xǐhuān de shūcài shì fānqié.
My favorite vegetable is tomato.
T
你的妈妈最不喜欢什么水果？
nǐ de māma zuì bù xǐhuān shénme shuǐguǒ?
What fruit does your mom dislike the most?
P
我妈妈最不喜欢草莓。
wǒ māma zuì bù xǐhuān cǎoméi.
My mom dislikes strawberries the most.

Grammar Spotlight

最 + 喜欢 — zuì + xǐhuān — "like the most"
最 (zuì) = "most". Put it before 喜欢 for "favorite", or before 不喜欢 for "like the least". It works with any verb/adjective: 最好 (best), 最大 (biggest).
我最喜欢红色，最不喜欢黄色。
wǒ zuì xǐhuān hóngsè, zuì bù xǐhuān huángsè.
I like red the most, and yellow the least.
你最喜欢什么 + category？ — asking favorites
Plug a category after 什么: 你最喜欢什么水果/蔬菜/动物/颜色？ Answer simply: 我最喜欢 + the item.
你最喜欢什么动物？我最喜欢猫。
nǐ zuì xǐhuān shénme dòngwù? wǒ zuì xǐhuān māo.
What's your favorite animal? I like cats the most.
我最喜欢的 + category + 是 + item — the full form
For a complete sentence: 我最喜欢的 [category] 是 [item] = "My favorite [category] is [item]". The 的…是 frame is optional but sounds polished.
我最喜欢的水果是草莓。
wǒ zuì xǐhuān de shuǐguǒ shì cǎoméi.
My favorite fruit is strawberry.
莓 = berry — a word-building block
Every berry ends in 莓 (méi): 草莓 (strawberry), 蓝莓 (blueberry), 红莓 (cranberry), 黑莓 (blackberry), 树莓 (raspberry).
草莓和蓝莓都是水果。
cǎoméi hé lánméi dōu shì shuǐguǒ.
Strawberries and blueberries are both fruit.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Say "favorite" (like the most) and "like the least":
最喜欢 / 最不喜欢 (zuì xǐhuān / zuì bù xǐhuān)
2. Ask "What's your favorite fruit?":
你最喜欢什么水果？(nǐ zuì xǐhuān shénme shuǐguǒ?)
3. Say "My favorite vegetable is tomato":
我最喜欢的蔬菜是番茄。(wǒ zuì xǐhuān de shūcài shì fānqié)
4. Ask "What fruit does your mom dislike the most?":
你的妈妈最不喜欢什么水果？(nǐ de māma zuì bù xǐhuān shénme shuǐguǒ?)
5. Say the berries: strawberry, blueberry, blackberry:
草莓，蓝莓，黑莓 (cǎoméi, lánméi, hēiméi)
6. Ask "What's your favorite animal?" and answer "I like cats the most":
你最喜欢什么动物？我最喜欢猫。(nǐ zuì xǐhuān shénme dòngwù? wǒ zuì xǐhuān māo)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages and write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, then answer the questions and send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
Small talk! Ask "how have you been?" with 怎么样, everyday tech (computer, phone, earphones), handy adjectives (busy, tired, pretty, fast/slow, long/short), and more nouns. It ends with a reading passage about 露露 (Lulu).
Step 1 — "How are you?": 怎么样 (zěnmeyàng) = "how / how about?". Ask about a time (最近 lately / 今天 today) or a thing. Answer with a status word.

New Words — Small Talk & Status

怎么样 zěnmeyàng how / how about?
最近 zuìjìn recently / lately
明天 míngtiān tomorrow
不错 búcuò not bad / pretty good
还可以 hái kěyǐ it's ok / passable
忙 máng busy
累 lèi tired
Step 2 — Tech & "a lot": Everyday gadgets, plus 很多 (a lot of) — put it straight before a noun.

New Words — Tech

电脑 diànnǎo computer (electric-brain)
手机 shǒujī phone (hand-machine)
耳机 ěrjī earphones (ear-machine)
很多 hěn duō a lot / many
Step 3 — Describing Words: Useful adjectives — several come in opposite pairs. In a plain statement, put 很 before them (很快 = "fast").

New Words — Adjectives (opposites)

漂亮 piàoliang pretty / beautiful
快 kuài fast ⟷
慢 màn slow ⟷
长 cháng long ⟷
短 duǎn short ⟷
Step 4 — More Nouns & Places: A grab-bag of useful words, including 家 (home) vs 家人 (family members).

New Words — Nouns & Places

家 jiā home / family
家人 jiārén family members
面 miàn noodles
熊猫 xióngmāo panda
龙 lóng dragon
医院 yīyuàn hospital
成都 Chéngdū Chengdu (city)
Extra — Animals & Metallic Colors: Two more animals, and two shiny colors (color + 色 as usual).

Extra Words

乌龟 wūguī turtle
大象 dàxiàng elephant
银色 yínsè silver
金色 jīnsè gold
Step 5 — Conversation Practice: Open with 你最近怎么样？, answer with a status word, and reuse 觉得…怎么样？to ask opinions.
T
你最近怎么样？
nǐ zuìjìn zěnmeyàng?
How have you been lately?
P
我最近很忙，也很累。
wǒ zuìjìn hěn máng, yě hěn lèi.
I've been very busy lately, and tired too.
T
你今天怎么样？
nǐ jīntiān zěnmeyàng?
How are you today?
P
今天还不错！我有很多耳机和一个电脑。
jīntiān hái búcuò! wǒ yǒu hěn duō ěrjī hé yí ge diànnǎo.
Today's not bad! I have lots of earphones and a computer.
T
你觉得成都怎么样？
nǐ juéde Chéngdū zěnmeyàng?
What do you think of Chengdu?
P
成都非常漂亮！熊猫也很漂亮。
Chéngdū fēicháng piàoliang! xióngmāo yě hěn piàoliang.
Chengdu is very pretty! The pandas are lovely too.

Grammar Spotlight

…怎么样？ — … zěnmeyàng? — "how is …?"
怎么样 asks "how is it?". Put a time or thing in front: 你最近怎么样？(How've you been lately?), 你今天怎么样？(How are you today?), 成都怎么样？(How's Chengdu?). Pair it with 觉得 for opinions: 你觉得…怎么样？
你最近怎么样？我很好。
nǐ zuìjìn zěnmeyàng? wǒ hěn hǎo.
How've you been lately? I'm good.
Status answers — from great to worn out
Reply with: 很好 (great) › 还不错 (pretty good) › 还可以 (just ok) › 很忙 / 很累 (busy / tired). 还 (hái) means "still / fairly" and softens the reply.
我还可以，就是很忙。
wǒ hái kěyǐ, jiùshì hěn máng.
I'm ok, just very busy.
很多 + noun — "a lot of ___"
Put 很多 (a lot) directly before a noun: 很多耳机 (lots of earphones), 很多裙子 (many skirts). No measure word needed.
她有很多绿色的裙子。
tā yǒu hěn duō lǜsè de qúnzi.
She has many green skirts.
很 + adjective — describing (快/慢, 长/短, 漂亮)
In a plain statement a Chinese adjective usually takes 很 in front: 很快 (fast), 很慢 (slow), 很长 (long), 很短 (short), 很漂亮 (pretty). Here 很 is just a link, not always "very".
这个面很好吃，猫很快，乌龟很慢。
zhège miàn hěn hǎochī, māo hěn kuài, wūguī hěn màn.
This noodle dish is tasty; the cat is fast, the turtle is slow.
Step 6 — Reading Practice: Read this paragraph about 露露 (Lulu) out loud. It reuses 最喜欢, 觉得, 好看, 白色的… style descriptions, 很多, 最近, and 比较累 — all from recent lessons!
Reading: 露露 (Lulu)
露露最喜欢的颜色是绿色，她觉得绿色非常好看。
Lùlù zuì xǐhuān de yánsè shì lǜsè, tā juéde lǜsè fēicháng hǎokàn.
Lulu's favorite color is green; she thinks green is very good-looking.

她有绿色的电脑，绿色的耳机，绿色的手机和很多绿色的裙子。
tā yǒu lǜsè de diànnǎo, lǜsè de ěrjī, lǜsè de shǒujī hé hěn duō lǜsè de qúnzi.
She has a green computer, green earphones, a green phone, and many green skirts.

露露最近比较累。她很喜欢喝咖啡和绿茶。
Lùlù zuìjìn bǐjiào lèi. tā hěn xǐhuān hē kāfēi hé lǜchá.
Lulu has been rather tired lately. She really likes drinking coffee and green tea.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "How have you been lately?":
你最近怎么样？(nǐ zuìjìn zěnmeyàng?)
2. Say "I've been very busy and tired lately":
我最近很忙，也很累。(wǒ zuìjìn hěn máng, yě hěn lèi)
3. Say the tech words: computer, phone, earphones:
电脑，手机，耳机 (diànnǎo, shǒujī, ěrjī)
4. Say "not bad" and "it's ok":
不错 / 还可以 (búcuò / hái kěyǐ)
5. Say the opposites: fast/slow, long/short:
快 / 慢，长 / 短 (kuài / màn, cháng / duǎn)
6. From the passage: What is Lulu's favorite color?

Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages and write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, then answer the questions and send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences. (3) Read the 露露 passage aloud and send a voice message.
What this lesson covers
How often you do things! A frequency scale from 每天都 (every day) down to 从不 (never), plus a big set of everyday activities — reading, movies, running, swimming, cooking, music, drawing, calling and chatting.
Step 1 — The Frequency Scale: These go before the verb to say how often. Here they are from most (100%) to never (0%) — the same idea as the degree scale in Lesson 9!

Frequency Words — 我 ___ 喝咖啡 (most → never)

每天都 měitiān dōu every day (100%)
经常 jīngcháng often
有时候 yǒu shíhou sometimes
很少 hěn shǎo rarely / seldom
几乎从不 jīhū cóng bù almost never
从不 cóng bù never (0%)
几乎 jīhū almost
Step 2 — Activities: Things you do. Notice many are verb + object pairs (看书 = "look-book", 做饭 = "make-food").

New Words — Activities

看书 kàn shū read (a book)
看电影 kàn diànyǐng watch a movie
跑步 pǎobù run / jog
爬山 páshān hike / climb mountains
游泳 yóuyǒng swim
做饭 zuò fàn cook
听音乐 tīng yīnyuè listen to music
画画 huàhuà draw / paint
学汉语 xué hànyǔ study Chinese
Step 3 — Activities with a Person: Two patterns take a person in the middle — just drop a name into the blank.

New Words — …with Someone

给…打电话 gěi… dǎ diànhuà call … (on the phone)
和…聊天 hé… liáotiān chat with …
以前 yǐqián before / in the past
Step 4 — Conversation Practice: Ask 你经常…吗？or 你经常做什么？, then answer with a frequency word before the verb.
T
你经常喝咖啡吗？
nǐ jīngcháng hē kāfēi ma?
Do you often drink coffee?
P
我几乎每天都喝咖啡！
wǒ jīhū měitiān dōu hē kāfēi!
I drink coffee almost every day!
T
你经常做什么？
nǐ jīngcháng zuò shénme?
What do you often do?
P
我经常跑步，有时候游泳。
wǒ jīngcháng pǎobù, yǒu shíhou yóuyǒng.
I often run, and sometimes swim.
T
你从不做什么？
nǐ cóng bù zuò shénme?
What do you never do?
P
我从不喝白酒，也很少画画。
wǒ cóng bù hē báijiǔ, yě hěn shǎo huàhuà.
I never drink baijiu, and rarely draw.
T
你每天都学汉语吗？
nǐ měitiān dōu xué hànyǔ ma?
Do you study Chinese every day?
P
我每天都学汉语，也经常和朋友聊天。
wǒ měitiān dōu xué hànyǔ, yě jīngcháng hé péngyou liáotiān.
I study Chinese every day, and often chat with friends.

Grammar Spotlight

frequency word + verb — how often
The frequency word goes before the verb: 我经常跑步 (I often run), 我从不喝白酒 (I never drink baijiu). Scale: 每天都 (every day) › 经常 (often) › 有时候 (sometimes) › 很少 (rarely) › 从不 / 几乎从不 (never).
我有时候看电影，很少爬山。
wǒ yǒu shíhou kàn diànyǐng, hěn shǎo páshān.
I sometimes watch movies, and rarely hike.
每天都 — měitiān dōu — "every single day"
每 (every) loves to pair with 都 (all): 每天都 = "every day without exception". The 都 stays even in questions: 你每天都学汉语吗？
我几乎每天都喝咖啡。
wǒ jīhū měitiān dōu hē kāfēi.
I drink coffee almost every day.
verb + object activities — 看书 / 做饭 / 听音乐
Many activities are already verb + object: 看书 (read-book), 看电影 (watch-movie), 做饭 (make-rice/food), 听音乐 (listen-music), 画画 (draw-drawing). Just add a frequency word in front.
我经常听音乐，有时候做饭。
wǒ jīngcháng tīng yīnyuè, yǒu shíhou zuò fàn.
I often listen to music, and sometimes cook.
给…打电话 / 和…聊天 — put a person in the blank
These wrap around a person: 给 [somebody] 打电话 = "call [somebody]"; 和 [somebody] 聊天 = "chat with [somebody]". 给妈妈打电话, 和朋友聊天.
我每天都给妈妈打电话。
wǒ měitiān dōu gěi māma dǎ diànhuà.
I call my mom every day.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Say the frequency scale: every day, often, sometimes, rarely, never:
每天都，经常，有时候，很少，从不 (měitiān dōu, jīngcháng, yǒu shíhou, hěn shǎo, cóng bù)
2. Say "I drink coffee almost every day":
我几乎每天都喝咖啡。(wǒ jīhū měitiān dōu hē kāfēi)
3. Say "I never drink baijiu":
我从不喝白酒。(wǒ cóng bù hē báijiǔ)
4. Ask "Do you often eat apples?":
你经常吃苹果吗？(nǐ jīngcháng chī píngguǒ ma?)
5. Say these activities: read, watch a movie, swim, cook:

给妈妈打电话，和朋友聊天 (gěi māma dǎ diànhuà, hé péngyou liáotiān)

What this lesson covers
Languages! Say "I speak ___" with 说 + a language, learn a dozen world languages, drill Chinese tone-pairs with real words, and read a fun paragraph your teacher wrote about you (Pawel).
Step 1 — 说 + Language: 说 (shuō) = "to speak". Almost every language is a root + 语 (yǔ). Say 我说 + a language.

New Words — Languages (说 … 语)

说 shuō to speak
汉语 hànyǔ Chinese
英语 yīngyǔ English
法语 fǎyǔ French
德语 déyǔ German
俄语 éyǔ Russian
日语 rìyǔ Japanese
韩语 hányǔ Korean
泰语 tàiyǔ Thai
印度语 yìndùyǔ Hindi
西班牙语 xībānyáyǔ Spanish
广东话 guǎngdōnghuà Cantonese (uses 话!)
香港 xiānggǎng Hong Kong
Step 2 — Tone-Pair Practice: Every word below ends in first tone. They're grouped by the first syllable's tone — a great way to train your ear. Say each group out loud! (✨ = new word)

1 + 1  (first + first)

咖啡 kāfēi coffee
西瓜 xīguā watermelon
飞机 fēijī airplane ✨
冰箱 bīngxiāng fridge ✨

2 + 1  (second + first)

成都 chéngdū Chengdu
钱包 qiánbāo wallet ✨
熊猫 xióngmāo panda
黄瓜 huángguā cucumber

3 + 1  (third + first)

老师 lǎoshī teacher
手机 shǒujī phone
火锅 huǒguō hotpot ✨
耳机 ěrjī earphones

4 + 1  (fourth + first)

蛋糕 dàngāo cake ✨
帅哥 shuàigē handsome guy
唱歌 chànggē to sing ✨
面包 miànbāo bread ✨

Bonus Word — Chocolate 🍫

巧克力 qiǎokèlì chocolate ✨
巧克力蛋糕 qiǎokèlì dàngāo chocolate cake
⚠️ Careful — friend vs. partner: 女朋友 (nǚ péngyou) = girlfriend (romantic), but 女生朋友 (nǚshēng péngyou) = a female friend (just a friend). Same for 男朋友 (boyfriend) vs 男生朋友 (male friend). The little 生 makes it platonic!
Step 3 — Conversation Practice: Ask 你说…吗？or 你说什么语？, and answer with 我说 + language(s).
T
你说汉语吗？
nǐ shuō hànyǔ ma?
Do you speak Chinese?
P
我说汉语，也说英语和法语。
wǒ shuō hànyǔ, yě shuō yīngyǔ hé fǎyǔ.
I speak Chinese, and also English and French.
T
你姐姐说什么语？
nǐ jiějie shuō shénme yǔ?
What language does your sister speak?
P
她说德语和西班牙语。
tā shuō déyǔ hé xībānyáyǔ.
She speaks German and Spanish.
T
香港人说广东话吗？
xiānggǎng rén shuō guǎngdōnghuà ma?
Do Hong Kong people speak Cantonese?
P
是的，他们说广东话，也说汉语。
shì de, tāmen shuō guǎngdōnghuà, yě shuō hànyǔ.
Yes, they speak Cantonese, and also Mandarin.

Grammar Spotlight

说 + language — shuō + … — "to speak ___"
Use 说 for speaking a language: 我说汉语 (I speak Chinese), 你说英语吗？(Do you speak English?). Ask which one with 你说什么语？
我说汉语和英语。
wǒ shuō hànyǔ hé yīngyǔ.
I speak Chinese and English.
root + 语 — naming languages
Most languages = a country/root + 语 (yǔ): 英语, 法语, 德语, 日语, 韩语. 汉语 (Chinese, 汉 = Han). Exception: 广东话 (Cantonese) takes 话 (huà, "speech"), because it's a spoken dialect.
法语和德语都很好听。
fǎyǔ hé déyǔ dōu hěn hǎotīng.
French and German both sound nice.
Tone pairs — training your ear
Chinese words often combine two tones. Drilling common patterns — 1+1 (咖啡), 2+1 (熊猫), 3+1 (老师), 4+1 (蛋糕) — makes your pronunciation smoother. Say each group out loud.
咖啡，熊猫，老师，蛋糕。
kāfēi, xióngmāo, lǎoshī, dàngāo.
coffee, panda, teacher, cake.
女生朋友 vs 女朋友 — friend vs. partner
Adding 生 makes it platonic: 女朋友 (girlfriend, romantic) → 女生朋友 (a female friend). 男朋友 (boyfriend) → 男生朋友 (a male friend). Don't mix them up!
她是我的女生朋友，不是我的女朋友。
tā shì wǒ de nǚshēng péngyou, bú shì wǒ de nǚ péngyou.
She's my (female) friend, not my girlfriend.
巧克力蛋糕 — qiǎokèlì dàngāo — "chocolate cake"
巧克力 (chocolate) + 蛋糕 (cake) = chocolate cake. Say how tasty it is with 非常好吃 (really delicious) — reusing 非常 (Lesson 9) and 好吃 (Lesson 6).
巧克力蛋糕非常好吃。
qiǎokèlì dàngāo fēicháng hǎochī.
Chocolate cake is really delicious.
Step 4 — Reading Practice: Your teacher wrote this about you! Read it out loud — it reviews 几乎从不, 最不喜欢, 有时候, 经常, 每天都, 很少, and 但是 from recent lessons.
Reading: Pawel in My Eyes (你…)
你几乎从不喝酒。你最不喜欢的水果是椰子。
nǐ jīhū cóng bù hē jiǔ. nǐ zuì bù xǐhuān de shuǐguǒ shì yēzi.
You almost never drink alcohol. Your least favorite fruit is coconut.

你有时候非常忙。你经常做饭。你每天都听音乐。
nǐ yǒu shíhou fēicháng máng. nǐ jīngcháng zuò fàn. nǐ měitiān dōu tīng yīnyuè.
You're sometimes very busy. You often cook. You listen to music every day.

你没有女朋友。你有时候看书，有时候看电影。
nǐ méiyǒu nǚ péngyou. nǐ yǒu shíhou kàn shū, yǒu shíhou kàn diànyǐng.
You don't have a girlfriend. You sometimes read, sometimes watch movies.

你很少和朋友聊天，但是你有时候和你姐姐聊天。
nǐ hěn shǎo hé péngyou liáotiān, dànshì nǐ yǒu shíhou hé nǐ jiějie liáotiān.
You rarely chat with friends, but sometimes you chat with your older sister.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "Do you speak Chinese?":
你说汉语吗？(nǐ shuō hànyǔ ma?)
2. Say these languages: English, French, Japanese:
英语，法语，日语 (yīngyǔ, fǎyǔ, rìyǔ)
3. Say "I speak Chinese and English":
我说汉语和英语。(wǒ shuō hànyǔ hé yīngyǔ)
4. Say the tone-pair words: coffee, panda, teacher, cake:
咖啡 (1+1)，熊猫 (2+1)，老师 (3+1)，蛋糕 (4+1)

飞机，冰箱，钱包 (fēijī, bīngxiāng, qiánbāo)
