What this lesson covers
Three big new verbs of intention: what you can do (会), what you want to do (想), and what you're interested in (对…感兴趣) — plus a set of fun activities like dancing, basketball, soccer and driving.
Step 1 — 会 = can / know how to: 会 (huì) goes before a verb for a learned skill. 你会说汉语吗？→ 我会说… / 我不会说… Say you're great at it with 非常会.

New Words — Ability

会 huì can / know how to
不会 bú huì cannot
一点儿 yìdiǎnr a little
非常会 fēicháng huì very good at
Step 2 — Activities: Fun things to do. Note the different verbs: 打 (hit) for basketball, 踢 (kick) for soccer, 开 (operate) for driving!

New Words — Activities

唱歌 chànggē to sing
跳舞 tiàowǔ to dance
做面包 zuò miànbāo to make bread
打篮球 dǎ lánqiú play basketball
踢足球 tī zúqiú play soccer
开车 kāichē to drive
Step 3 — 想 (want) & 对…感兴趣 (interested in): 想 + verb = "want to ___". 对 [thing] 感兴趣 = "interested in [thing]". Add 很 for "really".

New Words — Want & Interest

想 xiǎng to want to / would like
感兴趣 gǎn xìngqù interested
对…感兴趣 duì… gǎn xìngqù interested in …
Step 4 — Conversation Practice: Ask 你会…吗？(can you), 你想…吗？(want to), and 你对…感兴趣吗？(interested in). Answer with 会/不会, 想/不想, 感兴趣/不感兴趣.
T
你会说汉语吗？
nǐ huì shuō hànyǔ ma?
Can you speak Chinese?
P
我会说一点儿汉语，我不会说日语。
wǒ huì shuō yìdiǎnr hànyǔ, wǒ bú huì shuō rìyǔ.
I can speak a little Chinese, I can't speak Japanese.
T
你会做饭吗？
nǐ huì zuò fàn ma?
Can you cook?
P
我不会做饭，但是我妈妈非常会做饭。
wǒ bú huì zuò fàn, dànshì wǒ māma fēicháng huì zuò fàn.
I can't cook, but my mom is really good at cooking.
T
你想学开车吗？
nǐ xiǎng xué kāichē ma?
Do you want to learn to drive?
P
我很想学开车！但是我对做面包不感兴趣。
wǒ hěn xiǎng xué kāichē! dànshì wǒ duì zuò miànbāo bù gǎn xìngqù.
I really want to learn to drive! But I'm not interested in making bread.

Grammar Spotlight

会 + verb — huì + verb — "can / know how to"
会 is for a learned skill (a language, cooking, driving). 我会说汉语 (I can speak Chinese), 我不会做饭 (I can't cook). Negate with 不会; say you're great with 非常会.
我妈妈非常会做饭，我不会。
wǒ māma fēicháng huì zuò fàn, wǒ bú huì.
My mom is great at cooking; I can't.
…一点儿 — a little (softening)
Add 一点儿 (yìdiǎnr, "a little") after the verb to downplay: 我会说一点儿汉语 = "I can speak a little Chinese".
我会说一点儿汉语。
wǒ huì shuō yìdiǎnr hànyǔ.
I can speak a little Chinese.
想 + verb — xiǎng + verb — "want to ___"
想 before a verb = "want to do it": 我想学开车 (I want to learn to drive). Negate with 不想; strengthen with 很想 (really want to).
我很想学开车，不想学做面包。
wǒ hěn xiǎng xué kāichē, bù xiǎng xué zuò miànbāo.
I really want to learn to drive, not to bake bread.
对 … 感兴趣 — duì … gǎn xìngqù — "interested in …"
Wrap 对 … 感兴趣 around a thing: 我对做面包感兴趣 (I'm interested in baking). Negate by putting 不 before 感兴趣: 对…不感兴趣.
你对做面包感兴趣吗？我不感兴趣。
nǐ duì zuò miànbāo gǎn xìngqù ma? wǒ bù gǎn xìngqù.
Are you interested in baking? I'm not interested.
Sport & action verbs — 打 / 踢 / 开
Different actions use different verbs: 打篮球 (hit-basketball), 踢足球 (kick-soccer), 开车 (operate-car), 跳舞 (jump-dance), 唱歌 (sing-song).
我会打篮球，也会踢足球。
wǒ huì dǎ lánqiú, yě huì tī zúqiú.
I can play basketball, and soccer too.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "Can you speak Chinese?":
你会说汉语吗？(nǐ huì shuō hànyǔ ma?)
2. Say "I can speak a little Chinese":
我会说一点儿汉语。(wǒ huì shuō yìdiǎnr hànyǔ)
3. Say "I can't cook, but my mom is really good at it":
我不会做饭，但是我妈妈非常会做饭。(wǒ bú huì zuò fàn, dànshì wǒ māma fēicháng huì zuò fàn)
4. Say the activities: dance, play basketball, play soccer, drive:
跳舞，打篮球，踢足球，开车 (tiàowǔ, dǎ lánqiú, tī zúqiú, kāichē)
5. Say "I want to learn to drive":
我想学开车。(wǒ xiǎng xué kāichē)
6. Ask "Are you interested in making bread?":
你对做面包感兴趣吗？(nǐ duì zuò miànbāo gǎn xìngqù ma?)

What this lesson covers
Asking why (为什么) and answering with 因为…所以… (because…so…), saying you do things together on a certain day (周末和家人一起…), more tone-pair practice, and a reading passage about Jessica & Lulu.
Step 1 — Why / Because / So: Ask a reason with 为什么？(why), answer with 因为 (because) — often followed by 所以 (so). You met 因为…所以… back in Lesson 6; 为什么 is the new question word.

New Words — Reasons

为什么 wèishénme why
因为 yīnwèi because
所以 suǒyǐ so / therefore
Step 2 — Days & "Together": 周 + a number = weekday (周五 = Friday, 周六 = Saturday), and 周末 = weekend. Do something with someone using 和 [person] 一起 [verb].

New Words — Days & Together

周末 zhōumò weekend
周五 zhōuwǔ Friday
周六 zhōuliù Saturday
一起 yìqǐ together
Step 3 — Tone-Pair Practice (X + 2nd tone): More ear-training! Every word below ends in second tone, grouped by the first syllable's tone. Say each group out loud. (✨ = new word)

1 + 2  (first + second)

公园 gōngyuán park ✨
中国 zhōngguó China
空调 kōngtiáo air conditioning ✨
番茄 fānqié tomato

2 + 2  (second + second)

柠檬 níngméng lemon
足球 zúqiú soccer
红茶 hóngchá black tea
银行 yínháng bank ✨

3 + 2  (third + second)

美国 měiguó USA ✨
口红 kǒuhóng lipstick
草莓 cǎoméi strawberry
网球 wǎngqiú tennis ✨

4 + 2  (fourth + second)

绿茶 lǜchá green tea
大学 dàxué university ✨
地图 dìtú map ✨
面条 miàntiáo noodles ✨
Casual expression — 疯了 (fēng le) = "crazy": 你疯了吗？= "Are you crazy?!"; 我疯了 = "I'm crazy"; 她疯了 = "She's crazy". Negate with 没: 我没疯 (I'm not crazy). Playful among friends — not polite to strangers.
Step 4 — Conversation Practice: Combine it all — ask 为什么？, answer 因为…所以…, and describe doing things 和…一起 on a certain day.
T
你周末做什么？
nǐ zhōumò zuò shénme?
What do you do on weekends?
P
我周末经常和家人一起看电影。
wǒ zhōumò jīngcháng hé jiārén yìqǐ kàn diànyǐng.
On weekends I often watch movies with my family.
T
你为什么喜欢看电影？
nǐ wèishénme xǐhuān kàn diànyǐng?
Why do you like watching movies?
P
因为电影很好看，所以我很喜欢。
yīnwèi diànyǐng hěn hǎokàn, suǒyǐ wǒ hěn xǐhuān.
Because movies are great, so I like them a lot.
T
你周六做什么？
nǐ zhōuliù zuò shénme?
What do you do on Saturdays?
P
我周六和朋友一起打网球。
wǒ zhōuliù hé péngyou yìqǐ dǎ wǎngqiú.
On Saturdays I play tennis with friends.
T
你每天都打网球？你疯了吗？
nǐ měitiān dōu dǎ wǎngqiú? nǐ fēng le ma?
You play tennis every day? Are you crazy?
P
哈哈，我没疯！我很喜欢网球。
hāhā, wǒ méi fēng! wǒ hěn xǐhuān wǎngqiú.
Haha, I'm not crazy! I really like tennis.

Grammar Spotlight

为什么？→ 因为…（所以…） — asking & giving reasons
为什么 (why) asks the reason; answer with 因为 (because), often paired with 所以 (so). Using both 因为…所以… together is normal in Chinese.
你为什么喜欢她？因为她很漂亮。
nǐ wèishénme xǐhuān tā? yīnwèi tā hěn piàoliang.
Why do you like her? Because she's pretty.
[time] 和 [person] 一起 [verb] — doing things together
Word order: time first, then 和 [person] 一起, then the verb. 我周末和家人一起看电影 = "On weekends I watch movies together with my family". 一起 (together) sits right before the verb.
我周六和朋友一起打网球。
wǒ zhōuliù hé péngyou yìqǐ dǎ wǎngqiú.
On Saturday I play tennis with friends.
周 + number — days of the week
周 (week) + a number gives the weekday: 周一 (Mon), 周二 (Tue) … 周五 (Fri), 周六 (Sat). 周末 (week-end) = the weekend.
周五，周六，周末。
zhōuwǔ, zhōuliù, zhōumò.
Friday, Saturday, the weekend.
Tone pairs — X + 2nd tone
Keep training your ear with words that end in second tone: 1+2 (公园), 2+2 (足球), 3+2 (美国), 4+2 (大学). Say each pattern group aloud.
公园，足球，美国，大学。
gōngyuán, zúqiú, měiguó, dàxué.
park, soccer, USA, university.
Step 5 — Reading Practice: Read this out loud — it brings back 露露 (Lulu) and reviews 几乎每天都, 所以, 会说, 都很会, 因为…所以…, 一起, and 周五/周末!
Reading: Jessica & Lulu
我的哥哥有一个女朋友，她叫Jessica，是英国人。
wǒ de gēge yǒu yí ge nǚ péngyou, tā jiào Jessica, shì yīngguó rén.
My older brother has a girlfriend called Jessica; she's British.

Jessica几乎每天都学汉语，所以她的汉语非常好。
Jessica jīhū měitiān dōu xué hànyǔ, suǒyǐ tā de hànyǔ fēicháng hǎo.
Jessica studies Chinese almost every day, so her Chinese is very good.

Jessica的汉语老师是中国人，叫露露，露露会说一点儿英语。
Jessica de hànyǔ lǎoshī shì zhōngguó rén, jiào Lùlù, Lùlù huì shuō yìdiǎnr yīngyǔ.
Jessica's Chinese teacher is Chinese, called Lulu; Lulu can speak a little English.

露露和Jessica是好朋友，她们都很会做饭和唱歌。
Lùlù hé Jessica shì hǎo péngyou, tāmen dōu hěn huì zuò fàn hé chànggē.
Lulu and Jessica are good friends; they're both great at cooking and singing.

因为露露对电影很感兴趣，所以她经常周五和Jessica一起看电影。周末她们经常一起爬山。
yīnwèi Lùlù duì diànyǐng hěn gǎn xìngqù, suǒyǐ tā jīngcháng zhōuwǔ hé Jessica yìqǐ kàn diànyǐng. zhōumò tāmen jīngcháng yìqǐ páshān.
Because Lulu is very interested in movies, she often watches movies with Jessica on Fridays. On weekends they often hike together.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "Why?" and start an answer with "Because…":
为什么？— 因为… (wèishénme? — yīnwèi…)
2. Say "On weekends I watch movies with my family":
我周末和家人一起看电影。(wǒ zhōumò hé jiārén yìqǐ kàn diànyǐng)
3. Say the days: weekend, Friday, Saturday:
周末，周五，周六 (zhōumò, zhōuwǔ, zhōuliù)
4. Say the tone-pair words: park, soccer, USA, university:
公园 (1+2)，足球 (2+2)，美国 (3+2)，大学 (4+2)

因为露露对电影很感兴趣。(yīnwèi Lùlù duì diànyǐng hěn gǎn xìngqù) — Because Lulu is very interested in movies.
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages and write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, then answer the questions and send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences. (3) Read the Jessica & Lulu passage aloud and send a voice message.
What this lesson covers
Asking the date (几月几号) and your birthday (生日), all seven days of the week with 周几, ordinals with 第一个, a whole family of shops ending in 店, and tone-pair practice ending in third tone.
Step 1 — All the days of the week: You already know 周五, 周六 and 周末 from Lesson 16. Now complete the set: 周 + number for Monday–Saturday, and 周天 for Sunday. Ask "which day is it?" with 今天周几？

New Words — Days of the Week

周一 zhōuyī Monday
周二 zhōu'èr Tuesday
周三 zhōusān Wednesday
周四 zhōusì Thursday
周天 zhōutiān Sunday
周几 zhōujǐ which day of the week?
Step 2 — Dates & birthdays: A date is just number + 月 (month) + number + 号 (day): 3月9号 = March 9th. Ask with 几 (how many/which): 几月几号？ Your birthday = 生日.

New Words — Dates & Birthdays

几月几号 jǐ yuè jǐ hào what date?
号 hào day of the month
生日 shēngrì birthday
第一个 dì yī ge the first (one)
Step 3 — The 店 (diàn) shop family: [thing] + 店 = the shop that sells it. One word-block unlocks eight places! ⚠️ Watch out: 酒店 looks like "alcohol shop" but actually means hotel.

New Words — Shops (X + 店)

咖啡店 kāfēidiàn café / coffee shop
水果店 shuǐguǒdiàn fruit shop
花店 huādiàn flower shop
书店 shūdiàn bookstore
药店 yàodiàn pharmacy
奶茶店 nǎichádiàn bubble tea shop
酒店 jiǔdiàn hotel (not a liquor store!)
火锅店 huǒguōdiàn hotpot restaurant
Step 4 — Tone-Pair Practice (X + 3rd tone): This time every word ends in third tone, grouped by the first syllable's tone. Say each group out loud. (✨ = new word)

1 + 3  (first + third)

鸡脚 jījiǎo chicken feet ✨
香水 xiāngshuǐ perfume ✨
机场 jīchǎng airport ✨
烧烤 shāokǎo barbecue ✨

2 + 3  (second + third)

牛奶 niúnǎi milk ✨
游泳 yóuyǒng swimming
苹果 píngguǒ apple
啤酒 píjiǔ beer

3 + 3  (third + third)

水果 shuǐguǒ fruit
美女 měinǚ pretty girl
手表 shǒubiǎo wristwatch ✨
老板 lǎobǎn boss ✨

4 + 3  (fourth + third)

地铁 dìtiě subway / metro ✨
电影 diànyǐng movie
室友 shìyǒu roommate ✨
电脑 diànnǎo computer
Tone trick — 3 + 3 changes sound: When two third tones meet, the first one is pronounced like a second tone: 水果 sounds like shuí-guǒ, 美女 like méi-nǚ. The writing keeps both third-tone marks — only the pronunciation changes.
Extra — 买卖 (mǎimài) = business/trade: Literally "buy-sell"! 我是做买卖的 = "I'm in business / I'm a trader" — the same 你是做什么的 job pattern from Lesson 3.
Step 5 — Conversation Practice: Combine it all — ask the date and day, talk about birthdays, and use the 店 shops.
T
今天几月几号？周几？
jīntiān jǐ yuè jǐ hào? zhōujǐ?
What's the date today? What day of the week?
P
今天8月28号，周五。
jīntiān bā yuè èrshíbā hào, zhōuwǔ.
Today is August 28th, Friday.
T
你的生日是几月几号？
nǐ de shēngrì shì jǐ yuè jǐ hào?
What date is your birthday?
P
我的生日是3月9号。你呢？
wǒ de shēngrì shì sān yuè jiǔ hào. nǐ ne?
My birthday is March 9th. And you?
T
明天是我爸爸的生日 — 他的生日是2月的第一个周六！
míngtiān shì wǒ bàba de shēngrì — tā de shēngrì shì èr yuè de dì yī ge zhōuliù!
Tomorrow is my dad's birthday — his birthday is the first Saturday of February!
P
哈哈！你们周六一起做什么？
hāhā! nǐmen zhōuliù yìqǐ zuò shénme?
Haha! What are you doing together on Saturday?
T
因为爸爸很喜欢吃火锅，所以我们一起去火锅店。
yīnwèi bàba hěn xǐhuān chī huǒguō, suǒyǐ wǒmen yìqǐ qù huǒguōdiàn.
Because dad loves hotpot, we're going to a hotpot restaurant together.
P
我周天想去书店和咖啡店。
wǒ zhōutiān xiǎng qù shūdiàn hé kāfēidiàn.
On Sunday I want to go to the bookstore and a café.
T
你是做什么的？
nǐ shì zuò shénme de?
What do you do for work?
P
我是做买卖的！
wǒ shì zuò mǎimài de!
I'm in business!

Grammar Spotlight

几月几号？ — asking & saying dates
Dates are number + 月 (month) + number + 号 (day): 3月9号 = March 9th. Ask with 几 in both slots: 今天几月几号？ No 是 needed with 今天/明天, just like times.
你的生日是几月几号？我的生日是3月9号。
nǐ de shēngrì shì jǐ yuè jǐ hào? wǒ de shēngrì shì sān yuè jiǔ hào.
What date is your birthday? My birthday is March 9th.
今天周几？ — asking the day of the week
周几 = "which day of the week?". Answer with 周 + number (周一…周六) or 周天 (Sunday). 天 (day/sky) replaces the number for Sunday.
今天周几？明天周五。
jīntiān zhōujǐ? míngtiān zhōuwǔ.
What day is it today? Tomorrow is Friday.
第 + number + 个 — ordinal numbers
第 turns a number into "the Nth": 第一个 = the first, 第二个 = the second. 2月的第一个周五 = "the first Friday of February" — note the 的 linking the month to it.
爸爸的生日是2月的第一个周五。
bàba de shēngrì shì èr yuè de dì yī ge zhōuwǔ.
Dad's birthday is the first Friday of February.
[thing] + 店 — the shop word-block
Add 店 (shop) to what it sells: 花店 flower shop, 书店 bookstore, 药店 pharmacy, 奶茶店 bubble tea shop, 火锅店 hotpot restaurant. Exception: 酒店 = hotel, not a liquor store!
我想去咖啡店和水果店。
wǒ xiǎng qù kāfēidiàn hé shuǐguǒdiàn.
I want to go to the café and the fruit shop.
Tone pairs — X + 3rd tone
Words ending in third tone: 1+3 (香水), 2+3 (牛奶), 3+3 (水果 — first syllable sounds like 2nd tone!), 4+3 (地铁). Say each group aloud.
香水，牛奶，水果，地铁。
xiāngshuǐ, niúnǎi, shuǐguǒ, dìtiě.
perfume, milk, fruit, subway.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "What's the date today?" and "What day of the week?":
今天几月几号？今天周几？(jīntiān jǐ yuè jǐ hào? jīntiān zhōujǐ?)
2. Say "My birthday is March 9th":
我的生日是3月9号。(wǒ de shēngrì shì sān yuè jiǔ hào)
3. Say "Dad's birthday is the first Friday of February":
爸爸的生日是2月的第一个周五。(bàba de shēngrì shì èr yuè de dì yī ge zhōuwǔ)
4. Name these shops: bookstore, pharmacy, bubble tea shop, hotpot restaurant:
书店，药店，奶茶店，火锅店 (shūdiàn, yàodiàn, nǎichádiàn, huǒguōdiàn)
5. What does 酒店 mean? And 买卖?
酒店 = hotel (not liquor store!); 买卖 = business/trade — 我是做买卖的 (I'm in business)
6. Say the tone-pair words: perfume, milk, fruit, subway:
香水 (1+3)，牛奶 (2+3)，水果 (3+3 — say shuí-guǒ)，地铁 (4+3)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages, write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, answer the questions, then send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
Saying where someone is with 在 (at), asking 在哪儿？(where?), and the key word order [person] 在 [place] [verb] — "Lily reads at the café". All the 店 shops from Lesson 17 now become places you can be at, plus the gym, the park, and the airport.
Step 1 — 在 (zài) = to be at: One little word does it all: 我在咖啡店 = "I'm at the café". Ask where with 哪儿 (nǎr): 你在哪儿？ = "Where are you?"

New Words — Where & Work

在 zài to be at / in
哪儿 nǎr where
在哪儿 zài nǎr (at) where?
工作 gōngzuò to work / job
做什么 zuò shénme doing what?
Step 2 — Places to be: Three new places below — and all eight 店 shops from Lesson 17 (咖啡店, 水果店, 花店, 书店, 药店, 奶茶店, 酒店, 火锅店) work exactly the same way after 在. You met 公园 and 机场 in the tone drills; now they're full words!

New Words — Places

健身房 jiànshēnfáng gym ✨
公园 gōngyuán park
机场 jīchǎng airport
The big word-order rule: The place comes before the verb! Lily在咖啡店看书 = "Lily reads at the café" — literally "Lily at café reads". Never put the place after the verb like in English.
Step 3 — Conversation Practice: Ask where people are, what they're doing there, and where they work.
T
你在哪儿？
nǐ zài nǎr?
Where are you?
P
我在健身房。
wǒ zài jiànshēnfáng.
I'm at the gym.
T
你在健身房做什么？
nǐ zài jiànshēnfáng zuò shénme?
What are you doing at the gym?
P
我在健身房游泳。你呢？你在哪儿？
wǒ zài jiànshēnfáng yóuyǒng. nǐ ne? nǐ zài nǎr?
I'm swimming at the gym. And you? Where are you?
T
我在书店看书。妈妈在花店，爸爸在火锅店吃火锅。
wǒ zài shūdiàn kàn shū. māma zài huādiàn, bàba zài huǒguōdiàn chī huǒguō.
I'm reading at the bookstore. Mom is at the flower shop, dad is eating hotpot at the hotpot restaurant.
P
哈哈！你哥哥在哪儿工作？
hāhā! nǐ gēge zài nǎr gōngzuò?
Haha! Where does your older brother work?
T
他在机场工作。他周末喜欢和朋友一起去公园。
tā zài jīchǎng gōngzuò. tā zhōumò xǐhuān hé péngyou yìqǐ qù gōngyuán.
He works at the airport. On weekends he likes going to the park with friends.
P
我也很喜欢公园！我们周六一起去公园吧。
wǒ yě hěn xǐhuān gōngyuán! wǒmen zhōuliù yìqǐ qù gōngyuán ba.
I like the park too! Let's go to the park together on Saturday.

Grammar Spotlight

[person] 在 [place] — being somewhere
在 works as the verb "to be at" — no extra word needed: Lily在咖啡店 = "Lily is at the café". Negate with 不在: 她不在家 = "she's not home".
Lily在咖啡店。
Lily zài kāfēidiàn.
Lily is at the café.
在哪儿？ — asking where
Swap the place for 哪儿 (where) and you have the question — same word order as the answer: 你在哪儿？→ 我在健身房。
Lily在哪儿？Lily在咖啡店。
Lily zài nǎr? Lily zài kāfēidiàn.
Where is Lily? Lily is at the café.
[person] 在 [place] [verb] — doing something somewhere
The location phrase goes before the action: Lily在咖啡店看书 = "Lily reads at the café". Ask what someone is doing there with 在[place]做什么？ or combine both questions: 在哪儿做什么？
Lily在咖啡店看书。
Lily zài kāfēidiàn kàn shū.
Lily is reading at the café.
在哪儿工作？ — where do you work?
工作 (to work) follows the same rule: 你在哪儿工作？ = "Where do you work?" Answer with 我在 + place + 工作. Remember 你是做什么的？ (Lesson 3) asks what your job is; this asks where.
你在哪儿工作？我在机场工作。
nǐ zài nǎr gōngzuò? wǒ zài jīchǎng gōngzuò.
Where do you work? I work at the airport.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "Where are you?" and answer "I'm at the gym":
你在哪儿？我在健身房。(nǐ zài nǎr? wǒ zài jiànshēnfáng)
2. Say "Lily is reading at the café" (mind the word order!):
Lily在咖啡店看书。(Lily zài kāfēidiàn kàn shū) — place BEFORE the verb
3. Ask "What is Lily doing at the café?":
Lily在咖啡店做什么？(Lily zài kāfēidiàn zuò shénme?)
4. Ask "Where do you work?" and answer "I work at the airport":
你在哪儿工作？我在机场工作。(nǐ zài nǎr gōngzuò? wǒ zài jīchǎng gōngzuò)
5. Name the three new places: gym, park, airport:
健身房 (jiànshēnfáng)，公园 (gōngyuán)，机场 (jīchǎng)
6. Say "Dad is eating hotpot at the hotpot restaurant":
爸爸在火锅店吃火锅。(bàba zài huǒguōdiàn chī huǒguō)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages, write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, answer the questions, then send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
Inviting people out with 你有时间…吗？ (do you have time…?), saying 有时间 / 没时间, plus a handful of life words — singer, next year, travel, buy — and a reading passage about 小美 the singer who dreams of Spain.
Step 1 — New Words: 时间 (time) works with the 有/没有 pattern from Lesson 5. And you already met 买 hiding inside 买卖 (buy-sell = business) from Lesson 17 — now it stands alone. 歌手 is literally "song-hand" — 手 (hand) marks a person who does something!

New Words

时间 shíjiān time ✨
歌手 gēshǒu singer ✨
明年 míngnián next year ✨
旅游 lǚyóu to travel ✨
买 mǎi to buy ✨
但是 dànshì but (review)

From the Reading Passage

西班牙 xībānyá Spain ✨ (you knew 西班牙语!)
面包店 miànbāodiàn bakery ✨ (店 family +1)
Memory trick — 明天 → 明年: 明 (bright) points to "the next one": 明天 = tomorrow (next day), 明年 = next year. Same logic, new word for free!
Step 2 — The invitation pattern: [time] + 你有时间和我一起 + [verb] + 吗？ = "Do you have time to [verb] with me [time]?" Answer 有时间！ (I have time!) or 没时间 (no time). This stacks three old patterns: 有/没有 (L5), 和…一起 (L16), and 吗 questions (L1).
T
周末你有时间和我一起喝咖啡吗？
zhōumò nǐ yǒu shíjiān hé wǒ yìqǐ hē kāfēi ma?
Do you have time to have coffee with me on the weekend?
P
有时间！我们周六去咖啡店吧。
yǒu shíjiān! wǒmen zhōuliù qù kāfēidiàn ba.
I have time! Let's go to the café on Saturday.
T
明天你有时间和我一起吃饭吗？
míngtiān nǐ yǒu shíjiān hé wǒ yìqǐ chīfàn ma?
Do you have time to eat with me tomorrow?
P
哈哈，我明天没时间，因为我每天都非常忙。
bù xǐhuān ma? hāhā, wǒ míngtiān méi shíjiān, yīnwèi wǒ měitiān dōu fēicháng máng.
Haha, I don't have time tomorrow, because I'm very busy every day.
T
你为什么很忙？
nǐ wèishénme hěn máng?
Why are you busy?
P
因为我是做买卖的！但是我周末有时间。
yīnwèi wǒ shì zuò mǎimài de! dànshì wǒ zhōumò yǒu shíjiān.
Because I'm in business! But I have time on weekends.
T
你想明年做什么？
nǐ xiǎng míngnián zuò shénme?
What do you want to do next year?
P
我想明年和女朋友一起去中国旅游。我想去火锅店吃火锅，去书店买书！
wǒ xiǎng míngnián hé nǚpéngyou yìqǐ qù zhōngguó lǚyóu. wǒ xiǎng qù huǒguōdiàn chī huǒguō, qù shūdiàn mǎi shū!
Next year I want to travel to China with my girlfriend. I want to eat hotpot at a hotpot restaurant and buy books at a bookstore!

Grammar Spotlight

有时间 / 没时间 — having (no) time
时间 (time) plugs straight into the 有/没有 pattern from Lesson 5. Remember: negate 有 with 没, never 不. 没有时间去旅游 = "no time to travel".
我周末有时间，但是明天没时间。
wǒ zhōumò yǒu shíjiān, dànshì míngtiān méi shíjiān.
I have time on the weekend, but no time tomorrow.
[time] 你有时间和我一起 [verb] 吗？ — inviting someone
The polite way to ask someone out: time word first, then 你有时间, then 和我一起 + activity + 吗. Answer with 有时间！ or apologize with 没时间.
周末你有时间和我一起喝咖啡吗？
zhōumò nǐ yǒu shíjiān hé wǒ yìqǐ hē kāfēi ma?
Do you have time to have coffee with me on the weekend?
明年 — next year (the 明 family)
明天 = tomorrow, 明年 = next year — 明 (bright) marks "the next one". Combine with plans: 我想明年去旅游 = "I want to travel next year".
她想明年和男朋友一起去西班牙旅游。
tā xiǎng míngnián hé nánpéngyou yìqǐ qù xībānyá lǚyóu.
She wants to travel to Spain with her boyfriend next year.
去 [place] [verb] — going somewhere to do something
Chain 去 + place + activity: 去面包店买面包 (go to the bakery to buy bread), 去公园跑步 (go to the park to run). Stack several in one breath, just like the passage does!
去咖啡店看书，去面包店买面包，去公园跑步。
qù kāfēidiàn kàn shū, qù miànbāodiàn mǎi miànbāo, qù gōngyuán pǎobù.
Go to the café to read, to the bakery to buy bread, to the park to run.
Step 3 — Reading Practice: Read this out loud — it reviews 每天都, 在家 + verb, 因为, 觉得, 好听, 想, 和…一起, the 店 shops, and 但是!
Reading: 小美 the Singer
小美是一个歌手，她每天都在家学西班牙语，因为她觉得西班牙语歌非常好听。
xiǎoměi shì yí ge gēshǒu, tā měitiān dōu zài jiā xué xībānyáyǔ, yīnwèi tā juéde xībānyáyǔ gē fēicháng hǎotīng.
Xiaomei is a singer; she studies Spanish at home every day, because she thinks Spanish songs sound wonderful.

她想明年和男朋友一起去西班牙旅游。
tā xiǎng míngnián hé nánpéngyou yìqǐ qù xībānyá lǚyóu.
She wants to travel to Spain with her boyfriend next year.

去西班牙的咖啡店看书，去面包店买面包，去公园跑步。
qù xībānyá de kāfēidiàn kàn shū, qù miànbāodiàn mǎi miànbāo, qù gōngyuán pǎobù.
Go to Spanish cafés to read, to bakeries to buy bread, to parks to run.

小美的妹妹也想去，但是她是学生，每天都非常忙，没有时间去旅游。
xiǎoměi de mèimei yě xiǎng qù, dànshì tā shì xuéshēng, měitiān dōu fēicháng máng, méiyǒu shíjiān qù lǚyóu.
Xiaomei's younger sister wants to go too, but she's a student, very busy every day, with no time to travel.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "Do you have time to have coffee with me on the weekend?":
周末你有时间和我一起喝咖啡吗？(zhōumò nǐ yǒu shíjiān hé wǒ yìqǐ hē kāfēi ma?)
2. Answer "I have time!" and "I have no time":
有时间！/ 没时间 (yǒu shíjiān / méi shíjiān) — negate 有 with 没, never 不!
3. Say "I want to travel to Spain with my girlfriend next year":
我想明年和女朋友一起去西班牙旅游。(wǒ xiǎng míngnián hé nǚpéngyou yìqǐ qù xībānyá lǚyóu)
4. Say "Go to the bakery to buy bread, go to the park to run":
去面包店买面包，去公园跑步。(qù miànbāodiàn mǎi miànbāo, qù gōngyuán pǎobù)
5. What do 歌手 and 明年 mean? What's the logic behind each?
歌手 = singer ("song-hand" — 手 marks a doer); 明年 = next year (明 = "the next one", like 明天 tomorrow)
6. From the passage: Why can't Xiaomei's sister go traveling?
因为她是学生，每天都非常忙，没有时间去旅游。(yīnwèi tā shì xuéshēng, měitiān dōu fēicháng máng, méiyǒu shíjiān qù lǚyóu) — she's a student, too busy, no time.
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages, write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, answer the questions, then send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences. (3) Read the 小美 passage aloud and send a voice message.
What this lesson covers
Describing what people look like — eye color, hair color — with …是什么颜色的？, two new colors (棕色 brown, 金色 gold/blond), and a tone-pair drill where the second syllable is the neutral tone: short, light, unstressed. Family words, 子-words, and face words all live here!
Step 1 — Face words: Notice something? Every face word below ends in a light neutral-tone syllable — that's today's pronunciation theme hiding in the vocabulary.

New Words — Face & Colors

眼睛 yǎnjing eyes ✨
头发 tóufa hair ✨
鼻子 bízi nose ✨
耳朵 ěrduo ears ✨
棕色 zōngsè brown ✨
金色 jīnsè gold / blond
Step 2 — Tone-Pair Practice (X + neutral tone): The second syllable loses its tone — say it short, light, and quick, like an echo. Grouped by the first syllable's tone. (✨ = new word)

1 + 0  (first + neutral)

妈妈 māma mom
桌子 zhuōzi table ✨
哥哥 gēge older brother
包子 bāozi steamed bun

2 + 0  (second + neutral)

石头 shítou stone ✨
鞋子 xiézi shoes ✨
爷爷 yéye grandpa
朋友 péngyou friend

3 + 0  (third + neutral)

酒吧 jiǔbā bar ✨
喜欢 xǐhuan to like
饺子 jiǎozi dumplings ✨
姐姐 jiějie older sister

4 + 0  (fourth + neutral)

谢谢 xièxie thank you
爸爸 bàba dad
漂亮 piàoliang pretty
串串 chuànchuan skewers (Sichuan snack) ✨
Spot the pattern — the 子 (zi) suffix: 子 tacks onto nouns and always goes neutral: 桌子 table, 鞋子 shoes, 饺子 dumplings, 鼻子 nose, 包子 bun. And family doubles (妈妈, 爸爸, 哥哥, 姐姐, 爷爷) echo their second syllable the same way.
Step 3 — Conversation Practice: Ask what color someone's eyes and hair are — the answer ends in 的, just like A是[color]的 from Lesson 4.
T
你的眼睛是什么颜色的？
nǐ de yǎnjing shì shénme yánsè de?
What color are your eyes?
P
我的眼睛是蓝色的。你呢？
wǒ de yǎnjing shì lánsè de. nǐ ne?
My eyes are blue. And yours?
T
我的眼睛是棕色的。你妈妈的头发是什么颜色的？
wǒ de yǎnjing shì zōngsè de. nǐ māma de tóufa shì shénme yánsè de?
My eyes are brown. What color is your mom's hair?
P
她的头发是金色的，我的头发是棕色的。
tā de tóufa shì jīnsè de, wǒ de tóufa shì zōngsè de.
Her hair is blond, my hair is brown.
T
你喜欢吃饺子吗？
nǐ xǐhuan chī jiǎozi ma?
Do you like eating dumplings?
P
很喜欢！我周末想和朋友一起去酒吧喝啤酒，吃串串。
hěn xǐhuan! wǒ zhōumò xiǎng hé péngyou yìqǐ qù jiǔbā hē píjiǔ, chī chuànchuan.
Love them! This weekend I want to go to a bar with friends to drink beer and eat skewers.
T
哈哈！你的鞋子很漂亮。
hāhā! nǐ de xiézi hěn piàoliang.
Haha! Your shoes are pretty.
P
谢谢！
xièxie!
Thanks!

Grammar Spotlight

…是什么颜色的？ — asking what color something is
[thing] 是什么颜色的？ asks its color; answer [color]色的 — keeping the 的 at the end, exactly like A是[color]的 from Lesson 4. 他的眼睛是什么颜色的？棕色的。
他的眼睛是什么颜色的？棕色的。
tā de yǎnjing shì shénme yánsè de? zōngsè de.
What color are his eyes? Brown.
你妈妈的头发… — chaining 的 for family
Stack possessives: 你妈妈的头发 = "your mom's hair" (你[的]妈妈 usually drops the first 的 with family). Answers: 黑色的, 棕色的, 金色的.
你妈妈的头发是什么颜色的？金色的。
nǐ māma de tóufa shì shénme yánsè de? jīnsè de.
What color is your mom's hair? Blond.
Neutral tone (轻声) — the light second syllable
Some syllables lose their tone entirely — say them short and light: māma not māmā, xǐhuan not xǐhuān. Typical homes of the neutral tone: family doubles (爸爸/妈妈/哥哥), the 子 suffix (桌子/鞋子/饺子/鼻子), and face words (头发, 耳朵, 眼睛).
妈妈，石头，饺子，谢谢。
māma, shítou, jiǎozi, xièxie.
mom (1+0), stone (2+0), dumplings (3+0), thanks (4+0).
[noun] + 子 — the noun suffix
子 (neutral zi) rides along on many everyday nouns without adding meaning: 桌子 table, 鞋子 shoes, 饺子 dumplings, 鼻子 nose, 包子 bun. Spot it, say it lightly.
桌子，鞋子，饺子，鼻子。
zhuōzi, xiézi, jiǎozi, bízi.
table, shoes, dumplings, nose.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "What color are his eyes?" and answer "brown":
他的眼睛是什么颜色的？棕色的。(tā de yǎnjing shì shénme yánsè de? zōngsè de)
2. Ask "What color is your mom's hair?" and give three possible answers:
你妈妈的头发是什么颜色的？黑色的 / 棕色的 / 金色的 (hēisè de / zōngsè de / jīnsè de)
3. Name the four face words:
眼睛 (yǎnjing) eyes，头发 (tóufa) hair，鼻子 (bízi) nose，耳朵 (ěrduo) ears
4. Say one neutral-tone word from each group: 1+0, 2+0, 3+0, 4+0:
桌子 (1+0)，石头 (2+0)，饺子 (3+0)，串串 (4+0) — second syllable short and light!
5. Which suffix makes 桌/鞋/饺/鼻 into everyday nouns, and how is it pronounced?
子 — always neutral tone: 桌子, 鞋子, 饺子, 鼻子 (zhuōzi, xiézi, jiǎozi, bízi)
6. Say "I want to go to a bar with friends to eat skewers":
我想和朋友一起去酒吧吃串串。(wǒ xiǎng hé péngyou yìqǐ qù jiǔbā chī chuànchuan)
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages, write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, answer the questions, then send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
Five emotions (happy, angry, sad, surprised, scared), more body parts (head, mouth, teeth), and how to say something's wrong: 你怎么了？ — "What's wrong?" — answered with [body part] 很痛 or 我很不舒服. Plus three wonderful eye-ear-mouth idioms!
Step 1 — Emotions: These are adjectives, so they work with 很 and all the Lesson 9 degree words: 我很高兴, 我非常生气, 我有点儿害怕. Fun one: 吃惊 = "eat a shock" = surprised!

New Words — Emotions

高兴 gāoxìng happy ✨
生气 shēngqì angry ✨
难过 nánguò sad ✨
吃惊 chījīng surprised ("eat a shock") ✨
害怕 hàipà scared / afraid ✨
Step 2 — More body parts: Adding to Lesson 20's face words (眼睛, 头发, 鼻子, 耳朵). Note 嘴巴 ends in a light neutral tone — just like last lesson's words!

New Words — Body

头 tóu head ✨
嘴巴 zuǐba mouth ✨
牙齿 yáchǐ teeth ✨
Step 3 — Saying you're sick: 你怎么了？ = "What's wrong?" Answer with what hurts (我的牙齿很痛) or how you feel (我很不舒服). A sick person is a 病人 — that's what you are at the doctor's!

New Words — Feeling Sick

怎么了 zěnme le what's wrong? ✨
痛 tòng to hurt / painful ✨
舒服 shūfu comfortable / well ✨
病人 bìngrén sick person / patient ✨
Three idioms with your new body words:
左耳进右耳出 (zuǒ ěr jìn yòu ěr chū) — "left ear in, right ear out" = in one ear, out the other.
睁一只眼闭一只眼 (zhēng yì zhī yǎn bì yì zhī yǎn) — "open one eye, close one eye" = turn a blind eye.
七嘴八舌 (qī zuǐ bā shé) — "seven mouths, eight tongues" = everyone talking at once.
Step — Extra Words: A little grab-bag from the end of the lesson. 长颈鹿 is literally "long-neck deer", and 可以 is your new permission word (compare 会 from Lesson 15 = learned skill).

Extra Words

可以 kěyǐ can / may (permission) ✨
长颈鹿 chángjǐnglù giraffe ("long-neck deer") ✨
车 chē car (from 开车)
出 chū to go out
Step 4 — Conversation Practice: Ask what's wrong, describe what hurts, and throw the emotions around.
T
你怎么了？你很难过吗？
nǐ zěnme le? nǐ hěn nánguò ma?
What's wrong? Are you sad?
P
我不难过，但是我的牙齿很痛，很不舒服。
wǒ bù nánguò, dànshì wǒ de yáchǐ hěn tòng, hěn bù shūfu.
I'm not sad, but my teeth really hurt — I feel awful.
T
你是病人！你可以明天去药店。
nǐ shì bìngrén! nǐ kěyǐ míngtiān qù yàodiàn.
You're a sick man! You can go to the pharmacy tomorrow.
P
好。我的头也很痛。
hǎo. wǒ de tóu yě hěn tòng.
OK. My head hurts too.
T
我很吃惊！你昨天在酒吧吃串串喝啤酒吗？
wǒ hěn chījīng! nǐ zuótiān zài jiǔbā chī chuànchuan hē píjiǔ ma?
I'm shocked! Were you at the bar yesterday eating skewers and drinking beer?
P
是……妈妈很生气，但是爸爸睁一只眼闭一只眼。
shì…… māma hěn shēngqì, dànshì bàba zhēng yì zhī yǎn bì yì zhī yǎn.
Yes… Mom was furious, but dad turned a blind eye.
T
哈哈！妈妈的话，左耳进右耳出吗？
hāhā! māma de huà, zuǒ ěr jìn yòu ěr chū ma?
Haha! Mom's words — in one ear, out the other?
P
哈哈，我有点儿害怕她！明天我不去酒吧，我会很高兴。
hāhā, wǒ yǒudiǎnr hàipà tā! míngtiān wǒ bú qù jiǔbā, wǒ huì hěn gāoxìng.
Haha, I'm a little scared of her! Tomorrow I'm not going to the bar — I'll be happy.

Grammar Spotlight

你怎么了？ — asking what's wrong
The all-purpose "What's wrong? / What happened?". Answer with what hurts or how you feel. Works for anyone: 他怎么了？ = "What's wrong with him?"
你怎么了？我的牙齿很痛。
nǐ zěnme le? wǒ de yáchǐ hěn tòng.
What's wrong? My teeth hurt.
[body part] 很痛 — saying what hurts
Name the body part, add 很痛: 我的头很痛 (my head hurts), 牙齿很痛 (toothache). 痛 is an adjective — degree words work: 非常痛, 有点儿痛.
我的头很痛，牙齿也很痛。
wǒ de tóu hěn tòng, yáchǐ yě hěn tòng.
My head hurts, and my teeth hurt too.
舒服 / 不舒服 — well / unwell
舒服 = comfortable; 我很不舒服 = "I feel really unwell" — the polite way to say you're sick. Note the neutral tone: shūfu.
我今天很不舒服，我是病人。
wǒ jīntiān hěn bù shūfu, wǒ shì bìngrén.
I feel really unwell today — I'm sick.
Emotions with 很 — 我很高兴 / 很生气 / 很难过
Emotion words are adjectives, so they follow the Lesson 9 degree scale: 非常吃惊 (totally shocked), 有点儿害怕 (a bit scared), 一点儿也不高兴 (not happy at all).
妈妈很生气，我有点儿害怕。
māma hěn shēngqì, wǒ yǒudiǎnr hàipà.
Mom is angry — I'm a little scared.
可以 — can / may (permission)
可以 + verb = "can / is allowed to": 你可以明天去药店. Compare 会 (Lesson 15) = a learned skill; 可以 = permission or possibility.
你可以明天去药店。
nǐ kěyǐ míngtiān qù yàodiàn.
You can go to the pharmacy tomorrow.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "What's wrong?" and answer "My teeth hurt / I feel unwell":
你怎么了？我的牙齿很痛 / 我很不舒服。(nǐ zěnme le? wǒ de yáchǐ hěn tòng / wǒ hěn bù shūfu)
2. Name the five emotions: happy, angry, sad, surprised, scared:
高兴 (gāoxìng)，生气 (shēngqì)，难过 (nánguò)，吃惊 (chījīng)，害怕 (hàipà)
3. Name the three new body words: head, mouth, teeth:
头 (tóu)，嘴巴 (zuǐba)，牙齿 (yáchǐ)
4. What does 左耳进右耳出 mean literally and as an idiom?
"Left ear in, right ear out" — in one ear, out the other (not listening). (zuǒ ěr jìn yòu ěr chū)
5. Which idiom means "turn a blind eye"? And "everyone talking at once"?
睁一只眼闭一只眼 (open one eye, close one eye) / 七嘴八舌 (seven mouths, eight tongues)
6. Say "Mom is angry, I'm a little scared" and "giraffe":
妈妈很生气，我有点儿害怕。(māma hěn shēngqì, wǒ yǒudiǎnr hàipà) · 长颈鹿 (chángjǐnglù — "long-neck deer")
Homework tasks: (1) Listen to the voice messages, write the sentences in pinyin with tone marks, answer the questions, then send a photo. (2) Use today's new words and sentence patterns to make five sentences.
What this lesson covers
A milestone: your first character reading lesson! Nothing here is new by sound — you've known all these words since Lessons 1–6. The new skill is recognizing them written in characters, then reading ten full sentences with no pinyin help.
Step 1 — The 20 words in characters: Go through the grid and try to read the character before peeking at the pinyin next to it. These 20 are enough to build every sentence below.

Characters to Recognize

你 nǐ you
我 wǒ I / me
她 tā she
他 tā he
是 shì to be
不 bù not
我们 wǒmen we
吗 ma (question particle)
中国 zhōngguó China
美国 měiguó USA
英国 yīngguó UK
人 rén person
喜欢 xǐhuan to like
吃 chī to eat
喝 hē to drink
咖啡 kāfēi coffee
苹果 píngguǒ apple
你的 nǐ de your
爸爸 bàba dad
妈妈 māma mom
Radar for radicals — characters carry hints:
口 (mouth) hides in everything you do with your mouth: 吃 eat, 喝 drink, 吗 (spoken question), 咖啡 coffee.
女 (woman) marks 她 (she) and 妈妈 (mom) — while 他 (he) uses the person radical 亻, which also builds 我们/他们 (们 = plural).
And 人 (person) is simply two legs walking — 中国人 = "China person" = Chinese!
Step 2 — Read the ten sentences: This is the real test — each sentence uses only the characters above. Read it aloud first, then tap the blurred line to check your pinyin and meaning.

Reading Practice — No Pinyin!

Read each sentence aloud, then tap to check yourself.

1. 我是中国人。
wǒ shì zhōngguó rén — I am Chinese.
2. 我不是美国人。
wǒ bú shì měiguó rén — I am not American.
3. 她是英国人。
tā shì yīngguó rén — She is British.
4. 我的妈妈是中国人。
wǒ de māma shì zhōngguó rén — My mom is Chinese.
5. 你喝咖啡吗？
nǐ hē kāfēi ma? — Do you drink coffee?
6. 他不喝咖啡。
tā bù hē kāfēi — He doesn't drink coffee.
7. 我的爸爸喜欢美国。
wǒ de bàba xǐhuan měiguó — My dad likes America.
8. 你喜欢吃苹果吗？
nǐ xǐhuan chī píngguǒ ma? — Do you like eating apples?
9. 我爸爸的妈妈是英国人。
wǒ bàba de māma shì yīngguó rén — My dad's mom is British.
10. 美国人喜欢喝咖啡。
měiguó rén xǐhuan hē kāfēi — Americans like drinking coffee.

Reading Spotlight

Country + 人 — reading nationalities
Once you recognize 中国 / 美国 / 英国, adding 人 (person) reads as the nationality. Spot the shared 国 (country) box in all three!
中国人，美国人，英国人。
zhōngguó rén, měiguó rén, yīngguó rén.
Chinese, American, British.
Spotting 不 and 吗 — the sentence's mood at a glance
Scan for 不 before the verb (negative) and 吗 at the end (question) — you can tell what kind of sentence it is before reading every character.
他不喝咖啡。你喝咖啡吗？
tā bù hē kāfēi. nǐ hē kāfēi ma?
He doesn't drink coffee. Do you drink coffee?
的 chains in characters — 我爸爸的妈妈
Sentence 9 is the trickiest read: 我爸爸的妈妈 = "my dad's mom" — the 的 links left-to-right, and family words drop the first 的 (我[的]爸爸), just like Lesson 20 taught.
我爸爸的妈妈是英国人。
wǒ bàba de māma shì yīngguó rén.
My dad's mom is British.
Practice tip: The deck repeats every card twice on purpose — run the grid top-to-bottom, then bottom-to-top, until each character is instant. Then read all ten sentences aloud in one go. The Reading tab's flip cards are great extra practice for single characters!

What this lesson covers
Two parts in one: Part 1 — character deck #2, with 21 words you know from Lessons 2–5 (names, age, preferences, colors, fruits, drinks, pets) now written in characters, plus ten pinyin-free sentences. Part 2 — the secret of how characters are built: the 包 family, where one sound-part makes eleven characters!
Part 1 — Characters to recognize: All old friends by sound — read each character before peeking at the pinyin. Spot the shared parts: 犭 (beast) in both 猫 and 狗, 氵 (water) inside 酒 (liquid!), and 艹 (grass) on top of 草莓 and 香蕉.

Characters to Recognize

叫 jiào to be called
什么 shénme what
名字 míngzi name
多大了 duō dà le how old?
哪国人 nǎ guó rén which nationality?
更喜欢 gèng xǐhuan to prefer
还是 háishì or (in questions)
和 hé and
都 dōu both / all
有 yǒu to have
没有 méiyǒu to not have
红色 hóngsè red
绿色 lǜsè green
草莓 cǎoméi strawberry
香蕉 xiāngjiāo banana
啤酒 píjiǔ beer
白酒 báijiǔ baijiu (Chinese liquor)
猫 māo cat
狗 gǒu dog
男朋友 nánpéngyou boyfriend
女朋友 nǚpéngyou girlfriend
Read the ten sentences: Only characters from this deck and Lesson 22 — read aloud, then tap to check.

Reading Practice — No Pinyin!

Read each sentence aloud, then tap to check yourself.

1. 你叫什么名字？
nǐ jiào shénme míngzi? — What's your name?
2. 你更喜欢啤酒还是白酒？
nǐ gèng xǐhuan píjiǔ háishì báijiǔ? — Do you prefer beer or baijiu?
3. 你更喜欢红色还是绿色？
nǐ gèng xǐhuan hóngsè háishì lǜsè? — Do you prefer red or green?
4. 你的男朋友多大了？
nǐ de nánpéngyou duō dà le? — How old is your boyfriend?
5. 他没有女朋友。
tā méiyǒu nǚpéngyou — He doesn't have a girlfriend.
6. 我的爸爸不吃香蕉。
wǒ de bàba bù chī xiāngjiāo — My dad doesn't eat bananas.
7. 苹果和草莓，我都喜欢吃。
píngguǒ hé cǎoméi, wǒ dōu xǐhuan chī — Apples and strawberries, I like eating both.
8. 你有狗吗？
nǐ yǒu gǒu ma? — Do you have a dog?
9. 你更喜欢猫还是狗？
nǐ gèng xǐhuan māo háishì gǒu? — Do you prefer cats or dogs?
10. 红酒和啤酒，我的妈妈都不喜欢。
hóngjiǔ hé píjiǔ, wǒ de māma dōu bù xǐhuan — Red wine and beer, my mom likes neither.
Part 2 — The 包 family (how characters are really built!): Most Chinese characters are two parts glued together: a sound part and a meaning part (the radical). 包 (bāo) is the sound part here — add a different radical and you get a new character that still sounds like "bao/pao", but the radical tells you the meaning: hand 扌 → hug, foot ⻊ → run, water 氵 → bubble, fire 火 → cannon. Learn one family, unlock eleven characters!

The 包 Family — "b" Sounds First, Then "p" Sounds

包 bāo bag / bun (the base — as in 包子!)
苞 bāo flower bud (艹 grass)
胞 bāo cell / womb (月 flesh)
雹 báo hail (雨 rain)
饱 bǎo full (after eating) (饣 food)
抱 bào to hug (扌 hand)
鲍 bào abalone (鱼 fish)
袍 páo robe / gown (衤 clothing)
跑 pǎo to run (⻊ foot — as in 跑步!)
泡 pào bubble / to soak (氵 water)
炮 pào cannon / firecracker (火 fire)

Reading Spotlight

更喜欢 A 还是 B？ — reading choice questions
Spot 还是 in the middle of a sentence and you know it's an A-or-B question (Lesson 5 pattern). No 吗 needed — 还是 does the asking.
你更喜欢猫还是狗？
nǐ gèng xǐhuan māo háishì gǒu?
Do you prefer cats or dogs?
A 和 B，…都… — the topic comes first
Sentences 7 and 10 front the choices: 苹果和草莓，我都喜欢吃 — "Apples and strawberries, I like them both." The 都 (both) sits after the subject, before the verb.
红酒和啤酒，我的妈妈都不喜欢。
hóngjiǔ hé píjiǔ, wǒ de māma dōu bù xǐhuan.
Red wine and beer — my mom likes neither.
Radical + 包 — phono-semantic characters (形声字)
About 80% of all Chinese characters work like the 包 family: the radical hints at the meaning, the other part hints at the sound. When you meet a new character, look for a part you know — it probably tells you how to say it.
包 → 抱，跑，泡，炮，饱。
bāo → bào, pǎo, pào, pào, bǎo.
bag → hug, run, bubble, cannon, full.

What this lesson covers
Three parts: Money — yuan, jiao, big numbers and asking 多少钱？; Food — ordering with 我要, twelve dishes and snacks, and the measure words that go with them; Characters #3 — the degree & frequency words you know from Lessons 9 and 13, now in characters, with your first character-only reading passage!
Part 1 — Money: Chinese money has a written form and a spoken form: 元 (yuán) is formal, 块 (kuài) is what everyone actually says — like "dollar" vs "buck". Same for tenths: 角 (jiǎo) formal, 毛 (máo) spoken.

New Words — Money

元 yuán yuan (formal/written) ✨
块 kuài yuan (spoken, "buck") ✨
角 jiǎo 1/10 yuan (formal) ✨
毛 máo 1/10 yuan (spoken) ✨
多少钱 duōshao qián how much (money)? ✨
这个 zhège this one
百 bǎi hundred ✨
一共 yígòng altogether / in total ✨
Reading big numbers & prices:
101 = 一百零一 (say the zero! yì bǎi líng yī) · 110 = 一百一十 · 112 = 一百一十二 · 200 = 两百 (two hundred uses 两, not 二!)
¥5.50 = 五块五 (wǔ kuài wǔ) · ¥84.50 = 八十四块五 · ¥360.52 = 三百六十块五毛二 — kuai, then mao, digit by digit.
Part 2 — Food & ordering: Say 我要… ("I'll have…") plus a measure word: 一杯可乐 (a cup of cola), 一袋薯片 (a bag of chips), 一碗牛肉面 (a bowl of beef noodles). Chinese always counts things with a measure word in the middle!

New Words — Food

汉堡 hànbǎo hamburger ✨
披萨 pīsà pizza ✨
可乐 kělè cola ✨
薯片 shǔpiàn potato chips ✨
薯条 shǔtiáo french fries ✨
饺子 jiǎozi dumplings
饼干 bǐnggān biscuits / cookies ✨
酸奶 suānnǎi yogurt ✨
巧克力 qiǎokèlì chocolate ✨
牛肉面 niúròumiàn beef noodles ✨
宫保鸡丁 gōngbǎo jīdīng kung pao chicken ✨
番茄炒蛋 fānqié chǎodàn tomato & scrambled eggs ✨

Measure Words

一个 yí ge one (general: burger, pizza)
一杯 yì bēi a cup of ✨
一瓶 yì píng a bottle of ✨
一袋 yí dài a bag of ✨
一斤 yì jīn a jin (500 g) ✨
一盒 yì hé a box of ✨
一碗 yì wǎn a bowl of ✨
一份 yí fèn a portion of ✨
我要 wǒ yào I want / I'll have ✨
Extra — the 干 (gān) "dried" family: 干 = dried, and it makes snack words!

Extra Words — Dried Things

牛肉干 niúròugān beef jerky ✨
水果干 shuǐguǒgān dried fruit ✨
葡萄干 pútaogān raisins (dried grapes!) ✨
Step — Conversation Practice: A fast-food run — ordering with 我要, measure words, and paying with 多少钱 / 一共.
T
你好！你要什么？
nǐ hǎo! nǐ yào shénme?
Hello! What would you like?
P
我要一个汉堡和一份薯条。一共多少钱？
wǒ yào yí ge hànbǎo hé yí fèn shǔtiáo. yígòng duōshao qián?
I'll have a hamburger and a portion of fries. How much altogether?
T
一共二十五块五。你要可乐吗？
yígòng èrshíwǔ kuài wǔ. nǐ yào kělè ma?
25.50 altogether. Would you like a cola?
P
要！一瓶可乐多少钱？
yào! yì píng kělè duōshao qián?
Yes! How much is a bottle of cola?
T
一瓶可乐三块五。可乐3块5一瓶。
yì píng kělè sān kuài wǔ. kělè sān kuài wǔ yì píng.
A bottle of cola is 3.50. (Both word orders work!)
P
好，我要一瓶。
hǎo, wǒ yào yì píng.
OK, I'll take a bottle.
T
好的，一共二十九块。
hǎo de, yígòng èrshíjiǔ kuài.
Alright, 29 kuai altogether.
P
谢谢！我也要一袋葡萄干，哈哈！
xièxie! wǒ yě yào yí dài pútaogān, hāhā!
Thanks! I'll also take a bag of raisins, haha!

Grammar Spotlight

多少钱？ — asking the price
这个多少钱？ = "How much is this one?"; 一共多少钱？ = "How much altogether?". Answer with number + 块 (+ 毛 for tenths): 一共八十四块五.
这个多少钱？一共三百六十块五毛二。
zhège duōshao qián? yígòng sānbǎi liùshí kuài wǔ máo èr.
How much is this? 360.52 altogether.
[number] [measure word] [noun] — counting everything
Chinese never says "one cola" — it says "one bottle-of cola": 一瓶可乐. Match the measure to the shape: 杯 cup, 瓶 bottle, 袋 bag, 碗 bowl, 份 portion, 盒 box, 斤 by weight, 个 for most everything else.
我要一碗牛肉面和一份宫保鸡丁。
wǒ yào yì wǎn niúròumiàn hé yí fèn gōngbǎo jīdīng.
I'll have a bowl of beef noodles and a kung pao chicken.
一袋薯片3块5 / 薯片3块5一袋 — price per unit, two word orders
Both orders are correct: [measure+item] + price, or item + price + [per-measure]. Shop signs love the second one.
一袋薯片多少钱？薯片3块5一袋。
yí dài shǔpiàn duōshao qián? shǔpiàn sān kuài wǔ yí dài.
How much is a bag of chips? Chips are 3.50 a bag.
两百, 一百零一 — big-number rules
200 = 两百 (两 before measure/hundred, never 二百 in speech — same 两 rule as Lesson 5!). An inside zero is spoken: 101 = 一百零一. But 110 = 一百一十 (no 零).
一百零一，一百一十，两百。
yìbǎi líng yī, yìbǎi yīshí, liǎngbǎi.
101, 110, 200.
Part 3 — Characters #3: Deck three! All the degree words (Lesson 9) and frequency words (Lesson 13) plus fruits, 黄色, teacher/student and languages — now in characters. Read each one before peeking.

Characters to Recognize

西瓜 xīguā watermelon
芒果 mángguǒ mango
葡萄 pútao grape
黄色 huángsè yellow
老师 lǎoshī teacher
学生 xuéshēng student
汉语 hànyǔ Chinese (language)
英语 yīngyǔ English (language)
西班牙语 xībānyáyǔ Spanish (language)
很 hěn very
非常 fēicháng extremely
比较 bǐjiào rather / quite
不太 bú tài not too / not very
一点儿也不 yìdiǎnr yě bù not at all
可能 kěnéng maybe / probably
经常 jīngcháng often
有时候 yǒushíhou sometimes
很少 hěn shǎo rarely
几乎 jīhū almost
从不 cóngbù never
每天 měitiān every day

Reading Practice — No Pinyin!

Read each sentence aloud, then tap to check yourself.

1. 我的妈妈几乎从不喝咖啡。
wǒ de māma jīhū cóngbù hē kāfēi — My mom almost never drinks coffee.
2. 他一点儿也不喜欢吃葡萄。
tā yìdiǎnr yě bù xǐhuan chī pútao — He doesn't like eating grapes at all.
3. 你的汉语老师是哪国人？
nǐ de hànyǔ lǎoshī shì nǎ guó rén? — What nationality is your Chinese teacher?
4. 她的男朋友可能非常喜欢喝啤酒。
tā de nánpéngyou kěnéng fēicháng xǐhuan hē píjiǔ — Her boyfriend probably really likes beer.
5. 你的猫经常吃草莓吗？
nǐ de māo jīngcháng chī cǎoméi ma? — Does your cat often eat strawberries?
6. 我非常喜欢西班牙语。
wǒ fēicháng xǐhuan xībānyáyǔ — I really like Spanish.
7. 他的女朋友每天都吃苹果。
tā de nǚpéngyou měitiān dōu chī píngguǒ — His girlfriend eats apples every day.
8. 我的爸爸有时候更喜欢啤酒，有时候更喜欢白酒。
wǒ de bàba yǒushíhou gèng xǐhuan píjiǔ, yǒushíhou gèng xǐhuan báijiǔ — My dad sometimes prefers beer, sometimes baijiu.
Your first character-only passage! Four lines about Mary & Nick — every character comes from decks 1–3. Read each line aloud, then tap to check.

Reading: Mary & Nick

Mary是美国人，她非常喜欢喝咖啡，她几乎每天都喝。
Mary shì měiguó rén, tā fēicháng xǐhuan hē kāfēi, tā jīhū měitiān dōu hē. — Mary is American; she loves coffee and drinks it almost every day.
她的男朋友叫Nick，是西班牙人。Nick是老师。
tā de nánpéngyou jiào Nick, shì xībānyá rén. Nick shì lǎoshī. — Her boyfriend is called Nick; he's Spanish. Nick is a teacher.
他们有白色的猫，没有狗。
tāmen yǒu báisè de māo, méiyǒu gǒu. — They have a white cat, no dog.
他们都很喜欢西瓜和啤酒。
tāmen dōu hěn xǐhuan xīguā hé píjiǔ. — They both really like watermelon and beer.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "How much is this one?" and "How much altogether?":
这个多少钱？/ 一共多少钱？(zhège duōshao qián? / yígòng duōshao qián?)
2. Say these prices out loud: ¥5.50, ¥84.50, ¥360.52:
五块五 / 八十四块五 / 三百六十块五毛二 (wǔ kuài wǔ / bāshísì kuài wǔ / sānbǎi liùshí kuài wǔ máo èr)
3. Say 101, 110 and 200 in Chinese:
一百零一，一百一十，两百 (yìbǎi líng yī, yìbǎi yīshí, liǎngbǎi — 两 not 二!)
4. Order "a hamburger, a bowl of beef noodles and a bottle of cola":
我要一个汉堡，一碗牛肉面和一瓶可乐。(wǒ yào yí ge hànbǎo, yì wǎn niúròumiàn hé yì píng kělè)
5. Ask the price of a bag of chips and answer "3.50 a bag":
一袋薯片多少钱？薯片3块5一袋。(yí dài shǔpiàn duōshao qián? shǔpiàn sān kuài wǔ yí dài)
6. What are 牛肉干, 水果干 and 葡萄干?
Beef jerky, dried fruit, raisins — 干 (gān) = dried! (niúròugān, shuǐguǒgān, pútaogān)

What this lesson covers
A full restaurant visit, Chinese style: polite service talk with 您 and 需要, customizing your order (extra chili? no sugar?), and paying the way everyone actually pays — 支付宝 or 微信, "你扫我吧!" Then Characters #4: opinions, reasons and favorites in characters, with a Linda reading passage.
Quick review first: The lesson sheet re-runs Lesson 24's food and measure words — and levels up the ordering: 我要两袋薯片，一个面包，一瓶可乐，两斤苹果。一共多少钱？一共27块3. Spot 两 doing its thing before every measure word!
Step 1 — Polite restaurant talk: 您 (nín) is the polite "you" — it's what every waiter says to you. 需要 = to need: 您需要什么？ "What do you need?" is the standard service greeting.

New Words — At the Restaurant

您 nín you (polite) ✨
需要 xūyào to need ✨
菜单 càidān menu ✨
还要 hái yào to also want ✨
另一碗 lìng yì wǎn the other bowl (另 = other) ✨
多放 duō fàng add more (of) ✨
少放 shǎo fàng add less (of) ✨
别放 bié fàng don't add (别 = don't!) ✨
再 zài additionally / one more ✨
来 lái to have/bring (ordering word) ✨
那 nà then / in that case ✨
吧 ba (suggestion particle) ✨
Step 2 — Paying, Chinese style: Nobody carries cash! The cashier asks 支付宝，微信还是现金？ and then: 你扫我吧 — "scan me" (my QR code). 扫 = to scan.

New Words — Payment

支付宝 zhīfùbǎo Alipay ✨
微信 wēixìn WeChat (Pay) ✨
现金 xiànjīn cash ✨
你扫我 nǐ sǎo wǒ you scan me (my QR) ✨

New Words — Customizing & From the Dialogue

燕麦奶 yànmàinǎi oat milk ✨
冰块 bīngkuài ice cubes ✨
糖 táng sugar ✨
煎蛋 jiāndàn fried egg ✨
青菜 qīngcài leafy greens ✨
冰可乐 bīng kělè ice-cold cola (冰 = iced) ✨
收银员 shōuyínyuán cashier ✨
Step 3 — The restaurant conversation (from your lesson sheet!): Three roles — waiter (服务员), you the customer, and the cashier. Follow the whole visit: order, customize, add drinks, pay by scan.
W
您好，您需要什么？
nín hǎo, nín xūyào shénme?
Hello, what would you like? (Waiter)
P
我要两碗牛肉面，两个煎蛋，和一份青菜。
wǒ yào liǎng wǎn niúròumiàn, liǎng ge jiāndàn, hé yí fèn qīngcài.
I'll have two bowls of beef noodles, two fried eggs, and a portion of greens.
W
牛肉面要辣椒吗？
niúròumiàn yào làjiāo ma?
Chili in the beef noodles?
P
一碗不要，另一碗少放辣椒。
yì wǎn bú yào, lìng yì wǎn shǎo fàng làjiāo.
None in one bowl, and go easy on the chili in the other.
W
好的，还需要什么吗？
hǎo de, hái xūyào shénme ma?
Got it. Anything else?
P
再来两瓶冰可乐。
zài lái liǎng píng bīng kělè.
And two bottles of ice-cold cola.
C
您好，一共73块5。支付宝，微信还是现金呢？
nín hǎo, yígòng qīshísān kuài wǔ. zhīfùbǎo, wēixìn háishì xiànjīn ne?
Hello, 73.50 altogether. Alipay, WeChat or cash? (Cashier)
P
微信。
wēixìn.
WeChat.
C
好的，那您扫我吧。
hǎo de, nà nín sǎo wǒ ba.
OK — then scan me, please.

Grammar Spotlight

您 + 需要 — service politeness
您 is the respectful "you" (service staff, elders, strangers). 您需要什么？ / 还需要什么吗？ are the standard "What would you like / anything else?" — answer with 我要… or the casual 来….
您需要什么？我要一碗牛肉面。
nín xūyào shénme? wǒ yào yì wǎn niúròumiàn.
What would you like? A bowl of beef noodles.
多放 / 少放 / 别放 — customizing your food
放 = to put/add. 多放辣椒 = extra chili, 少放糖 = less sugar, 别放冰块 = no ice (别 = "don't", your new negative command word!).
多放辣椒，别放糖。
duō fàng làjiāo, bié fàng táng.
Extra chili, no sugar.
再来 + [measure] — "one more round"
再 = additionally, 来 = the casual ordering verb: 再来两瓶冰可乐 = "bring two more bottles of iced cola". 另一 + measure picks out "the other one": 另一碗 = the other bowl.
再来一份薯条！
zài lái yí fèn shǔtiáo!
One more fries, please!
支付宝，微信还是现金？你扫我吧 — paying by QR
The 还是 choice question you know, now at the till. 吧 softens a suggestion: 你扫我吧 = "(how about) you scan me". 那 = "in that case". This is daily life in China — cash is the rare answer!
支付宝还是微信？那你扫我吧。
zhīfùbǎo háishì wēixìn? nà nǐ sǎo wǒ ba.
Alipay or WeChat? Then scan me.
Part 2 — Characters #4: Deck four! Opinions (觉得, 好吃/好喝, 一般般), reasons (因为/所以/为什么, 但是), favorites (最喜欢) and more — all words you've known for months, now in characters.

Characters to Recognize

好吃 hǎochī tasty (food)
好喝 hǎohē tasty (drink)
觉得 juéde to think / feel
但是 dànshì but
因为 yīnwèi because
所以 suǒyǐ so / therefore
为什么 wèishénme why
番茄 fānqié tomato
土豆 tǔdòu potato
最喜欢 zuì xǐhuan to like most / favorite
颜色 yánsè color
水果 shuǐguǒ fruit
动物 dòngwù animal
白色 báisè white
黑色 hēisè black
一般般 yìbānbān so-so
知道 zhīdào to know

Reading Practice — No Pinyin!

Read each sentence aloud, then tap to check yourself.

1. 我不知道她喜欢什么颜色。
wǒ bù zhīdào tā xǐhuan shénme yánsè — I don't know what color she likes.
2. 我觉得葡萄很好吃。
wǒ juéde pútao hěn hǎochī — I think grapes are delicious.
3. 我觉得他的女朋友可能是美国人。
wǒ juéde tā de nǚpéngyou kěnéng shì měiguó rén — I think his girlfriend is probably American.
4. 你最喜欢什么颜色？
nǐ zuì xǐhuan shénme yánsè? — What's your favorite color?
5. 你的男朋友最喜欢什么动物？
nǐ de nánpéngyou zuì xǐhuan shénme dòngwù? — What's your boyfriend's favorite animal?
6. 什么动物是黑色和白色的？
shénme dòngwù shì hēisè hé báisè de? — What animal is black and white? (熊猫 — the panda!)
7. 你每天都吃水果吗？
nǐ měitiān dōu chī shuǐguǒ ma? — Do you eat fruit every day?
8. 你更喜欢番茄还是土豆？
nǐ gèng xǐhuan fānqié háishì tǔdòu? — Do you prefer tomatoes or potatoes?
9. 因为喝酒不好所以我很少喝酒。
yīnwèi hē jiǔ bù hǎo suǒyǐ wǒ hěn shǎo hē jiǔ — Because drinking isn't good, I rarely drink.
10. 我男朋友觉得白酒一点也不好喝。
wǒ nánpéngyou juéde báijiǔ yìdiǎn yě bù hǎohē — My boyfriend thinks baijiu tastes awful.
Character passage #2 — Linda: Five lines, all characters from decks 1–4. Read each aloud, then tap to check.

Reading: Linda

Linda是英国人，她没有男朋友。她是英语老师，她的学生都是中国人。
Linda shì yīngguó rén, tā méiyǒu nánpéngyou. tā shì yīngyǔ lǎoshī, tā de xuéshēng dōu shì zhōngguó rén. — Linda is British and has no boyfriend. She's an English teacher; her students are all Chinese.
Linda的汉语一点儿也不好。她的爸爸是英国人，妈妈是美国人。
Linda de hànyǔ yìdiǎnr yě bù hǎo. tā de bàba shì yīngguó rén, māma shì měiguó rén. — Linda's Chinese isn't good at all. Her dad is British, her mom American.
她几乎每天都喝咖啡，有时候喝啤酒。
tā jīhū měitiān dōu hē kāfēi, yǒushíhou hē píjiǔ. — She drinks coffee almost every day, sometimes beer.
她从不喝白酒，因为她觉得白酒不好喝。
tā cóngbù hē báijiǔ, yīnwèi tā juéde báijiǔ bù hǎohē. — She never drinks baijiu, because she thinks it tastes bad.
她喜欢吃番茄，土豆，苹果和芒果。她最喜欢的颜色是红色。
tā xǐhuan chī fānqié, tǔdòu, píngguǒ hé mángguǒ. tā zuì xǐhuan de yánsè shì hóngsè. — She likes tomatoes, potatoes, apples and mangoes. Her favorite color is red.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. The waiter asks what you'd like (politely). What does he say, and how do you order two bowls of beef noodles?
您需要什么？— 我要两碗牛肉面。(nín xūyào shénme? — wǒ yào liǎng wǎn niúròumiàn)
2. Say "extra chili", "less sugar", "no ice":
多放辣椒 / 少放糖 / 别放冰块 (duō fàng làjiāo / shǎo fàng táng / bié fàng bīngkuài)
3. Order "two more bottles of ice-cold cola":
再来两瓶冰可乐。(zài lái liǎng píng bīng kělè)
4. The cashier asks how you'll pay — give the full question and answer "WeChat, scan me":
支付宝，微信还是现金呢？— 微信，你扫我吧。(zhīfùbǎo, wēixìn háishì xiànjīn ne? — wēixìn, nǐ sǎo wǒ ba)
5. Say "one bowl without, go easy on the chili in the other":
一碗不要，另一碗少放辣椒。(yì wǎn bú yào, lìng yì wǎn shǎo fàng làjiāo)
6. From the Linda passage: why does she never drink baijiu?
因为她觉得白酒不好喝。(yīnwèi tā juéde báijiǔ bù hǎohē) — Because she thinks it tastes bad.

What this lesson covers
Your whole day in Chinese! Telling the time (现在几点？), the daily routine from 起床 (getting up) to 睡觉 (going to sleep), and two handy patterns: 一般来说 ("generally speaking") and 从…到… ("from… to…").
Step 1 — What time is it? 点 (diǎn) = o'clock. Ask 现在几点？ "What time is it now?" Answer with number + 点: 现在两点. Watch out: 2 o'clock is 两点, never 二点 — just like 两个 and 两碗!

New Words — Time

几点 jǐ diǎn what time ✨
现在 xiànzài now ✨
一般来说 yìbān lái shuō generally speaking ✨
从……到…… cóng…dào… from… to…

Telling the Time

两点 liǎng diǎn 2:00 (两, not 二!)
两点零五（分） liǎng diǎn líng wǔ (fēn) 2:05 (零 = zero) ✨
两点十分 liǎng diǎn shí fēn 2:10 (分 = minute) ✨
两点半 liǎng diǎn bàn 2:30 (半 = half) ✨
Clock formula: [hour] 点 + [minutes] 分. Minutes under 10 get a 零 in front: 2:05 = 两点零五. Half past = 半: 6:30 = 六点半. The 分 at the end is optional when it's clear — 两点十 works too.
Step 2 — Your day, start to finish: Every routine word below is a verb you can drop a time in front of. Notice the meal family: 早饭 (breakfast), 午饭 (lunch), 晚饭 (dinner) — 早 = morning, 午 = noon, 晚 = evening, 饭 = meal.

New Words — Daily Routine

起床 qǐchuáng to get up ✨
吃早饭 chī zǎofàn to eat breakfast ✨
上班 shàngbān to start work / go to work ✨
吃午饭 chī wǔfàn to eat lunch ✨
下班 xiàbān to finish work ✨
回家 huí jiā to go home ✨
吃晚饭 chī wǎnfàn to eat dinner ✨
睡觉 shuìjiào to go to sleep ✨
Memory trick — 上 & 下: 上班 = "go UP to the shift" (start work), 下班 = "come DOWN from the shift" (finish work). Same 上/下 pair as 上周/下周 in the Timeline tab.
Step 3 — Put it together: Time goes before the verb: 我六点半起床 — "I at 6:30 get up". Same rule as place-before-verb from Lesson 18. Add 一般来说 at the front for "usually", and 从…到… for a range of days.
T
Pawel，现在几点？
Pawel, xiànzài jǐ diǎn?
Pawel, what time is it now?
P
现在两点十分。
xiànzài liǎng diǎn shí fēn.
It's 2:10 now.
T
你几点起床？
nǐ jǐ diǎn qǐchuáng?
What time do you get up?
P
一般来说，我七点起床，七点半吃早饭。
yìbān lái shuō, wǒ qī diǎn qǐchuáng, qī diǎn bàn chī zǎofàn.
Generally, I get up at 7 and eat breakfast at 7:30.
T
你几点上班？几点下班？
nǐ jǐ diǎn shàngbān? jǐ diǎn xiàbān?
What time do you start work? When do you finish?
P
从周一到周五，我九点上班，六点下班。
cóng zhōuyī dào zhōuwǔ, wǒ jiǔ diǎn shàngbān, liù diǎn xiàbān.
From Monday to Friday, I start work at 9 and finish at 6.
T
下班以后你做什么？
xiàbān yǐhòu nǐ zuò shénme?
What do you do after work?
P
我六点半回家，七点吃晚饭，十一点睡觉。
wǒ liù diǎn bàn huí jiā, qī diǎn chī wǎnfàn, shíyī diǎn shuìjiào.
I go home at 6:30, eat dinner at 7, and go to sleep at 11.

Grammar Spotlight

现在几点？— asking the time
几 = "how many" (small numbers), so 几点 = "how many o'clock". Answer by replacing 几 with the number: 现在几点？→ 现在两点半.
现在几点？现在两点零五分。
xiànzài jǐ diǎn? xiànzài liǎng diǎn líng wǔ fēn.
What time is it? It's 2:05.
Person + [time] + verb — time before the verb
The time always comes before the action, never at the end like in English. To ask, swap the time for 几点: 你几点起床？
我六点半起床。你几点起床？
wǒ liù diǎn bàn qǐchuáng. nǐ jǐ diǎn qǐchuáng?
I get up at 6:30. What time do you get up?
一般来说，… — generally speaking
Put it at the very start of the sentence to say what you usually do. 一般 = general (you know it from 一般般 = so-so!), 来说 = "speaking".
一般来说，我七点起床。
yìbān lái shuō, wǒ qī diǎn qǐchuáng.
Generally speaking, I get up at 7.
从 A 到 B — from A to B
Works for days or times. The 从…到… chunk goes at the front, before the person or right after it.
从周三到周五，我十点上班。
cóng zhōusān dào zhōuwǔ, wǒ shí diǎn shàngbān.
From Wednesday to Friday, I start work at 10.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Ask "What time is it now?" and answer "It's 2:30":
现在几点？— 现在两点半。(xiànzài jǐ diǎn? — xiànzài liǎng diǎn bàn)
2. Say these times: 2:05, 2:10, 8:30
两点零五（分）/ 两点十分 / 八点半 (liǎng diǎn líng wǔ (fēn) / liǎng diǎn shí fēn / bā diǎn bàn)
3. Why is 2 o'clock 两点 and not 二点?
Before a measure word like 点 (or 个, 碗, 杯), "two" is always 两 (liǎng).
4. Say "I get up at 6:30":
我六点半起床。(wǒ liù diǎn bàn qǐchuáng)
5. Say "Generally speaking, I eat lunch at 12":
一般来说，我十二点吃午饭。(yìbān lái shuō, wǒ shí'èr diǎn chī wǔfàn)
6. Say "From Wednesday to Friday, I start work at 10":
从周三到周五，我十点上班。(cóng zhōusān dào zhōuwǔ, wǒ shí diǎn shàngbān)

What this lesson covers
Your daily routine, part 2: saying something happens early (就…了) or late (才), "after" and "before" (以后 / 以前), "how come so early?" (怎么那么早), and bouncing a question back with 你呢. Then Characters #5: skills and hobbies in characters (会, 唱歌, 游泳, 开车…), with a Lulu reading passage.
Quick review first: The lesson sheet repeats all of Lesson 26 — 几点, 现在, 起床, 吃早饭, 上班, 吃午饭, 下班, 回家, 吃晚饭, 睡觉, 一般来说, 从…到…, and clock times like 两点零五 and 两点半. Today builds on top of them.
Step 1 — Early or late? 早 = early, 晚 = late. To react to a time, put 就 or 才 before the verb. 就…了 = "already, so early!" and 才 = "only then, so late!" Same clock time, opposite feeling.

New Words — Early, Late & Reacting

早 zǎo early ✨
晚 wǎn late ✨
就……了 jiù…le already / as early as (earlier than expected) ✨
才 cái only then / as late as (later than expected) ✨
怎么 zěnme how come / why (surprised) ✨
那么 nàme so / that (much) ✨
The teacher's slide — same time, two reactions: Someone says they go to sleep at 11:30. The young guy thinks that's early; the grandpa thinks it's late!
A
我每天11:30睡觉。
wǒ měitiān shíyī diǎn bàn shuìjiào.
I go to sleep at 11:30 every day.
😮
什么？你11:30就睡觉了？
shénme? nǐ shíyī diǎn bàn jiù shuìjiào le?
What? You're asleep by 11:30 already?! (young guy: so EARLY)
👴
什么？你11:30才睡觉？
shénme? nǐ shíyī diǎn bàn cái shuìjiào?
What? You don't go to bed until 11:30?! (grandpa: so LATE)
Memory trick: 就 comes with 了 at the end ("done already!"). 才 never takes 了: 他6点就起床了 (up at 6, so early!) vs 他10点才起床 (not up until 10, so late!).
Step 2 — After & before: 以后 = after, 以前 = before (you met 以前 in Lesson 13). In Chinese they go after the action: 起床以后 = "get-up after" = after getting up. 你呢? = "and you?", to bounce any question back.

New Words — After, Before & Asking Back

以后 yǐhòu after ✨
以前 yǐqián before
你呢 nǐ ne and you? / what about you? ✨

Extra Words — From the Expressions

吃东西 chī dōngxi to eat something / have a snack ✨
去跑步 qù pǎobù to go running
Step 3 — The conversation (from your lesson sheet!): A gets up at 6:20, and B can't believe it. Spot 就…了, 怎么那么早, 以后, 从…到… and 你呢.
A
我几乎每天都6:20起床。
wǒ jīhū měitiān dōu liù diǎn èrshí qǐchuáng.
I get up at 6:20 almost every day.
B
什么？你6:20就起床了？你怎么那么早起床？
shénme? nǐ liù diǎn èrshí jiù qǐchuáng le? nǐ zěnme nàme zǎo qǐchuáng?
What? You're up at 6:20 already? How come you get up so early?
A
因为我喜欢起床以后去公园跑步。
yīnwèi wǒ xǐhuan qǐchuáng yǐhòu qù gōngyuán pǎobù.
Because I like going running in the park after I get up.
B
那你几点上班？
nà nǐ jǐ diǎn shàngbān?
So what time do you start work?
A
从周一到周四，我9点上班，周五10点上班。你呢？
cóng zhōuyī dào zhōusì, wǒ jiǔ diǎn shàngbān, zhōuwǔ shí diǎn shàngbān. nǐ ne?
Monday to Thursday I start at 9, on Friday at 10. And you?
B
一般来说，我每天都8:30上班。
yìbān lái shuō, wǒ měitiān dōu bā diǎn bàn shàngbān.
Generally, I start work at 8:30 every day.
Time check — 6:20: 六点二十 (liù diǎn èrshí). Minutes from 10 up need no 零, and twenty is 二十, never 两十. 两 only replaces 二 before a measure word like 点.

Grammar Spotlight

[time] + 就 + verb + 了 — earlier than expected
Shows surprise that something happens early. The 了 at the end is part of the pattern.
他6点就起床了。
tā liù diǎn jiù qǐchuáng le.
He's up at 6 already (so early!).
[time] + 才 + verb — later than expected
Shows surprise that something happens late. No 了 with 才!
他10点才起床。
tā shí diǎn cái qǐchuáng.
He doesn't get up until 10 (so late!).
[action] + 以后 / 以前 — after / before doing something
The order is the reverse of English: the action comes first, then 以后/以前. Then say what you do.
我起床以后去跑步。我睡觉以前吃东西。
wǒ qǐchuáng yǐhòu qù pǎobù. wǒ shuìjiào yǐqián chī dōngxi.
After I get up I go running. Before I sleep I have a snack.
你怎么那么 + adjective + verb? — how come so…?
怎么 here means "how come" (surprised), not "how to". 那么 = "that much / so". You know 怎么 from 你怎么了? in Lesson 21.
你怎么那么早起床？你怎么那么晚睡觉？
nǐ zěnme nàme zǎo qǐchuáng? nǐ zěnme nàme wǎn shuìjiào?
How come you get up so early? How come you go to bed so late?
…你呢？ — and you?
Answer a question, then add 你呢? to ask the same thing back. No need to repeat the whole question.
我9点上班。你呢？
wǒ jiǔ diǎn shàngbān. nǐ ne?
I start work at 9. And you?
Part 2 — Characters #5: Deck five! Skills and hobbies from Lessons 13–15 (会, 想, 唱歌, 游泳, 开车, 跳舞…), all words you already know by sound, now in characters.

Characters to Recognize

会 huì can / know how to
唱歌 chànggē to sing
游泳 yóuyǒng to swim
说 shuō to speak
做饭 zuòfàn to cook
开车 kāichē to drive
烧烤 shāokǎo barbecue
跑步 pǎobù to run / jog
面包 miànbāo bread
蛋糕 dàngāo cake
想 xiǎng to want to
鞋子 xiézi shoes
很多 hěn duō many / a lot
跳舞 tiàowǔ to dance
打篮球 dǎ lánqiú to play basketball
Spot the radicals: 足 (foot) is in 跑 (run) and 跳 (jump) — both use your feet! 氵 (water) is in 游泳 (swim). 口 (mouth) is in 唱 (sing). And 包 in 面包 is your old friend from the 包 family (Lesson 23).

Reading Practice — No Pinyin!

Read each sentence aloud, then tap to check yourself.

1. 你会游泳吗？
nǐ huì yóuyǒng ma? — Can you swim?
2. 你更喜欢吃面包还是蛋糕？
nǐ gèng xǐhuan chī miànbāo háishì dàngāo? — Do you prefer bread or cake?
3. 我觉得Adele非常会唱歌。
wǒ juéde Adele fēicháng huì chànggē — I think Adele is a really good singer.
4. 你有很多黑色的鞋子吗？
nǐ yǒu hěn duō hēisè de xiézi ma? — Do you have lots of black shoes?
5. 他不太会做饭，所以他很少做饭。
tā bú tài huì zuòfàn, suǒyǐ tā hěn shǎo zuòfàn — He isn't very good at cooking, so he rarely cooks.
6. 我几乎每天都跑步。
wǒ jīhū měitiān dōu pǎobù — I run almost every day.
7. 你的女朋友会说汉语吗？
nǐ de nǚpéngyou huì shuō hànyǔ ma? — Can your girlfriend speak Chinese?
8. 我不会开车，但是我的男朋友会开车。
wǒ bú huì kāichē, dànshì wǒ de nánpéngyou huì kāichē — I can't drive, but my boyfriend can.
9. 我一点儿也不想吃苹果。
wǒ yìdiǎnr yě bù xiǎng chī píngguǒ — I don't want to eat apples at all.
10. 我有白色和黄色的鞋子。
wǒ yǒu báisè hé huángsè de xiézi — I have white and yellow shoes.
Character passage #3 — Lulu: Five lines, all characters from decks 1–5. Read each aloud, then tap to check.

Reading: Lulu

Lulu 28岁，她几乎从不吃苹果，因为她觉得苹果一点儿也不好吃。
Lulu èrshíbā suì, tā jīhū cóngbù chī píngguǒ, yīnwèi tā juéde píngguǒ yìdiǎnr yě bù hǎochī. — Lulu is 28. She almost never eats apples, because she thinks they aren't tasty at all.
但是她的男朋友觉得苹果非常好，所以他们家经常有很多苹果。
dànshì tā de nánpéngyou juéde píngguǒ fēicháng hǎo, suǒyǐ tāmen jiā jīngcháng yǒu hěn duō píngguǒ. — But her boyfriend thinks apples are great, so there are often lots of apples at their home.
Lulu是中国人，她会说汉语，也很会说英语和西班牙语。
Lulu shì zhōngguó rén, tā huì shuō hànyǔ, yě hěn huì shuō yīngyǔ hé xībānyáyǔ. — Lulu is Chinese. She speaks Chinese, and she's also very good at English and Spanish.
她的男朋友Jason会说英语。Jason想学汉语，所以Lulu也是他的汉语老师。
tā de nánpéngyou Jason huì shuō yīngyǔ. Jason xiǎng xué hànyǔ, suǒyǐ Lulu yě shì tā de hànyǔ lǎoshī. — Her boyfriend Jason speaks English. Jason wants to learn Chinese, so Lulu is also his Chinese teacher.
他们每天都学汉语。
tāmen měitiān dōu xué hànyǔ. — They study Chinese every day.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Your friend gets up at 6. React: "What? You're up at 6 already?"
什么？你6点就起床了？(shénme? nǐ liù diǎn jiù qǐchuáng le?)
2. Say "He doesn't get up until 10" (so late!):
他10点才起床。(tā shí diǎn cái qǐchuáng) — no 了 with 才!
3. Ask "How come you go to bed so late?"
你怎么那么晚睡觉？(nǐ zěnme nàme wǎn shuìjiào?)
4. Say "After I get up, I go running":
我起床以后去跑步。(wǒ qǐchuáng yǐhòu qù pǎobù)
5. Say "I start work at 9. And you?"
我9点上班。你呢？(wǒ jiǔ diǎn shàngbān. nǐ ne?)
6. From the Lulu passage: why does Lulu almost never eat apples?
因为她觉得苹果一点儿也不好吃。(yīnwèi tā juéde píngguǒ yìdiǎnr yě bù hǎochī) — She thinks apples aren't tasty at all.

What this lesson covers
The six parts of the day, from 凌晨 (small hours) to 晚上 (evening), so you can say 早上七点 or 晚上十点. Then the four seasons (季节), 可爱 (cute), and how many times you do something: 每周两次 = twice a week. Then Characters #6: family, places and shops in characters (哥哥, 妹妹, 书店, 电影院…), with a 小王 reading passage.
Quick review first, the neighbourhood: 附近 = nearby, 超市 = supermarket. The sheet opens with 你家附近有超市吗？ and two ways to answer: 我家附近有一个超市 or 有一个超市在我家附近.

Review Words — From Class

附近 fùjìn nearby
超市 chāoshì supermarket

Review Sentences

你家附近有超市吗？
nǐ jiā fùjìn yǒu chāoshì ma? — Is there a supermarket near your home?
对，我家附近有一个超市。/ 有一个超市在我家附近。
duì, wǒ jiā fùjìn yǒu yí ge chāoshì. / yǒu yí ge chāoshì zài wǒ jiā fùjìn. — Yes, there's a supermarket near my home.
你经常去超市吗？
nǐ jīngcháng qù chāoshì ma? — Do you often go to the supermarket?
我很少去超市，但是我去超市买很多水果和蔬菜。
wǒ hěn shǎo qù chāoshì, dànshì wǒ qù chāoshì mǎi hěn duō shuǐguǒ hé shūcài. — I rarely go to the supermarket, but when I go I buy lots of fruit and vegetables.
Step 1 — The six parts of the day: Chinese splits the day into named blocks. The teacher's slide shows them on two clocks, AM and PM. Here they are in order, starting at midnight:
凌晨
língchén
12–5 am
早上
zǎoshang
5–9 am
上午
shàngwǔ
9–12 am
中午
zhōngwǔ
12–1 pm
下午
xiàwǔ
1–7 pm
晚上
wǎnshang
7 pm–12

New Words — Parts of the Day

凌晨 língchén small hours (midnight–5 am) ✨
早上 zǎoshang early morning (5–9 am) ✨
上午 shàngwǔ late morning (9–12) ✨
中午 zhōngwǔ noon (12–1 pm) ✨
下午 xiàwǔ afternoon (1–7 pm) ✨
晚上 wǎnshang evening / night (7 pm–midnight) ✨
Memory trick, 午 = noon: 上午 = "above noon" (before noon), 中午 = "middle noon", 下午 = "below noon" (after noon). Same 上/下 as 上班/下班! And you already know the meals: 早饭, 午饭, 晚饭 match 早上, 中午, 晚上.
Step 2 — The four seasons: 季节 = season. Every season is [something] + 天 (day/sky), just like 今天 and 明天.

New Words — Seasons & More

季节 jìjié season ✨
春天 chūntiān spring 🌸 ✨
夏天 xiàtiān summer 🌳 ✨
秋天 qiūtiān autumn 🍂 ✨
冬天 dōngtiān winter ❄️ ✨
可爱 kě'ài cute
Step 3 — How many times? 次 (cì) = time / occurrence. 每 + period + number + 次: 每周两次 = twice a week. Like 两点 and 两个, "two times" is 两次, never 二次.

Extra Words — How Often

每天两次 měi tiān liǎng cì twice a day ✨
每周两次 měi zhōu liǎng cì twice a week ✨
每月两次 měi yuè liǎng cì twice a month ✨
每年两次 měi nián liǎng cì twice a year ✨
Step 4 — Put it together: A chat about your week, using parts of the day, 次, seasons and the supermarket review.
T
你早上几点起床？
nǐ zǎoshang jǐ diǎn qǐchuáng?
What time do you get up in the morning?
P
一般来说，我早上七点起床，上午九点上班。
yìbān lái shuō, wǒ zǎoshang qī diǎn qǐchuáng, shàngwǔ jiǔ diǎn shàngbān.
Generally I get up at 7 in the morning and start work at 9.
T
你家附近有超市吗？你经常去吗？
nǐ jiā fùjìn yǒu chāoshì ma? nǐ jīngcháng qù ma?
Is there a supermarket near your home? Do you go often?
P
有。我每周去两次超市，一般是下午下班以后。
yǒu. wǒ měi zhōu qù liǎng cì chāoshì, yìbān shì xiàwǔ xiàbān yǐhòu.
Yes. I go to the supermarket twice a week, usually in the afternoon after work.
T
你最喜欢什么季节？
nǐ zuì xǐhuan shénme jìjié?
What's your favorite season?
P
我最喜欢秋天，因为秋天很漂亮。你呢？
wǒ zuì xǐhuan qiūtiān, yīnwèi qiūtiān hěn piàoliang. nǐ ne?
I like autumn best, because autumn is beautiful. And you?
T
我最喜欢春天。春天公园里有很多可爱的小狗！
wǒ zuì xǐhuan chūntiān. chūntiān gōngyuán lǐ yǒu hěn duō kě'ài de xiǎo gǒu!
I like spring best. In spring the park is full of cute puppies!

Grammar Spotlight

Part of day + clock time — big before small
Chinese goes from the bigger unit to the smaller: first the part of the day, then the hour. Both go before the verb.
我晚上十一点睡觉。我下午三点喝咖啡。
wǒ wǎnshang shíyī diǎn shuìjiào. wǒ xiàwǔ sān diǎn hē kāfēi.
I go to sleep at 11 pm. I drink coffee at 3 pm.
每 + period + verb + number + 次 + object — how many times
每天 / 每周 / 每月 / 每年 goes before the verb. 两次 comes right after the verb and before the object.
我每天喝两次咖啡。我每周去两次超市。
wǒ měi tiān hē liǎng cì kāfēi. wǒ měi zhōu qù liǎng cì chāoshì.
I drink coffee twice a day. I go to the supermarket twice a week.
你最喜欢什么季节？— favorites, now with seasons
The 最喜欢 pattern from Lesson 11 works for seasons too. Add 因为… to say why.
你最喜欢什么季节？我最喜欢夏天。
nǐ zuì xǐhuan shénme jìjié? wǒ zuì xǐhuan xiàtiān.
What's your favorite season? I like summer best.
[place] + 附近有… — there is … near [place]
Place first, then 附近有 + thing. Ask with 吗 at the end.
我家附近有一个公园。
wǒ jiā fùjìn yǒu yí ge gōngyuán.
There's a park near my home.
Part 2 — Characters #6: Deck six! Family, places and shops from Lessons 3–18 (哥哥, 妹妹, 书店, 火锅, 在哪儿…), all words you already know by sound, now in characters. Only 电影院 (cinema) is new.

Characters to Recognize

岁 suì years old
哥哥 gēge older brother
弟弟 dìdi younger brother
姐姐 jiějie older sister
妹妹 mèimei younger sister
去 qù to go
旅游 lǚyóu to travel
店 diàn shop / store
书店 shūdiàn bookshop
火锅 huǒguō hot pot
花店 huādiàn flower shop
看书 kàn shū to read (a book)
在 zài to be at / in
哪儿 nǎr where
家 jiā home / family
电影 diànyǐng movie
电影院 diànyǐngyuàn cinema ✨
牛奶 niúnǎi milk
蓝色 lánsè blue
律师 lǜshī lawyer
Spot the radicals: 女 (woman) is in 姐 and 妹, and in 奶 of 牛奶. 艹 (plant) sits on top of 花 (flower). 钅 (metal) is in 锅 (pot). 广 (a roof / building) covers 店 (shop). And 院 = a place or courtyard, so 电影 + 院 = cinema.
们 for groups of people: The passage adds 们 to nouns for people: 学生们 (the students), 妹妹们 (the sisters), 家人们 (the family). Same 们 as in 我们 / 你们 / 他们. Only for people, never for things.

Reading Practice — No Pinyin!

Read each sentence aloud, then tap to check yourself.

1. 我20岁，我的哥哥32岁，姐姐25岁，妹妹18岁，我没有弟弟。
wǒ èrshí suì, wǒ de gēge sānshí'èr suì, jiějie èrshíwǔ suì, mèimei shíbā suì, wǒ méiyǒu dìdi — I'm 20, my older brother is 32, my older sister 25, my younger sister 18. I have no younger brother.
2. 我非常想去英国和西班牙旅游。
wǒ fēicháng xiǎng qù yīngguó hé xībānyá lǚyóu — I really want to travel to the UK and Spain.
3. 她的爸爸和妈妈都是律师。
tā de bàba hé māma dōu shì lǜshī — Her dad and mom are both lawyers.
4. 你的姐姐更喜欢蓝色还是红色？
nǐ de jiějie gèng xǐhuan lánsè háishì hóngsè? — Does your older sister prefer blue or red?
5. 你经常去电影院看电影吗？
nǐ jīngcháng qù diànyǐngyuàn kàn diànyǐng ma? — Do you often go to the cinema to see movies?
6. 成都有很多火锅店。
chéngdū yǒu hěn duō huǒguō diàn — Chengdu has lots of hot pot restaurants.
7. 你更喜欢在咖啡店还是书店看书？
nǐ gèng xǐhuan zài kāfēi diàn háishì shūdiàn kàn shū? — Do you prefer reading in a café or in a bookshop?
8. 我的姐姐和妹妹想在家做面包，但是我想去打篮球。
wǒ de jiějie hé mèimei xiǎng zài jiā zuò miànbāo, dànshì wǒ xiǎng qù dǎ lánqiú — My sisters want to bake bread at home, but I want to go play basketball.
9. 我非常喜欢开车去旅游。
wǒ fēicháng xǐhuan kāichē qù lǚyóu — I really love road trips (driving to travel).
10. 你家有很多书吗？
nǐ jiā yǒu hěn duō shū ma? — Do you have a lot of books at home?
Character passage #4 — 小王 (Xiao Wang): Six lines, all characters from decks 1–6. Read each aloud, then tap to check.

Reading: 小王

小王有一个哥哥和两个妹妹，他没有弟弟和姐姐。
xiǎo wáng yǒu yí ge gēge hé liǎng ge mèimei, tā méiyǒu dìdi hé jiějie. — Xiao Wang has one older brother and two younger sisters. He has no younger brother or older sister.
小王23岁，是老师，他有很多学生。
xiǎo wáng èrshísān suì, shì lǎoshī, tā yǒu hěn duō xuésheng. — Xiao Wang is 23 and a teacher. He has lots of students.
他的学生们都很喜欢他，因为他会很多也知道很多。
tā de xuéshengmen dōu hěn xǐhuan tā, yīnwèi tā huì hěn duō yě zhīdào hěn duō. — His students all like him a lot, because he can do a lot and knows a lot.
小王的哥哥有一个书店和电影院。
xiǎo wáng de gēge yǒu yí ge shūdiàn hé diànyǐngyuàn. — Xiao Wang's older brother has a bookshop and a cinema.
妹妹们经常去哥哥的店看书和看电影。
mèimeimen jīngcháng qù gēge de diàn kàn shū hé kàn diànyǐng. — The sisters often go to their brother's shops to read and watch movies.
家人们有时候也去旅游。
jiārénmen yǒushíhou yě qù lǚyóu. — The family sometimes goes travelling too.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Name the six parts of the day, in order from midnight:
凌晨 → 早上 → 上午 → 中午 → 下午 → 晚上 (língchén → zǎoshang → shàngwǔ → zhōngwǔ → xiàwǔ → wǎnshang)

我每周去两次超市。(wǒ měi zhōu qù liǎng cì chāoshì)

你家附近有超市吗？(nǐ jiā fùjìn yǒu chāoshì ma?)

他有一个书店和电影院。(tā yǒu yí ge shūdiàn hé diànyǐngyuàn) — A bookshop and a cinema.

What this lesson covers
How far is it? 离 (lí) measures the distance between two places, then 远 (far) or 近 (near): 小美家离地铁站很近 = Xiao Mei's home is very close to the subway station. Your degree words from Lesson 9 (非常 / 很 / 比较 / 不太) now say how far. Then Characters #7: work, time, places and food in characters (工作, 有时间, 饭馆, 地铁站, 鸡蛋…), with a 小张 reading passage.
Step 1 — Three new words: 离 = from (distance between two points), 远 = far, 近 = near. The pattern is always A 离 B + 很 + 远/近.

New Words — Near & Far

离 lí from / away from (distance) ✨
远 yuǎn far ✨
近 jìn near / close ✨
地铁站 dìtiězhàn subway station ✨
Memory trick, 远 vs 近: both sit on 辶, the "walking road" radical, because distance is about walking. 远 (yuǎn) hides 元 (yuán), the money word from Lesson 24, so it sounds like yuán → yuǎn. 近 (jìn) hides 斤 (jīn), the half-kilo measure word from Lesson 24, so it sounds like jīn → jìn.
Step 2 — Xiao Mei's neighbourhood: The teacher's slides put 小美家 🏠 in the middle of a map, then drew arrows: a short one to the subway 🚇 (很近) and a long one to the airport ✈️ (很远). Here is the whole map as a ladder, from closest to farthest:
🏠 小美家 xiǎo měi jiā

fēicháng jìn

很近
hěn jìn
