Places on the Map

医院 yīyuàn hospital
学校 xuéxiào school
银行 yínháng bank
酒店 jiǔdiàn hotel
水果店 shuǐguǒdiàn fruit shop
火锅店 huǒguōdiàn hot pot restaurant
花店 huādiàn flower shop
机场 jīchǎng airport

Sentences from Class

Read each sentence aloud, then tap to check yourself.

小美家离地铁站很近。
xiǎo měi jiā lí dìtiězhàn hěn jìn — Xiao Mei's home is very close to the subway station.
小美家离机场很远。
xiǎo měi jiā lí jīchǎng hěn yuǎn — Xiao Mei's home is very far from the airport.
你觉得小美家离火锅店远吗？近吗？
nǐ juéde xiǎo měi jiā lí huǒguōdiàn yuǎn ma? jìn ma? — Do you think Xiao Mei's home is far from / close to the hot pot restaurant?
小美家离医院 / 学校 / 银行很近。
xiǎo měi jiā lí yīyuàn / xuéxiào / yínháng hěn jìn — Xiao Mei's home is very close to the hospital / school / bank.
小美家离酒店不太远。
xiǎo měi jiā lí jiǔdiàn bú tài yuǎn — Xiao Mei's home isn't too far from the hotel.
小美家离水果店比较远。
xiǎo měi jiā lí shuǐguǒdiàn bǐjiào yuǎn — Xiao Mei's home is fairly far from the fruit shop.
我家离地铁站不太远。
wǒ jiā lí dìtiězhàn bú tài yuǎn — My home isn't too far from the subway station.
医院离花店非常近。
yīyuàn lí huādiàn fēicháng jìn — The hospital is extremely close to the flower shop.
离 vs 从…到…: 从…到… (Lesson 26) is a journey or a time span: 从周一到周五. 离 is a fixed distance between two places: 我家离公园很近. No movement, just "how far apart".
Step 3 — Put it together: A chat about where you live.
T
你家离地铁站远吗？
nǐ jiā lí dìtiězhàn yuǎn ma?
Is your home far from the subway station?
P
不太远。我家附近有两个地铁站。
bú tài yuǎn. wǒ jiā fùjìn yǒu liǎng ge dìtiězhàn.
Not too far. There are two subway stations near my home.
T
你家离机场远吗？
nǐ jiā lí jīchǎng yuǎn ma?
Is your home far from the airport?
P
非常远！所以我很少去机场。
fēicháng yuǎn! suǒyǐ wǒ hěn shǎo qù jīchǎng.
Really far! So I rarely go to the airport.
T
你觉得你家离超市近吗？
nǐ juéde nǐ jiā lí chāoshì jìn ma?
Do you think your home is close to the supermarket?
P
很近。我经常下班以后去超市买水果。你呢？
hěn jìn. wǒ jīngcháng xiàbān yǐhòu qù chāoshì mǎi shuǐguǒ. nǐ ne?
Very close. I often go buy fruit there after work. And you?
T
我家离超市比较远，但是离饭馆很近！
wǒ jiā lí chāoshì bǐjiào yuǎn, dànshì lí fànguǎn hěn jìn!
My home is fairly far from the supermarket, but very close to a restaurant!

Grammar Spotlight

A 离 B + 很 + 远 / 近 — how far apart
A is the place you start from, B is the place you measure to. 离 always comes before B, and 远/近 go at the end, like any adjective, with 很 in front.
我家离公园很近。
wǒ jiā lí gōngyuán hěn jìn.
My home is very close to the park.
Degree word + 远 / 近 — how far exactly
Swap 很 for any degree word from Lesson 9: 非常 > 很 > 比较 > 不太.
医院离花店非常近。小美家离水果店比较远。
yīyuàn lí huādiàn fēicháng jìn. xiǎo měi jiā lí shuǐguǒdiàn bǐjiào yuǎn.
The hospital is extremely close to the flower shop. Xiao Mei's home is fairly far from the fruit shop.
你觉得 A 离 B 远吗？— asking about distance
Add 吗 for a yes/no question. Add 你觉得 (Lesson 9) to ask for an opinion.
你觉得小美家离火锅店远吗？
nǐ juéde xiǎo měi jiā lí huǒguōdiàn yuǎn ma?
Do you think Xiao Mei's home is far from the hot pot restaurant?
A 离 B 不远 — saying no
Put 不 or 不太 before 远/近, never before 离.
我家离地铁站不太远。
wǒ jiā lí dìtiězhàn bú tài yuǎn.
My home isn't too far from the subway station.
Part 2 — Characters #7: Deck seven! Work, time, places and food (工作, 有时间, 明年, 超市, 鸡蛋…). You know all of these by sound except 饭馆 (restaurant) and 爱 (to love).

Characters to Recognize

工作 gōngzuò to work / job
买 mǎi to buy
和…一起 hé … yìqǐ together with …
有时间 yǒu shíjiān to have time
一个 yí ge one (of something)
两个 liǎng ge two (of something) — 两 not 二
中国菜 zhōngguó cài Chinese food
明年 míngnián next year
今天 jīntiān today
明天 míngtiān tomorrow
超市 chāoshì supermarket
医院 yīyuàn hospital
饭馆 fànguǎn restaurant ✨
公园 gōngyuán park
机场 jīchǎng airport
附近 fùjìn nearby
地铁站 dìtiězhàn subway station
累 lèi tired
裙子 qúnzi skirt / dress
鸡蛋 jīdàn egg
爱 ài to love ✨
Spot the radicals: 饣 (food) is on both halves of 饭馆, a restaurant is food + food. 钅 (metal) is in 铁 of 地铁, the "iron road". 站 starts with 立 (to stand), a station is where you stand and wait. 衤 (clothing) is in 裙 (skirt). 院 = place again, like 电影院 in Lesson 28: 医 (medicine) + 院 = hospital.

Reading Practice — No Pinyin!

Read each sentence aloud, then tap to check yourself.

1. 他和哥哥都在机场工作。
tā hé gēge dōu zài jīchǎng gōngzuò — He and his older brother both work at the airport.
2. 我很想学汉语，但是我没时间。
wǒ hěn xiǎng xué hànyǔ, dànshì wǒ méi shíjiān — I really want to learn Chinese, but I don't have time.
3. 她的妈妈很会做中国菜。
tā de māma hěn huì zuò zhōngguó cài — Her mom is really good at cooking Chinese food.
4. 我每天都吃一个鸡蛋。
wǒ měitiān dōu chī yí ge jīdàn — I eat an egg every day.
5. 妹妹非常爱绿色的裙子。
mèimei fēicháng ài lǜsè de qúnzi — My younger sister really loves green skirts.
6. 你家附近有医院吗？
nǐ jiā fùjìn yǒu yīyuàn ma? — Is there a hospital near your home?
7. 你明天有时间去超市买牛奶吗？
nǐ míngtiān yǒu shíjiān qù chāoshì mǎi niúnǎi ma? — Do you have time tomorrow to go to the supermarket to buy milk?
8. 我想和弟弟一起去饭馆吃饭。
wǒ xiǎng hé dìdi yìqǐ qù fànguǎn chīfàn — I want to go eat at a restaurant with my younger brother.
9. 我家附近有两个地铁站。
wǒ jiā fùjìn yǒu liǎng ge dìtiězhàn — There are two subway stations near my home.
10. 我很累，不想去公园。
wǒ hěn lèi, bù xiǎng qù gōngyuán — I'm very tired, I don't want to go to the park.
Character passage #5 — 小张 (Xiao Zhang): Eight lines, all characters from decks 1–7. Read each aloud, then tap to check. A few new combos first:

Extra Words — From the Passage

在一起 zài yìqǐ to be together (as a couple) ✨
第五年 dì-wǔ nián the fifth year ✨
给…买 gěi … mǎi to buy for (someone)
水果蛋糕 shuǐguǒ dàngāo fruit cake ✨

Reading: 小张

小张家附近没有地铁站，他每天都开车去工作。
xiǎo zhāng jiā fùjìn méiyǒu dìtiězhàn, tā měitiān dōu kāichē qù gōngzuò. — There's no subway station near Xiao Zhang's home, so he drives to work every day.
今天是他和女朋友在一起的第五年，
jīntiān shì tā hé nǚpéngyou zài yìqǐ de dì-wǔ nián, — Today marks five years that he and his girlfriend have been together,
所以他想去给女朋友买一个水果蛋糕。
suǒyǐ tā xiǎng qù gěi nǚpéngyou mǎi yí ge shuǐguǒ dàngāo. — so he wants to go buy his girlfriend a fruit cake.
小张的女朋友叫小美。小美是律师。
xiǎo zhāng de nǚpéngyou jiào xiǎo měi. xiǎo měi shì lǜshī. — Xiao Zhang's girlfriend is called Xiao Mei. Xiao Mei is a lawyer.
她非常喜欢吃草莓和芒果。
tā fēicháng xǐhuan chī cǎoméi hé mángguǒ. — She really loves strawberries and mangoes.
她最喜欢的颜色是蓝色。
tā zuì xǐhuan de yánsè shì lánsè. — Her favorite color is blue.
她经常和小张一起去咖啡店看书，去超市买花和面包，去公园跑步，
tā jīngcháng hé xiǎo zhāng yìqǐ qù kāfēidiàn kàn shū, qù chāoshì mǎi huā hé miànbāo, qù gōngyuán pǎobù, — She often goes with Xiao Zhang to the café to read, to the supermarket to buy flowers and bread, and to the park to run;
有时候他们也在家看电影，做饭。
yǒushíhou tāmen yě zài jiā kàn diànyǐng, zuò fàn. — sometimes they also stay home, watch movies and cook.

Homework — Test Yourself

Tap the blurred text to check your answer!

1. Say "My home is very close to the park":
我家离公园很近。(wǒ jiā lí gōngyuán hěn jìn)
2. Ask "Is your home far from the airport?"
你家离机场远吗？(nǐ jiā lí jīchǎng yuǎn ma?)
3. Say "The hospital is extremely close to the flower shop":
医院离花店非常近。(yīyuàn lí huādiàn fēicháng jìn)
4. Say "My home isn't too far from the subway station":
我家离地铁站不太远。(wǒ jiā lí dìtiězhàn bú tài yuǎn)
5. Which radical do 远 and 近 share, and what does it mean?
辶 — walking / road (distance is about walking)
6. Say "I want to go eat at a restaurant with my younger brother":
我想和弟弟一起去饭馆吃饭。(wǒ xiǎng hé dìdi yìqǐ qù fànguǎn chīfàn)
7. From the 小张 passage: why does Xiao Zhang drive to work?
因为他家附近没有地铁站。(yīnwèi tā jiā fùjìn méiyǒu dìtiězhàn) — There's no subway station near his home.
